# M1 主线（后端 + CLI）Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 做出 AI Studio 的后端：FastAPI 服务 + SQLite + 单 GPU 任务队列 + ComfyUI 适配（zimage / h3_image / h3_video / wan22_i2v）+ LLM 网关（openai / claude / manual）+ 剧本 → 分镜 → 首帧 → 视频 → 合成五个阶段 + `studio` CLI + doctor。做完后，用 CLI 就能把一段梗概跑成带 H3 原生声音的成片。

**Architecture:** HTTP API 是唯一入口，CLI（以及之后的前端、MCP）都只是它的客户端。生成类操作一律提交成 Job，由 GPU worker（串行）和 CPU worker（2 个）执行。引擎只负责把请求拼成 ComfyUI 工作流（纯函数，可单测），由 ComfyRunner 负责上传、提交、等结果、下载。每个产物都是某个 Slot 下的 Candidate，记录输入哈希，用来判断是否过期。

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12、uv、FastAPI、uvicorn、SQLAlchemy 2、Alembic、Pydantic 2、httpx、websockets、Jinja2、typer、pytest；ffmpeg 8.1；ComfyUI Desktop 0.38.1；LM Studio；Claude Code CLI 2.1。

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-07-ai-studio-design.md`

**范围说明：** 设计文档的 M1 包含前端（项目中心 / 剧本 / 导演台 / 任务 / 设置）。前端单独写成 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-m1-frontend.md`，在本计划完成后执行。M1 的最终验收（"在网页里走完全流程"）在前端计划的最后一个任务里做；本计划的最后一个任务用 CLI 在真实环境里跑通同一条流程。

## Global Constraints

- 只监听 `127.0.0.1`，默认端口 `8700`；单用户，不做登录。
- Python 依赖用 uv 管理（`uv sync` / `uv run`），Python ≥ 3.12。
- 路径、模型文件名、服务地址全部写在 `config.yaml` / `workflows/*.map.yaml`，不写进 Python 代码；密钥只放 `.env` / 环境变量。
- `config.yaml`、`.env`、`projects/`、`logs/` 已在 `.gitignore` 里，绝不提交。
- 数据库：`projects/studio.db`（SQLite），用 Alembic 迁移；媒体文件放在 `projects/<slug>/media/<kind>/`，数据库里只存相对 `projects/` 的 posix 路径。
- 生成类操作一律是任务：API 只提交任务、查询状态，不在请求里同步跑生成。
- 产物都是候选：同一槽位可以有多个候选，下游只用选定的那个，旧候选不删。
- 每次 ComfyUI 调用都把最终工作流 JSON 和参数写到 `projects/<slug>/runs/<job_id>/`。
- 提示词模板全部自己写（`backend/prompts/*.md`，Jinja2），不复制 BigBanana 的任何代码和提示词原文。
- image_prompt 规则：80–150 个英文词，顺序为景别/焦段 → 主体 → 三个以上具体物件 → 光线 → 色彩/质感 → 风格锚点；角色外貌每镜原样复述，禁止"same as before"；台词每句 ≤16 字。
- 用户可见文案（错误信息、日志、CLI 输出）用中文。
- git 提交信息不带任何 Claude 署名（不加 Co-Authored-By、Generated with 之类）。
- 所有单元测试不依赖 GPU、不依赖真实 ComfyUI / LM Studio / Claude；只有最后一个任务碰真实环境。

## Review Focus

1. **中文 slug、带中文的路径**（`projects/提词员/media/...`、`D:\...\成片\第1集.mp4`）要一路贯通：入库、ComfyUI 上传、ffmpeg 合成都不能因为路径里有中文而失败。→ Task 9 的 ffmpeg 集成测试用中文目录；Task 16 用中文标题建项目。
2. **上游任务失败、被取消或服务重启后**，依赖它的排队任务不能永远挂在 queued，应该直接失败并写明原因。→ Task 5 `test_dependency_failure_fails_dependent`。
3. **有声镜头（H3）和无声镜头（Wan2.2）混剪**时，成片要保留声音，并且音画时长对齐，不能整条变成无声。→ Task 9 `test_assemble_mixed_audio_keeps_sound`。
4. **LLM 一直给错镜头数或用了不存在的角色**时，任务要失败并保留原始输出，数据库里不能留下半截分镜。→ Task 13 `test_wrong_count_fails_and_keeps_db_clean`。
5. **改了提示词或换了首帧之后**，旧候选要显示过期；重跑后新候选要自动选上，而不是继续用旧的。→ Task 14 `test_rerun_after_prompt_change_selects_new`，Task 17 `test_video_stale_after_new_first_frame`。

**Interfaces:**

  - `ProviderCfg(id, kind: "openai"|"claude"|"manual", base_url, model, api_key_env, local_gpu: bool, unload_cmd: list[str]|None, use_sdk: bool, timeout: float)`，方法 `api_key() -> str|None`

`backend/tests/test_config.py`：

```python
import textwrap

import pytest

from backend.config import ROOT, load_config


def write_cfg(tmp_path, text):
    p = tmp_path / "config.yaml"
    p.write_text(textwrap.dedent(text), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def test_relative_paths_resolve_against_config_dir(tmp_path):
    cfg = load_config(write_cfg(tmp_path, """
        server: {data_dir: data}
        comfyui: {models_dir: models}
    """))
    assert cfg.data_dir == (tmp_path / "data").resolve()
    assert cfg.comfyui.models_dir == (tmp_path / "models").resolve()
    # 没写的路径用仓库内默认值
    assert cfg.workflows_dir == ROOT / "workflows"
    assert cfg.log_dir == ROOT / "logs"


def test_provider_routing(tmp_path):
    cfg = load_config(write_cfg(tmp_path, """
        llm:
          default: lmstudio
          providers:
            - {id: lmstudio, kind: openai, base_url: "http://127.0.0.1:1234/v1", local_gpu: true}
            - {id: claude, kind: claude}
          routes: {script: claude}
    """))
    assert cfg.llm.provider_for("script").id == "claude"
    assert cfg.llm.provider_for("storyboard").id == "lmstudio"
    assert cfg.llm.provider("lmstudio").local_gpu is True


def test_unknown_provider_raises(tmp_path):
    cfg = load_config(write_cfg(tmp_path, """
        llm:
          providers: [{id: claude, kind: claude}]
          routes: {script: nope}
    """))
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="nope"):
        cfg.llm.provider_for("script")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="storyboard"):
        cfg.llm.provider_for("storyboard")


def test_api_key_from_env(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("TEST_STUDIO_KEY", "sk-test")
    cfg = load_config(write_cfg(tmp_path, """
        llm:
          providers: [{id: ds, kind: openai, base_url: "http://x/v1", api_key_env: TEST_STUDIO_KEY}]
    """))
    assert cfg.llm.provider("ds").api_key() == "sk-test"


def test_engine_defaults_when_missing(tmp_path):
    cfg = load_config(write_cfg(tmp_path, "engines: {zimage: {params: {steps: 8}}}\n"))
    assert cfg.engine("zimage").params == {"steps": 8}
    assert cfg.engine("not_configured").enabled is True


def test_example_config_loads():
    cfg = load_config(ROOT / "config.example.yaml")
    assert cfg.server.port == 8700
    assert cfg.server.host == "127.0.0.1"
    assert {"zimage", "h3_image", "h3_video", "wan22_i2v"} <= set(cfg.engines)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 config.py 和 config.example.yaml**

`backend/config.py`：

```python
"""配置加载：config.yaml + .env。

相对路径一律相对配置文件所在目录解析；没写的路径用仓库内默认值。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import yaml
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


class ServerCfg(BaseModel):
    host: str = "127.0.0.1"
    port: int = 8700
    data_dir: Path = ROOT / "projects"


class ComfyCfg(BaseModel):
    url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8188"
    models_dir: Path | None = None  # 只给 doctor 检查模型文件用
    timeout: float = 30.0
    max_retries: int = 6


class ProviderCfg(BaseModel):
    id: str
    kind: Literal["openai", "claude", "manual"]
    base_url: str | None = None
    model: str | None = None
    api_key_env: str | None = None
    # 跑在本机显卡上（LM Studio）：调用前要让 GPU 任务让路，调用后卸载模型
    local_gpu: bool = False
    unload_cmd: list[str] | None = None
    use_sdk: bool = True  # 仅 claude：先试 Agent SDK，失败退回 claude -p
    timeout: float = 1800.0

    def api_key(self) -> str | None:
        return os.environ.get(self.api_key_env) if self.api_key_env else None


class LLMCfg(BaseModel):
    default: str | None = None
    providers: list[ProviderCfg] = Field(default_factory=list)
    routes: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    def provider(self, provider_id: str) -> ProviderCfg:
        for p in self.providers:
            if p.id == provider_id:
                return p
        raise KeyError(f"未配置 LLM provider {provider_id!r}，已有：{[p.id for p in self.providers]}")

    def provider_for(self, task: str) -> ProviderCfg:
        pid = self.routes.get(task) or self.default
        if not pid:
            raise KeyError(f"任务 {task!r} 没有配置 llm.routes，也没有 llm.default")
        return self.provider(pid)


class EngineCfg(BaseModel):
    enabled: bool = True
    params: dict = Field(default_factory=dict)


class FfmpegCfg(BaseModel):
    ffmpeg: str = "ffmpeg"
    ffprobe: str = "ffprobe"
    font: str | None = None


class Config(BaseModel):
    server: ServerCfg = Field(default_factory=ServerCfg)
    comfyui: ComfyCfg = Field(default_factory=ComfyCfg)
    llm: LLMCfg = Field(default_factory=LLMCfg)
    engines: dict[str, EngineCfg] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    ffmpeg: FfmpegCfg = Field(default_factory=FfmpegCfg)
    workflows_dir: Path = ROOT / "workflows"
    log_dir: Path = ROOT / "logs"

    def engine(self, name: str) -> EngineCfg:
        return self.engines.get(name) or EngineCfg()

    @property
    def data_dir(self) -> Path:
        return self.server.data_dir


def _absolutize(cfg: Config, base: Path) -> Config:
    def fix(p: Path | None) -> Path | None:
        if p is None:
            return None
        return p if p.is_absolute() else (base / p).resolve()

    cfg.server.data_dir = fix(cfg.server.data_dir)
    cfg.comfyui.models_dir = fix(cfg.comfyui.models_dir)
    cfg.workflows_dir = fix(cfg.workflows_dir)
    cfg.log_dir = fix(cfg.log_dir)
    return cfg


def load_config(path: Path | None = None) -> Config:
    """读配置。不给路径时依次找 $STUDIO_CONFIG、config.yaml、config.example.yaml。"""
    load_dotenv(ROOT / ".env")
    if path is None:
        env = os.environ.get("STUDIO_CONFIG")
        path = Path(env) if env else ROOT / "config.yaml"
        if not path.exists():
            path = ROOT / "config.example.yaml"
    data = yaml.safe_load(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    return _absolutize(Config.model_validate(data), Path(path).resolve().parent)
```

```python
import logging

from backend.logging_setup import setup_logging


def test_setup_logging_is_idempotent_and_writes(tmp_path):
    setup_logging(tmp_path)
    setup_logging(tmp_path)
    root = logging.getLogger()
    ours = [h for h in root.handlers if getattr(h, "_studio", False)]
    assert len(ours) == 1
    logging.getLogger("x").info("你好")
    for h in ours:
        h.flush()
    assert "你好" in (tmp_path / "studio.log").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for h in ours:
        root.removeHandler(h)
        h.close()
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 实现 logging_setup.py**

```python
"""后端日志：logs/studio.log，按天轮转，保留 14 天。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from logging.handlers import TimedRotatingFileHandler
from pathlib import Path


def setup_logging(log_dir: Path, level: int = logging.INFO) -> None:
    root = logging.getLogger()
    if any(getattr(h, "_studio", False) for h in root.handlers):
        return
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fh = TimedRotatingFileHandler(log_dir / "studio.log", when="midnight",
                                  backupCount=14, encoding="utf-8")
    fh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s"))
    fh._studio = True  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    root.addHandler(fh)
    root.setLevel(level)
```

- Consumes: 无
- Produces:
  - `backend.domain.models`：`Base`、`utcnow() -> datetime`（UTC、无时区）、实体 `StyleBible`、`Project`、`Character`、`Variation`、`Location`、`Prop`、`Episode`、`SceneBlock`、`Shot`、`Slot`、`Candidate`、`PromptVersion`、`Job`、`Export`。字段见下方代码。
  - 关系：`Project.style_bible / episodes / characters / locations / props`，`Episode.project / scene_blocks（按 order）/ shots（按 order）`，`Slot.candidates（按 id）`，`Character.variations`。
  - `backend.domain.db.Database(path: Path)`：属性 `engine`、`url`；方法 `create_all()`、`upgrade()`（Alembic 升到 head）、`session()`（上下文管理器，正常退出时提交，异常时回滚）。
  - conftest fixture `db`：临时目录下已 `create_all()` 的 `Database`。

说明：设计文档里 Shot 的 `first_frame_slot` / `end_frame_slot` / `video_slot` 统一用 `Slot(owner_type="shot", owner_id=shot.id, kind=...)` 表示，并给 `(owner_type, owner_id, kind)` 加唯一约束，Shot 表本身不存槽位 id。Job 的 `outputs[]` 放在 `Job.result` 里。另加三个设计文档没列、但流程需要的字段：`Shot.code`（镜头编号 S01…，CLI 用它选镜头）、`Episode.stage_hashes`（各步输入指纹，用来跳过没变的步骤）、`Job.depends_on`（依赖的任务 id）。Job 状态在设计文档的五种之外再加一种 `waiting_input`（manual provider 等人粘贴结果时用）。

`backend/tests/test_models.py`：

```python
from alembic.autogenerate import compare_metadata
from alembic.migration import MigrationContext
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.db import Database
from backend.domain.models import Base, Candidate, Episode, Job, Project, SceneBlock, Shot, Slot


def test_shot_json_fields_roundtrip(db):
    with db.session() as s:
        p = Project(slug="提词员", title="提词员")
        s.add(p)
        s.flush()
        ep = Episode(project_id=p.id, number=1, source_text="梗概")
        s.add(ep)
        s.flush()
        s.add(Shot(episode_id=ep.id, order=0, code="S01",
                   dialogue=[{"speaker": "林", "zh": "你好", "start": 0.5, "end": 1.5}],
                   cast=[{"character_id": 1, "variation_id": None}]))
    with db.session() as s:
        shot = s.scalar(select(Shot))
        assert shot.dialogue[0]["zh"] == "你好"
        assert shot.transition_out == "hard_cut"
        assert shot.duration == 5.0
        ep = s.scalar(select(Episode))
        assert ep.stage_hashes == {}
        assert [x.code for x in ep.shots] == ["S01"]


def test_delete_project_cascades(db):
    with db.session() as s:
        p = Project(slug="demo", title="Demo")
        s.add(p)
        s.flush()
        ep = Episode(project_id=p.id, number=1)
        s.add(ep)
        s.flush()
        s.add(SceneBlock(episode_id=ep.id, order=0, text="段落"))
        s.add(Shot(episode_id=ep.id, order=0, code="S01"))
    with db.session() as s:
        s.delete(s.scalar(select(Project)))
    with db.session() as s:
        assert s.scalar(select(Shot)) is None
        assert s.scalar(select(SceneBlock)) is None


def test_job_defaults(db):
    with db.session() as s:
        j = Job(type="x")
        s.add(j)
        s.flush()
        assert j.status == "queued"
        assert j.payload == {} and j.depends_on == [] and j.log == ""


def test_slot_candidates_ordered(db):
    with db.session() as s:
        slot = Slot(owner_type="shot", owner_id=1, kind="first_frame")
        s.add(slot)
        s.flush()
        for i in range(3):
            s.add(Candidate(slot_id=slot.id, file_path=f"a/{i}.png", engine="zimage"))
    with db.session() as s:
        slot = s.scalar(select(Slot))
        assert [c.file_path for c in slot.candidates] == ["a/0.png", "a/1.png", "a/2.png"]


def test_migrations_match_models(tmp_path):
    d = Database(tmp_path / "m.db")
    d.upgrade()
    with d.engine.connect() as conn:
        mc = MigrationContext.configure(conn, opts={"render_as_batch": True})
        diff = compare_metadata(mc, Base.metadata)
    d.engine.dispose()
    assert diff == []
```

"""SQLAlchemy 实体。字段含义见设计文档第 5 节。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import (JSON, Boolean, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text,
                        UniqueConstraint)
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column, relationship


def utcnow() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


class StyleBible(Base):
    __tablename__ = "style_bibles"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    palette: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    lighting: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    texture: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    character_rules: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    mood_keywords: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSON, default=list)
    anchor_text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    negative: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")


class Project(Base):
    __tablename__ = "projects"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    slug: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), unique=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    aspect: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="9:16")
    image_engine: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="zimage")
    video_engine: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="h3_video")
    seed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=12345)
    negative: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    style_bible_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("style_bibles.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=utcnow)

    style_bible: Mapped[StyleBible | None] = relationship()
    episodes: Mapped[list[Episode]] = relationship(
        back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Episode.number")
    characters: Mapped[list[Character]] = relationship(
        back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Character.id")
    locations: Mapped[list[Location]] = relationship(
        back_populates="project", cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Location.id")
    props: Mapped[list[Prop]] = relationship(cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="Prop.id")


class Character(Base):
    __tablename__ = "characters"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("project_id", "name"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    project_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    visual_prompt: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    core_features: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    voice: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)

    project: Mapped[Project] = relationship(back_populates="characters")
    variations: Mapped[list[Variation]] = relationship(cascade="all, delete-orphan",
                                                       order_by="Variation.id")


class Variation(Base):
    __tablename__ = "variations"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    character_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("characters.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    visual_prompt: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")


class Location(Base):
    __tablename__ = "locations"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("project_id", "name"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    project_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    time_of_day: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), default="")
    atmosphere: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    visual_prompt: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")

    project: Mapped[Project] = relationship(back_populates="locations")


class Prop(Base):
    __tablename__ = "props"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    project_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")
    visual_prompt: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, default="")


class Episode(Base):
    __tablename__ = "episodes"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("project_id", "number"),)
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    project_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))

    scene_blocks: Mapped[list[SceneBlock]] = relationship(
        cascade="all, delete-orphan", order_by="SceneBlock.order")

class Candidate(Base):

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 db.py**

```python
"""数据库：SQLite + WAL。服务启动时用 Alembic 升到最新，测试里直接 create_all。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

from alembic import command
from alembic.config import Config as AlembicConfig
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from backend.domain.models import Base

MIGRATIONS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "migrations"


def _pragmas(dbapi_conn, _record) -> None:
    cur = dbapi_conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    cur.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
    cur.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=30000")
    cur.close()


class Database:
    def __init__(self, path: Path):
        path = Path(path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.path = path
        self.url = f"sqlite:///{path.resolve().as_posix()}"
        self.engine = create_engine(self.url, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False,
                                                            "timeout": 30})
        event.listen(self.engine, "connect", _pragmas)
        self._factory = sessionmaker(self.engine, expire_on_commit=False)

    def create_all(self) -> None:
        Base.metadata.create_all(self.engine)

    def upgrade(self) -> None:
        cfg = AlembicConfig()
        cfg.set_main_option("script_location", str(MIGRATIONS))
        cfg.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", self.url)
        command.upgrade(cfg, "head")

    @contextmanager
    def session(self) -> Iterator[Session]:
        s = self._factory()
        try:
            yield s
            s.commit()
        except BaseException:
            s.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            s.close()
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 初始化 Alembic 并生成首个迁移**

Run: `uv run alembic init backend/migrations`
Expected: 生成 `alembic.ini`、`backend/migrations/env.py`、`script.py.mako`、`versions/`。

把 `alembic.ini` 里 `sqlalchemy.url` 那一行改成：

```ini
sqlalchemy.url = sqlite:///projects/_alembic_dev.db
```

用下面内容整体替换 `backend/migrations/env.py`：

```python
"""Alembic 环境。url 由 Database.upgrade() 或 alembic.ini 提供。"""
from alembic import context
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

from backend.domain.models import Base

config = context.config
target_metadata = Base.metadata


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    context.configure(url=config.get_main_option("sqlalchemy.url"),
                      target_metadata=target_metadata, literal_binds=True,
                      render_as_batch=True)
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    connectable = engine_from_config(config.get_section(config.config_ini_section, {}),
                                     prefix="sqlalchemy.", poolclass=pool.NullPool)
    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata,
                          render_as_batch=True)
        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
```

### Task 3: 媒体路径、槽位候选、输入哈希、镜头编号

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/domain/media.py`, `backend/domain/slots.py`, `backend/domain/hashing.py`, `backend/domain/shots.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_domain_helpers.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 的 `Slot`、`Candidate`、`Shot`、`Database`
- Produces:
  - `MediaStore(data_dir: Path)`：`root`、`project_dir(slug) -> Path`、`new_media_path(slug, kind, ext) -> Path`（会建目录）、`run_dir(slug, job_id) -> Path`（会建目录）、`export_path(slug, name) -> Path`、`rel(path) -> str`（相对 root 的 posix 路径）、`abs(rel) -> Path`（越界时抛 `ValueError`）、`public_file(rel) -> Path`（只允许 `<slug>/media/...` 和 `<slug>/exports/...`，否则抛 `PermissionError`）
  - `input_hash(*parts, **named) -> str`（16 位十六进制）
  - `get_or_create_slot(s, owner_type, owner_id, kind) -> Slot`
  - `add_candidate(s, slot, *, file_path, engine, params, input_hash, job_id, auto_select=True) -> Candidate`：`auto_select` 时，槽位为空或当前选定候选的 `input_hash` 与新候选不同（旧的已过期），就选定新候选
  - `select_candidate(s, candidate_id) -> Candidate`（找不到抛 `LookupError`）
  - `selected_candidate(s, owner_type, owner_id, kind) -> Candidate | None`
  - `slots_for(s, owner_type, owner_id) -> list[Slot]`
  - `renumber_shots(s, episode_id, ordered_ids: list[int] | None = None) -> list[Shot]`：按给定顺序（不给则按现有 order、id）重排，order 从 0 连续，code 为 `S01`、`S02`…；`ordered_ids` 不是这一集全部镜头的排列时抛 `ValueError`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_domain_helpers.py`：

```python
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.hashing import input_hash
from backend.domain.media import MediaStore
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Project, Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots
from backend.domain.slots import (add_candidate, get_or_create_slot, select_candidate,
                                  selected_candidate, slots_for)


def test_media_paths_with_chinese_slug(tmp_path):
    m = MediaStore(tmp_path)
    p = m.new_media_path("提词员", "first_frame", ".PNG")
    assert p.parent == (tmp_path / "提词员" / "media" / "first_frame").resolve()
    assert p.suffix == ".png" and p.parent.is_dir()
    rel = m.rel(p)
    assert rel.startswith("提词员/media/first_frame/")
    assert m.abs(rel) == p
    assert m.run_dir("提词员", 7).is_dir()


def test_media_rejects_traversal_and_private_files(tmp_path):
    m = MediaStore(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        m.abs("../outside.txt")
    (tmp_path / "studio.db").write_text("x")
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        m.public_file("studio.db")
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        m.public_file("demo/runs/1/workflow.api.json")
    f = m.new_media_path("demo", "video", "mp4")
    f.write_bytes(b"v")
    assert m.public_file(m.rel(f)) == f


def test_input_hash_stable_and_sensitive():
    a = input_hash("kf", "prompt", engine="zimage", size=[768, 1344])
    assert a == input_hash("kf", "prompt", size=[768, 1344], engine="zimage")
    assert a != input_hash("kf", "prompt2", engine="zimage", size=[768, 1344])
    assert len(a) == 16


def test_candidates_auto_select_rules(db):
    with db.session() as s:
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", 1, "first_frame")
        c1 = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path="a.png", engine="zimage", params={},
                           input_hash="h1", job_id=None)
        assert slot.selected_candidate_id == c1.id          # 空槽位：选新的
        c2 = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path="b.png", engine="zimage", params={},
                           input_hash="h1", job_id=None)
        assert slot.selected_candidate_id == c1.id          # 同输入：保留人工选择
        c3 = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path="c.png", engine="zimage", params={},
                           input_hash="h2", job_id=None)
        assert slot.selected_candidate_id == c3.id          # 旧的已过期：选新的
        assert get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", 1, "first_frame").id == slot.id
    with db.session() as s:
        select_candidate(s, c2.id)
    with db.session() as s:
        assert selected_candidate(s, "shot", 1, "first_frame").id == c2.id
        assert selected_candidate(s, "shot", 1, "video") is None
        assert [x.kind for x in slots_for(s, "shot", 1)] == ["first_frame"]
        with pytest.raises(LookupError):
            select_candidate(s, 999)


def _episode_with_shots(db, n):
    with db.session() as s:
        p = Project(slug="demo", title="Demo")
        s.add(p)
        s.flush()
        ep = Episode(project_id=p.id, number=1)
        s.add(ep)
        s.flush()
        ids = []
        for i in range(n):
            sh = Shot(episode_id=ep.id, order=i * 10, code="")
            s.add(sh)
            s.flush()
            ids.append(sh.id)
        return ep.id, ids


def test_renumber_compacts_and_reorders(db):
    ep_id, ids = _episode_with_shots(db, 3)
    with db.session() as s:
        renumber_shots(s, ep_id)
    with db.session() as s:
        shots = s.scalars(select(Shot).order_by(Shot.order)).all()
        assert [(x.order, x.code) for x in shots] == [(0, "S01"), (1, "S02"), (2, "S03")]
    with db.session() as s:
        renumber_shots(s, ep_id, [ids[2], ids[0], ids[1]])
    with db.session() as s:
        assert [x.id for x in s.scalars(select(Shot).order_by(Shot.order))] == [ids[2], ids[0], ids[1]]
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            renumber_shots(s, ep_id, [ids[0], ids[1]])
```

`backend/domain/media.py`：

```python
"""媒体文件路径。数据库里只存相对 data_dir 的 posix 路径。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from pathlib import Path

PUBLIC_DIRS = {"media", "exports"}


class MediaStore:
    def __init__(self, data_dir: Path):
        self.root = Path(data_dir).resolve()
        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def project_dir(self, slug: str) -> Path:
        return self.root / slug

    def new_media_path(self, slug: str, kind: str, ext: str) -> Path:
        d = self.root / slug / "media" / kind
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return d / f"{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.{ext.lstrip('.').lower()}"

    def run_dir(self, slug: str, job_id: int) -> Path:
        d = self.root / slug / "runs" / str(job_id)
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return d

    def export_path(self, slug: str, name: str) -> Path:
        d = self.root / slug / "exports"
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return d / name

    def rel(self, path: Path) -> str:
        return Path(path).resolve().relative_to(self.root).as_posix()

    def abs(self, rel: str) -> Path:
        p = (self.root / rel).resolve()
        if not p.is_relative_to(self.root):
            raise ValueError(f"路径越界：{rel}")
        return p

    def public_file(self, rel: str) -> Path:
        p = self.abs(rel)
        parts = p.relative_to(self.root).parts
        if len(parts) < 3 or parts[1] not in PUBLIC_DIRS:
            raise PermissionError(f"不允许访问：{rel}")
        return p
```

`backend/domain/hashing.py`：

```python
"""产物的输入指纹。输入变了，指纹就变，旧候选即为过期。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any


def input_hash(*parts: Any, **named: Any) -> str:
    blob = json.dumps({"p": parts, "n": named}, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
```

`backend/domain/slots.py`：

```python
"""槽位与候选。下游只使用槽位里选定的那个候选。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.domain.models import Candidate, Slot


def get_or_create_slot(s: Session, owner_type: str, owner_id: int, kind: str) -> Slot:
    slot = s.scalar(select(Slot).where(Slot.owner_type == owner_type,
                                       Slot.owner_id == owner_id, Slot.kind == kind))
    if slot is None:
        slot = Slot(owner_type=owner_type, owner_id=owner_id, kind=kind)
        s.add(slot)
        s.flush()
    return slot


def add_candidate(s: Session, slot: Slot, *, file_path: str, engine: str, params: dict,
                  input_hash: str, job_id: int | None, auto_select: bool = True) -> Candidate:
    c = Candidate(slot_id=slot.id, file_path=file_path, engine=engine, params=params,
                  input_hash=input_hash, job_id=job_id)
    s.add(c)
    s.flush()
    if auto_select:
        current = s.get(Candidate, slot.selected_candidate_id) if slot.selected_candidate_id else None
        if current is None or current.input_hash != input_hash:
            slot.selected_candidate_id = c.id
    s.flush()
    return c


def select_candidate(s: Session, candidate_id: int) -> Candidate:
    c = s.get(Candidate, candidate_id)
    if c is None:
        raise LookupError(f"找不到候选 {candidate_id}")
    s.get(Slot, c.slot_id).selected_candidate_id = c.id
    s.flush()
    return c


def selected_candidate(s: Session, owner_type: str, owner_id: int, kind: str) -> Candidate | None:
    slot = s.scalar(select(Slot).where(Slot.owner_type == owner_type,
                                       Slot.owner_id == owner_id, Slot.kind == kind))
    if slot is None or slot.selected_candidate_id is None:
        return None
    return s.get(Candidate, slot.selected_candidate_id)


def slots_for(s: Session, owner_type: str, owner_id: int) -> list[Slot]:
    return list(s.scalars(select(Slot).where(Slot.owner_type == owner_type,
                                             Slot.owner_id == owner_id).order_by(Slot.id)))
```

`backend/domain/shots.py`：

```python
"""镜头重新编号：order 从 0 连续，code 为 S01、S02…"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.domain.models import Shot


def renumber_shots(s: Session, episode_id: int, ordered_ids: list[int] | None = None) -> list[Shot]:
    shots = list(s.scalars(select(Shot).where(Shot.episode_id == episode_id)
                           .order_by(Shot.order, Shot.id)))
    if ordered_ids is not None:
        by_id = {sh.id: sh for sh in shots}
        if sorted(ordered_ids) != sorted(by_id):
            raise ValueError("shot_ids 必须正好是这一集的全部镜头")
        shots = [by_id[i] for i in ordered_ids]
    for i, sh in enumerate(shots):
        sh.order = i
        sh.code = f"S{i + 1:02d}"
    s.flush()
    return shots
```

### Task 4: 队列基础件：错误类型、事件总线、调度规则、处理器注册表

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/queue/__init__.py`, `backend/queue/errors.py`, `backend/queue/events.py`, `backend/queue/scheduler.py`, `backend/queue/handlers.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_queue_basics.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 无
- Produces:
  - `JobCanceled(Exception)`；`NeedsInput(Exception)`，属性 `request: dict`（至少含 `key`）；`JobError(RuntimeError)`（可预期的业务错误，原样展示给用户）
  - `EventBus(maxsize=1000)`：`bind_loop(loop)`、`subscribe() -> asyncio.Queue`、`unsubscribe(q)`、`publish(event: dict)`（线程安全；没绑定 loop 时什么都不做）
  - `QueuedJob(id: int, priority: int, model_family: str | None)`（frozen dataclass）
  - `pick_next(jobs: Sequence[QueuedJob], current_family: str | None) -> QueuedJob | None`：取最高优先级；同优先级里优先和 `current_family` 同族的；再按 id 最小
  - `HandlerSpec(type, fn, gpu)`；`HandlerRegistry`：`register(job_type, *, gpu=False)`（装饰器）、`get(job_type) -> HandlerSpec`（未知类型抛 `JobError`）、`types() -> list[str]`；全局实例 `HANDLERS`
  - 处理器签名：`fn(ctx: JobContext, payload: dict) -> dict | None`（JobContext 在 Task 5 定义）

import asyncio
import threading

import pytest

from backend.queue.errors import JobError, NeedsInput
from backend.queue.events import EventBus
from backend.queue.handlers import HandlerRegistry
from backend.queue.scheduler import QueuedJob, pick_next


def test_publish_from_thread_reaches_subscriber():
    async def main():
        bus = EventBus()
        bus.bind_loop(asyncio.get_running_loop())
        q = bus.subscribe()
        threading.Thread(target=bus.publish, args=({"type": "x"},)).start()
        return await asyncio.wait_for(q.get(), 2)

    assert asyncio.run(main()) == {"type": "x"}


def test_unsubscribed_queue_receives_nothing():
    async def main():
        bus = EventBus()
        bus.bind_loop(asyncio.get_running_loop())
        q = bus.subscribe()
        bus.unsubscribe(q)
        bus.publish({"type": "x"})
        await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
        return q.empty()

    assert asyncio.run(main())


def test_publish_without_loop_is_noop():
    EventBus().publish({"type": "x"})


def J(i, prio=0, fam=None):
    return QueuedJob(id=i, priority=prio, model_family=fam)


def test_pick_next_priority_then_family_then_id():
    assert pick_next([], None) is None
    assert pick_next([J(3), J(1), J(2)], None).id == 1
    assert pick_next([J(1), J(2, prio=5)], None).id == 2
    jobs = [J(1, fam="zimage"), J(2, fam="h3"), J(3, fam="h3")]
    assert pick_next(jobs, "h3").id == 2
    assert pick_next(jobs, "wan22").id == 1
    # 优先级高于同族偏好
    assert pick_next([J(1, fam="h3"), J(2, prio=1, fam="zimage")], "h3").id == 2


def test_registry():
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("demo", gpu=True)
    def demo(ctx, payload):
        return {"ok": True}

    spec = reg.get("demo")
    assert spec.gpu is True and spec.fn is demo
    assert reg.types() == ["demo"]
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="未知任务类型"):
        reg.get("nope")


def test_needs_input_carries_request():

    assert e.request["key"] == "k"

`backend/queue/errors.py`：

```python
"""任务执行中的控制流异常。"""


class JobCanceled(Exception):
    """任务被取消。处理器检测到取消时抛出。"""


class NeedsInput(Exception):
    """需要人工输入（manual provider）。request 里放给人看的提示词等信息。"""

    def __init__(self, request: dict):
        super().__init__("等待人工输入")
        self.request = request


class JobError(RuntimeError):
    """可预期的业务错误，错误信息原样展示给用户。"""
```

`backend/queue/events.py`：

```python
"""事件总线：worker 线程发布，WebSocket（asyncio）订阅。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import threading


class EventBus:
    def __init__(self, maxsize: int = 1000):
        self._subs: set[asyncio.Queue] = set()
        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._maxsize = maxsize

    def bind_loop(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
        self._loop = loop

    def subscribe(self) -> asyncio.Queue:
        q: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=self._maxsize)
        with self._lock:
            self._subs.add(q)
        return q

    def unsubscribe(self, q: asyncio.Queue) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._subs.discard(q)

    def publish(self, event: dict) -> None:
        loop = self._loop
        if loop is None or loop.is_closed():
            return
        try:
            loop.call_soon_threadsafe(self._fanout, event)
        except RuntimeError:  # loop 已关闭
            pass

    def _fanout(self, event: dict) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            subs = list(self._subs)
        for q in subs:
            if not q.full():  # 慢消费者直接丢事件，不阻塞 worker
                q.put_nowait(event)
```

`backend/queue/scheduler.py`：

```python
"""选下一个任务：优先级最高的先跑；同优先级优先选与当前已加载模型同族的，减少换模型。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueuedJob:
    id: int
    priority: int
    model_family: str | None


def pick_next(jobs: Sequence[QueuedJob], current_family: str | None) -> QueuedJob | None:
    if not jobs:
        return None
    top = max(j.priority for j in jobs)
    pool = [j for j in jobs if j.priority == top]
    same = [j for j in pool if current_family and j.model_family == current_family]
    return min(same or pool, key=lambda j: j.id)
```

`backend/queue/handlers.py`：

```python
"""任务类型 → 处理函数。pipeline 模块在导入时用 HANDLERS.register 登记。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from backend.queue.errors import JobError


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HandlerSpec:
    type: str
    fn: Callable[[Any, dict], dict | None]
    gpu: bool = False


class HandlerRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self._specs: dict[str, HandlerSpec] = {}

    def register(self, job_type: str, *, gpu: bool = False):
        def deco(fn):
            self._specs[job_type] = HandlerSpec(job_type, fn, gpu)
            return fn
        return deco

    def get(self, job_type: str) -> HandlerSpec:
        try:
            return self._specs[job_type]
        except KeyError:
            raise JobError(f"未知任务类型 {job_type}") from None

    def types(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted(self._specs)


HANDLERS = HandlerRegistry()
```

### Task 5: JobManager（GPU/CPU worker、取消、依赖、重试、人工输入、GPU 互斥）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/queue/context.py`, `backend/queue/manager.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_manager.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 `Database`、`Job`、`utcnow`；Task 4 全部
- Produces:
  - `JobContext(manager, job_id, cancel_event)`：属性 `manager`、`job_id`、`cancel_event: threading.Event`、`services`（= `manager.services`）；方法 `log(msg)`、`progress(value, maximum)`、`check_canceled()`（已取消时抛 `JobCanceled`）
  - `job_to_dict(job: Job) -> dict`：键 `id type status project_id owner_type owner_id payload gpu model_family priority depends_on log error result created_at started_at finished_at`（时间为 ISO 字符串）
  - `JobManager(db, registry, bus=None, *, cpu_workers=2, poll=0.5)`：
    - 可设置的属性：`services`、`on_family_switch: Callable[[str|None, str], None] | None`（GPU 任务换模型族前调用）、`on_gpu_handover: Callable[[], None] | None`（`gpu_exclusive()` 拿到显卡后调用，用来让 ComfyUI 释放显存）
    - `current_family: str | None`、`paused: bool`（属性）
    - `recover() -> int`：把 running 标成 failed（error 以 `interrupted` 开头）
    - `start()`、`stop(timeout=10.0)`
    - `submit(job_type, payload=None, *, project_id=None, owner_type=None, owner_id=None, priority=0, model_family=None, depends_on=None) -> int`（`gpu` 取自处理器登记）
    - `cancel(job_id) -> bool`、`retry(job_id, payload_override=None) -> int`、`provide_input(job_id, text) -> dict`
    - `pause()`、`resume()`、`queue_state() -> {"paused", "counts": {queued, running, waiting_input}, "current_family"}`
    - `get(job_id) -> dict`（找不到抛 `LookupError`）、`list_jobs(statuses=None, project_id=None, limit=100) -> list[dict]`（新的在前）
    - `append_log(job_id, msg)`
    - `gpu_exclusive()`：上下文管理器，等当前 GPU 任务结束后独占显卡，期间 GPU worker 不领新任务
  - 事件：`{"type": "job", "job": job_to_dict}`、`{"type": "log", "job_id", "line"}`、`{"type": "progress", "job_id", "value", "max"}`、`{"type": "queue", ...queue_state}`
  - 人工输入约定：处理器抛 `NeedsInput({"key": k, ...})` → 状态 `waiting_input`，`result = {"input_request": request}`；`provide_input(job_id, text)` 把文本写进 `payload["manual"][k]` 并重新排队

`backend/tests/test_manager.py`：

```python
import threading
import time

import pytest

from backend.domain.models import Job
from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled, JobError, NeedsInput
from backend.queue.handlers import HandlerRegistry
from backend.queue.manager import JobManager


def wait_for(pred, timeout=5.0):
    end = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        v = pred()
        if v:
            return v
        time.sleep(0.02)
    raise AssertionError("等待超时")


class RecordingBus:
    def __init__(self):
        self.events = []

    def publish(self, e):
        self.events.append(e)


@pytest.fixture
def managers():
    made = []
    yield made
    for m in made:
        m.stop()


def make(db, reg, managers, start=True):
    m = JobManager(db, reg, RecordingBus(), poll=0.02)
    managers.append(m)
    if start:
        m.start()
    return m


def wait_status(m, jid, *statuses):
    return wait_for(lambda: (d := m.get(jid))["status"] in statuses and d)


def test_job_runs_to_done(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("echo")
    def echo(ctx, payload):
        ctx.log("开始")
        return {"x": payload["x"]}

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    jid = m.submit("echo", {"x": 1})
    j = wait_status(m, jid, "done")
    assert j["result"] == {"x": 1}
    assert "开始" in j["log"]
    assert any(e["type"] == "job" and e["job"]["status"] == "done" for e in m.bus.events)


def test_failures_record_error(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("biz")
    def biz(ctx, payload):
        raise JobError("镜头 S03 还没有选定首帧")

    @reg.register("bug")
    def bug(ctx, payload):
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    a, b = m.submit("biz"), m.submit("bug")
    assert wait_status(m, a, "failed")["error"] == "镜头 S03 还没有选定首帧"
    jb = wait_status(m, b, "failed")
    assert jb["error"].startswith("RuntimeError: boom")
    assert "Traceback" in jb["log"]


def test_cancel_queued(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    reg.register("noop")(lambda ctx, p: None)
    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    m.pause()
    jid = m.submit("noop")
    assert m.cancel(jid) is True
    assert m.get(jid)["status"] == "canceled"
    m.resume()
    time.sleep(0.1)
    assert m.get(jid)["status"] == "canceled"


def test_cancel_running(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    started = threading.Event()

    @reg.register("loop", gpu=True)
    def loop(ctx, payload):
        started.set()
        while True:
            ctx.check_canceled()
            time.sleep(0.01)

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    jid = m.submit("loop")
    assert started.wait(3)
    m.cancel(jid)
    wait_status(m, jid, "canceled")


def test_recover_marks_running_failed(db, managers):
    with db.session() as s:
        j = Job(type="x", status="running")
        s.add(j)
        s.flush()
        jid = j.id
    m = make(db, HandlerRegistry(), managers, start=False)
    assert m.recover() == 1
    d = m.get(jid)
    assert d["status"] == "failed" and d["error"].startswith("interrupted")


def test_gpu_jobs_run_one_at_a_time(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    lock = threading.Lock()
    state = {"active": 0, "max": 0}

    @reg.register("g", gpu=True)
    def g(ctx, payload):
        with lock:
            state["active"] += 1
            state["max"] = max(state["max"], state["active"])
        time.sleep(0.05)
        with lock:
            state["active"] -= 1

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    ids = [m.submit("g") for _ in range(3)]
    for jid in ids:
        wait_status(m, jid, "done")
    assert state["max"] == 1


def test_family_preference_and_switch_hook(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    order = []

    @reg.register("g", gpu=True)
    def g(ctx, payload):
        order.append(payload["name"])

    m = make(db, reg, managers, start=False)
    switches = []
    m.on_family_switch = lambda old, new: switches.append((old, new))
    a = m.submit("g", {"name": "A"}, model_family="zimage")
    b = m.submit("g", {"name": "B"}, model_family="h3")
    c = m.submit("g", {"name": "C"}, model_family="zimage")
    m.start()
    for jid in (a, b, c):
        wait_status(m, jid, "done")
    assert order == ["A", "C", "B"]
    assert switches == [("zimage", "h3")]


def test_dependency_waits_for_upstream(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    release = threading.Event()

    @reg.register("slow")
    def slow(ctx, payload):
        release.wait(5)

    reg.register("ok")(lambda ctx, p: {"ran": True})
    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    up = m.submit("slow")
    down = m.submit("ok", depends_on=[up])
    time.sleep(0.2)
    assert m.get(down)["status"] == "queued"
    release.set()
    assert wait_status(m, down, "done")["result"] == {"ran": True}


def test_dependency_failure_fails_dependent(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("bad")
    def bad(ctx, payload):
        raise JobError("上游坏了")

    reg.register("ok")(lambda ctx, p: None)
    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    up = m.submit("bad")
    down = m.submit("ok", depends_on=[up])
    d = wait_status(m, down, "failed")
    assert "上游" in d["error"]


def test_needs_input_then_provide(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("ask")
    def ask(ctx, payload):
        got = (payload.get("manual") or {}).get("k")
        if got is None:
            raise NeedsInput({"key": "k", "user": "请粘贴结果"})
        return {"got": got}

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    jid = m.submit("ask")
    d = wait_status(m, jid, "waiting_input")
    assert d["result"]["input_request"]["user"] == "请粘贴结果"
    m.provide_input(jid, "答案")
    assert wait_status(m, jid, "done")["result"] == {"got": "答案"}
    with pytest.raises(JobError):
        m.provide_input(jid, "再来")


def test_gpu_exclusive_blocks_gpu_jobs(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    reg.register("g", gpu=True)(lambda ctx, p: None)
    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    handover = []
    m.on_gpu_handover = lambda: handover.append(1)
    with m.gpu_exclusive():
        jid = m.submit("g")
        time.sleep(0.3)
        assert m.get(jid)["status"] == "queued"
    wait_status(m, jid, "done")
    assert handover == [1]


def test_retry_creates_new_job_with_override(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()

    @reg.register("flaky")
    def flaky(ctx, payload):
        if not payload.get("fixed"):
            raise JobError("还没修")
        return {"ok": True}

    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    first = m.submit("flaky", {"a": 1}, project_id=7)
    wait_status(m, first, "failed")
    second = m.retry(first, {"fixed": True})
    assert second != first
    d = wait_status(m, second, "done")
    assert d["payload"] == {"a": 1, "fixed": True} and d["project_id"] == 7
    with pytest.raises(JobError):
        m.retry(second)


def test_queue_state_and_list(db, managers):
    reg = HandlerRegistry()
    reg.register("noop")(lambda ctx, p: None)
    m = make(db, reg, managers)
    m.pause()
    a = m.submit("noop", project_id=1)
    m.submit("noop", project_id=2)
    st = m.queue_state()
    assert st["paused"] is True and st["counts"]["queued"] == 2
    assert [j["id"] for j in m.list_jobs(project_id=1)] == [a]
    assert len(m.list_jobs(statuses=["queued"])) == 2
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        m.get(9999)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 context.py**

```python
"""处理器拿到的上下文：写日志、报进度、检查取消、访问服务。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from typing import Any

from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled


class JobContext:
    def __init__(self, manager: Any, job_id: int, cancel_event: threading.Event):
        self.manager = manager
        self.job_id = job_id
        self.cancel_event = cancel_event

    @property
    def services(self) -> Any:
        return self.manager.services

    def log(self, msg: str) -> None:
        self.manager.append_log(self.job_id, msg)

    def progress(self, value: int, maximum: int) -> None:
        self.manager.bus.publish({"type": "progress", "job_id": self.job_id,
                                  "value": value, "max": maximum})

    def check_canceled(self) -> None:
        if self.cancel_event.is_set():
            raise JobCanceled()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 manager.py**

```python
"""任务队列：一个 GPU worker（串行）+ N 个 CPU worker，状态持久化在 jobs 表。

- GPU worker 持有 _gpu_lock 期间执行任务；gpu_exclusive() 抢同一把锁，
  用于本机 LLM（LM Studio）与 ComfyUI 互斥。
- 依赖：depends_on 里的任务全部 done 才可执行；任一 failed/canceled/不存在，
  依赖它的任务直接判失败，不会永远挂着。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
import time
import traceback
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import func, select

from backend.domain.db import Database
from backend.domain.models import Job, utcnow
from backend.queue.context import JobContext
from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled, JobError, NeedsInput
from backend.queue.events import EventBus
from backend.queue.handlers import HandlerRegistry
from backend.queue.scheduler import QueuedJob, pick_next

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ACTIVE = ("queued", "running", "waiting_input")
DEAD = ("failed", "canceled", "missing")


def _iso(dt: datetime | None) -> str | None:
    return dt.isoformat() + "Z" if dt else None


def job_to_dict(j: Job) -> dict:
    return {"id": j.id, "type": j.type, "status": j.status, "project_id": j.project_id,
            "owner_type": j.owner_type, "owner_id": j.owner_id, "payload": j.payload or {},
            "gpu": j.gpu, "model_family": j.model_family, "priority": j.priority,
            "depends_on": j.depends_on or [], "log": j.log or "", "error": j.error,
            "result": j.result, "created_at": _iso(j.created_at),
            "started_at": _iso(j.started_at), "finished_at": _iso(j.finished_at)}


class JobManager:
    def __init__(self, db: Database, registry: HandlerRegistry, bus: Any = None, *,
                 cpu_workers: int = 2, poll: float = 0.5):
        self.db = db
        self.registry = registry
        self.bus = bus or EventBus()
        self.services: Any = None
        self.on_family_switch: Callable[[str | None, str], None] | None = None
        self.on_gpu_handover: Callable[[], None] | None = None
        self.cpu_workers = cpu_workers
        self.poll = poll
        self.current_family: str | None = None
        self._gpu_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._claim_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._count_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._wake = threading.Condition()
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._paused = threading.Event()
        self._llm_waiting = 0
        self._cancel: dict[int, threading.Event] = {}
        self._threads: list[threading.Thread] = []

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 生命周期
    def recover(self) -> int:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            jobs = list(s.scalars(select(Job).where(Job.status == "running")))
            for j in jobs:
                j.status = "failed"
                j.error = "interrupted：服务重启时任务仍在运行，可一键重试"
                j.finished_at = utcnow()
        return len(jobs)

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._stop.clear()
        loops = [("gpu-worker", self._gpu_loop)] + [
            (f"cpu-worker-{i}", self._cpu_loop) for i in range(self.cpu_workers)]
        for name, fn in loops:
            t = threading.Thread(target=fn, name=name, daemon=True)
            t.start()
            self._threads.append(t)

    def stop(self, timeout: float = 10.0) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        for ev in list(self._cancel.values()):
            ev.set()
        self._notify()
        for t in self._threads:
            t.join(timeout)
        self._threads.clear()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 提交与控制
    def submit(self, job_type: str, payload: dict | None = None, *, project_id: int | None = None,
               owner_type: str | None = None, owner_id: int | None = None, priority: int = 0,
               model_family: str | None = None, depends_on: list[int] | None = None) -> int:
        spec = self.registry.get(job_type)
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = Job(type=job_type, payload=dict(payload or {}), gpu=spec.gpu,
                    project_id=project_id, owner_type=owner_type, owner_id=owner_id,
                    priority=priority, model_family=model_family,
                    depends_on=list(depends_on or []))
            s.add(j)
            s.flush()
            jid, d = j.id, job_to_dict(j)
        self._publish(d)
        self._notify()
        return jid

    def cancel(self, job_id: int) -> bool:
        d = None
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, job_id)
            if j is None:
                raise LookupError(f"找不到任务 {job_id}")
            if j.status in ("queued", "waiting_input"):
                j.status = "canceled"
                j.finished_at = utcnow()
                d = job_to_dict(j)
            elif j.status == "running":
                ev = self._cancel.get(job_id)
                if ev is None:
                    return False
                ev.set()
                return True
            else:
                return False
        self._publish(d)
        return True

    def retry(self, job_id: int, payload_override: dict | None = None) -> int:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, job_id)
            if j is None:
                raise LookupError(f"找不到任务 {job_id}")
            if j.status not in ("failed", "canceled"):
                raise JobError(f"只有失败或取消的任务可以重试（当前 {j.status}）")
            payload = {**(j.payload or {}), **(payload_override or {})}
            meta = dict(project_id=j.project_id, owner_type=j.owner_type, owner_id=j.owner_id,
                        priority=j.priority, model_family=j.model_family)
            job_type = j.type
        return self.submit(job_type, payload, **meta)

    def provide_input(self, job_id: int, text: str) -> dict:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, job_id)
            if j is None:
                raise LookupError(f"找不到任务 {job_id}")
            if j.status != "waiting_input":
                raise JobError(f"任务 {job_id} 不在等待输入状态（当前 {j.status}）")
            key = ((j.result or {}).get("input_request") or {}).get("key", "default")
            payload = dict(j.payload or {})
            manual = dict(payload.get("manual") or {})
            manual[key] = text
            payload["manual"] = manual
            j.payload = payload
            j.status = "queued"
            d = job_to_dict(j)
        self._publish(d)
        self._notify()
        return d

    @property
    def paused(self) -> bool:
        return self._paused.is_set()

    def pause(self) -> None:
        self._paused.set()
        self.bus.publish({"type": "queue", **self.queue_state()})

    def resume(self) -> None:
        self._paused.clear()
        self._notify()
        self.bus.publish({"type": "queue", **self.queue_state()})

    def queue_state(self) -> dict:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            rows = s.execute(select(Job.status, func.count()).where(Job.status.in_(ACTIVE))
                             .group_by(Job.status)).all()
        counts = {st: 0 for st in ACTIVE}
        counts.update({st: n for st, n in rows})
        return {"paused": self.paused, "counts": counts, "current_family": self.current_family}

    def get(self, job_id: int) -> dict:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, job_id)
            if j is None:
                raise LookupError(f"找不到任务 {job_id}")
            return job_to_dict(j)

    def list_jobs(self, statuses: list[str] | None = None, project_id: int | None = None,
                  limit: int = 100) -> list[dict]:
        q = select(Job).order_by(Job.id.desc()).limit(limit)
        if statuses:
            q = q.where(Job.status.in_(statuses))
        if project_id is not None:
            q = q.where(Job.project_id == project_id)
        with self.db.session() as s:
            return [job_to_dict(j) for j in s.scalars(q)]

    def append_log(self, job_id: int, msg: str) -> None:
        line = f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}\n"
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, job_id)
            if j is not None:
                j.log = (j.log or "") + line
        self.bus.publish({"type": "log", "job_id": job_id, "line": line})

    @contextmanager
    def gpu_exclusive(self) -> Iterator[None]:
        with self._count_lock:
            self._llm_waiting += 1
        try:
            self._gpu_lock.acquire()
        finally:
            with self._count_lock:
                self._llm_waiting -= 1
        try:
            if self.on_gpu_handover:
                try:
                    self.on_gpu_handover()
                except Exception as e:  # 释放显存失败不影响 LLM 调用
                    log.warning("让出显卡前释放显存失败：%s", e)
            self.current_family = None
            yield
        finally:
            self._gpu_lock.release()
            self._notify()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ worker
    def _gpu_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            if self._paused.is_set() or self._llm_waiting:
                self._sleep()
                continue
            ran = False
            with self._gpu_lock:
                jid = self._claim(gpu=True)
                if jid is not None:
                    self._execute(jid)
                    ran = True
            if not ran:
                self._sleep()

    def _cpu_loop(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            if self._paused.is_set():
                self._sleep()
                continue
            jid = self._claim(gpu=False)
            if jid is None:
                self._sleep()
                continue
            self._execute(jid)

    def _claim(self, *, gpu: bool) -> int | None:
        events: list[dict] = []
        picked: int | None = None
        with self._claim_lock, self.db.session() as s:
            rows = list(s.scalars(select(Job).where(Job.status == "queued", Job.gpu == gpu)))
            dep_ids = {d for j in rows for d in (j.depends_on or [])}
            statuses: dict[int, str] = {}
            if dep_ids:
                statuses = {i: st for i, st in s.execute(
                    select(Job.id, Job.status).where(Job.id.in_(dep_ids))).all()}
            ready = []
            for j in rows:
                deps = [statuses.get(d, "missing") for d in (j.depends_on or [])]
                if any(st in DEAD for st in deps):
                    j.status = "failed"
                    j.error = "上游任务失败、被取消或不存在，本任务未执行"
                    j.finished_at = utcnow()
                    events.append(job_to_dict(j))
                elif all(st == "done" for st in deps):
                    ready.append(QueuedJob(j.id, j.priority, j.model_family))
            choice = pick_next(ready, self.current_family if gpu else None)
            if choice is not None:
                j = s.get(Job, choice.id)
                j.status = "running"
                j.started_at = utcnow()
                self._cancel[j.id] = threading.Event()
                picked = j.id
                events.append(job_to_dict(j))
        for d in events:
            self._publish(d)
        return picked

    def _execute(self, jid: int) -> None:
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, jid)
            job_type, payload, family, is_gpu = j.type, dict(j.payload or {}), j.model_family, j.gpu
        cancel = self._cancel.setdefault(jid, threading.Event())
        status, error, result = "done", None, None
        try:
            spec = self.registry.get(job_type)
            if is_gpu and family:
                if self.current_family and family != self.current_family and self.on_family_switch:
                    try:
                        self.on_family_switch(self.current_family, family)
                    except Exception as e:
                        log.warning("换模型族前释放显存失败：%s", e)
                self.current_family = family
            if cancel.is_set():
                raise JobCanceled()
            result = spec.fn(JobContext(self, jid, cancel), payload)
        except JobCanceled:
            status = "canceled"
        except NeedsInput as e:
            status, result = "waiting_input", {"input_request": e.request}
        except JobError as e:
            status, error = "failed", str(e)
        except Exception as e:
            status, error = "failed", f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            log.exception("任务 %s 失败", jid)
            self.append_log(jid, traceback.format_exc())
        finally:
            self._cancel.pop(jid, None)
        with self.db.session() as s:
            j = s.get(Job, jid)
            j.status = status
            j.error = error
            if result is not None:
                j.result = result
            if status != "waiting_input":
                j.finished_at = utcnow()
            d = job_to_dict(j)
        self._publish(d)
        self._notify()

    def _publish(self, d: dict | None) -> None:
        if d is not None:
            self.bus.publish({"type": "job", "job": d})

    def _sleep(self) -> None:
        with self._wake:
            self._wake.wait(self.poll)

    def _notify(self) -> None:
        with self._wake:
            self._wake.notify_all()
```

### Task 6: ComfyUI HTTP 客户端与错误分类

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/engines/__init__.py`, `backend/engines/comfyui/__init__.py`, `backend/engines/comfyui/errors.py`, `backend/engines/comfyui/client.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_comfy_client.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 无
- Produces:
  - `ComfyError(RuntimeError)`：属性 `retryable: bool`、`node_id: str | None`、`node_type: str | None`
  - `error_from_response(path, response) -> ComfyError`（解析 4xx 的 `node_errors`）
  - `execution_error(history_entry: dict) -> ComfyError | None`（解析 history 里的 `execution_error`）
  - `ComfyClient(base_url, *, timeout=30.0, max_retries=6, backoff=1.0, transport=None)`：属性 `base_url`、`client_id`、`ws_url`；方法 `system_stats(*, tries=None, timeout=None) -> dict`、`object_info() -> dict`、`queue_prompt(workflow) -> str`（prompt_id）、`history(prompt_id) -> dict | None`、`upload_image(path) -> str`（返回 LoadImage 可用的名字；同内容只传一次）、`view(filename, subfolder="", type="output") -> bytes`、`interrupt()`、`clear_queue()`、`free()`、`vram_free_gb() -> float | None`
  - 重试规则：连接错误、超时、5xx 重试到 `max_retries` 次后抛 `ComfyError(retryable=True)`；4xx 立刻抛 `ComfyError(retryable=False)`，带出错节点

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_comfy_client.py`：

```python
import httpx
import pytest

from backend.engines.comfyui.client import ComfyClient
from backend.engines.comfyui.errors import ComfyError, execution_error


def make(handler, **kw):
    return ComfyClient("http://comfy.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler), backoff=0, **kw)


def test_retries_connection_errors_then_succeeds():
    calls = {"n": 0}

    def handler(req):
        calls["n"] += 1
        if calls["n"] < 3:
            raise httpx.ConnectError("refused", request=req)
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"devices": []})

    assert make(handler).system_stats() == {"devices": []}
    assert calls["n"] == 3


def test_gives_up_as_retryable_after_max_retries():
    c = make(lambda req: httpx.Response(503, text="busy"), max_retries=2)
    with pytest.raises(ComfyError) as ei:
        c.system_stats()
    assert ei.value.retryable is True
    assert "重试 2 次" in str(ei.value)


def test_node_error_is_not_retried():
    calls = {"n": 0}
    body = {"error": {"type": "prompt_outputs_failed_validation",
                      "message": "Prompt outputs failed validation", "details": ""},
            "node_errors": {"1": {"class_type": "UNETLoader", "errors": [
                {"type": "value_not_in_list", "message": "Value not in list",
                 "details": "unet_name: 'x.safetensors' not in []"}]}}}

    def handler(req):
        calls["n"] += 1
        return httpx.Response(400, json=body)

    with pytest.raises(ComfyError) as ei:
        make(handler).queue_prompt({"1": {}})
    e = ei.value
    assert calls["n"] == 1
    assert e.retryable is False and e.node_id == "1" and e.node_type == "UNETLoader"
    assert "x.safetensors" in str(e)


def test_upload_dedupes_and_names_by_content(tmp_path):
    bodies = []

    def handler(req):
        bodies.append(req.read().decode("latin-1"))
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"name": "studio_x.png", "subfolder": "sub", "type": "input"})

    img = tmp_path / "参考 图.PNG"
    img.write_bytes(b"\x89PNGdata")
    c = make(handler)
    assert c.upload_image(img) == "sub/studio_x.png"
    assert c.upload_image(img) == "sub/studio_x.png"
    assert len(bodies) == 1
    assert 'filename="studio_' in bodies[0] and '.png"' in bodies[0]


def test_history_missing_returns_none():
    c = make(lambda req: httpx.Response(200, json={}))
    assert c.history("abc") is None


def test_ws_url():
    c = ComfyClient("http://127.0.0.1:8188/")
    assert c.ws_url == f"ws://127.0.0.1:8188/ws?clientId={c.client_id}"


def test_execution_error_parsing():
    entry = {"status": {"status_str": "error", "messages": [
        ["execution_start", {}],
        ["execution_error", {"node_id": "12", "node_type": "VAEDecode",
                             "exception_message": "CUDA out of memory\n"}]]}}
    e = execution_error(entry)
    assert e.node_id == "12" and "CUDA out of memory" in str(e) and not e.retryable
    assert execution_error({"status": {"status_str": "success", "messages": []}}) is None
    assert execution_error({"status": {"status_str": "error", "messages": []}}) is not None
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 errors.py**

创建空文件 `backend/engines/__init__.py`、`backend/engines/comfyui/__init__.py`。

`backend/engines/comfyui/errors.py`：

```python
"""ComfyUI 错误。连接类可重试；工作流校验、模型缺失、执行报错不可重试，并带出错节点。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import httpx


class ComfyError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, *, retryable: bool = False,
                 node_id: str | None = None, node_type: str | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.retryable = retryable
        self.node_id = node_id
        self.node_type = node_type


def error_from_response(path: str, r: httpx.Response) -> ComfyError:
    try:
        body = r.json()
    except ValueError:
        return ComfyError(f"ComfyUI {path} 返回 HTTP {r.status_code}：{r.text[:500]}")
    for nid, ne in (body.get("node_errors") or {}).items():
        e0 = (ne.get("errors") or [{}])[0]
        detail = e0.get("details") or ""
        msg = f"ComfyUI 拒绝工作流：节点 {nid}（{ne.get('class_type', '?')}）{e0.get('message', '')}"
        if detail:
            msg += f"：{detail}"
        return ComfyError(msg, node_id=str(nid), node_type=ne.get("class_type"))
    err = body.get("error")
    if isinstance(err, dict):
        msg = err.get("message") or str(body)[:500]
        if err.get("details"):
            msg += f"：{err['details']}"
    else:
        msg = str(err or body)[:500]
    return ComfyError(f"ComfyUI {path} 返回 HTTP {r.status_code}：{msg}")


def execution_error(entry: dict) -> ComfyError | None:
    status = entry.get("status") or {}
    for m in status.get("messages") or []:
        if isinstance(m, list) and len(m) == 2 and m[0] == "execution_error":
            d = m[1] or {}
            return ComfyError(
                f"ComfyUI 执行失败：节点 {d.get('node_id')}（{d.get('node_type')}）"
                f"{str(d.get('exception_message', '')).strip()}",
                node_id=str(d.get("node_id")), node_type=d.get("node_type"))
    if status.get("status_str") == "error":
        return ComfyError("ComfyUI 执行失败（没有返回详细信息，看 ComfyUI 控制台）")
    return None
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 client.py**

```python
"""ComfyUI HTTP 客户端。移植自 ai_videos/vglib/core.py，改用 httpx。

和旧版的区别：
- 只有连接失败、超时、5xx 才重试；4xx（工作流校验失败、模型文件不存在）立即失败并带出错节点。
- 参考图走 /upload/image，按内容哈希命名，同一张图只传一次，不再往 input 目录直接写文件。
- trust_env=False：绕开系统代理，否则 localhost 请求会挂住。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import time
import uuid
from pathlib import Path

import httpx

from backend.engines.comfyui.errors import ComfyError, error_from_response


class ComfyClient:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str, *, timeout: float = 30.0, max_retries: int = 6,
                 backoff: float = 1.0, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None):
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.client_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.max_retries = max(1, max_retries)
        self.backoff = backoff
        self._http = httpx.Client(base_url=self.base_url, timeout=timeout, trust_env=False,
                                  transport=transport)
        self._uploaded: dict[str, str] = {}

    @property
    def ws_url(self) -> str:
        url = self.base_url.replace("https://", "wss://", 1).replace("http://", "ws://", 1)
        return f"{url}/ws?clientId={self.client_id}"

    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, *, tries: int | None = None,
                 timeout: float | None = None, **kw) -> httpx.Response:
        tries = tries or self.max_retries
        if timeout is not None:
            kw["timeout"] = timeout
        last = ""
        for attempt in range(tries):
            try:
                r = self._http.request(method, path, **kw)
            except httpx.TransportError as e:
                last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
            else:
                if r.status_code < 400:
                    return r
                if r.status_code < 500:
                    raise error_from_response(path, r)
                last = f"HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:300]}"
            if attempt + 1 < tries:
                time.sleep(self.backoff * min(attempt + 1, 5))
        raise ComfyError(f"连不上 ComfyUI（{self.base_url}{path}），重试 {tries} 次仍失败：{last}",
                         retryable=True)

    def system_stats(self, *, tries: int | None = None, timeout: float | None = None) -> dict:
        return self._request("GET", "/system_stats", tries=tries, timeout=timeout).json()

    def object_info(self) -> dict:
        return self._request("GET", "/object_info", timeout=120).json()

    def queue_prompt(self, workflow: dict) -> str:
        r = self._request("POST", "/prompt", json={"prompt": workflow, "client_id": self.client_id})
        return r.json()["prompt_id"]

    def history(self, prompt_id: str) -> dict | None:
        return self._request("GET", f"/history/{prompt_id}").json().get(prompt_id)

    def upload_image(self, path: Path) -> str:
        path = Path(path)
        data = path.read_bytes()
        digest = hashlib.sha1(data).hexdigest()[:16]
        if digest in self._uploaded:
            return self._uploaded[digest]
        name = f"studio_{digest}{path.suffix.lower() or '.png'}"
        r = self._request("POST", "/upload/image",
                          files={"image": (name, data, "application/octet-stream")},
                          data={"overwrite": "true", "type": "input"}, timeout=180)
        d = r.json()
        ref = f"{d['subfolder']}/{d['name']}" if d.get("subfolder") else d["name"]
        self._uploaded[digest] = ref
        return ref

    def view(self, filename: str, subfolder: str = "", type: str = "output") -> bytes:
        return self._request("GET", "/view", params={"filename": filename, "subfolder": subfolder,
                                                     "type": type}, timeout=300).content

    def interrupt(self) -> None:
        self._request("POST", "/interrupt", json={}, tries=3)

    def clear_queue(self) -> None:
        self._request("POST", "/queue", json={"clear": True}, tries=3)

    def free(self) -> None:
        self._request("POST", "/free", json={"unload_models": True, "free_memory": True}, tries=3)

    def vram_free_gb(self) -> float | None:
        try:
            return self.system_stats(tries=2)["devices"][0]["vram_free"] / 2**30
        except (ComfyError, KeyError, IndexError):
            return None
```
