### Task 7: 引擎基础类型与 ComfyRunner（上传、提交、进度、取消、下载）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/engines/base.py`, `backend/engines/comfyui/runner.py`
- Create: `backend/tests/fakes/__init__.py`, `backend/tests/fakes/fake_comfy.py`
- Modify: `backend/tests/conftest.py`（加 `fake_comfy` fixture）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_comfy_runner.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 `ComfyClient`、`ComfyError`、`execution_error`；Task 4 `JobCanceled`
- Produces（`backend/engines/base.py`，Task 8 以后都用）：
  - `UPLOAD_PREFIX = "@upload:"`；`upload_ref(path) -> str`（`"@upload:" + 绝对路径`）。引擎在工作流里用它标记要上传的本地图片，由 runner 换成上传后的名字。
  - `ImageCaps(max_refs, sizes: dict[str, tuple[int,int]], supports_negative)`、`VideoCaps(max_refs, supports_end_frame, has_audio, fps, sizes, default_seconds, max_seconds)`
  - `size_for(sizes, aspect) -> tuple[int,int]`（不支持的画幅抛 `ValueError`）
  - `ImageRequest(prompt, aspect="9:16", seed=0, negative="", refs=[], prefix="studio/img")`
  - `VideoRequest(prompt, first_frame: Path, aspect="9:16", seed=0, seconds=5.0, negative="", end_frame=None, refs=[], prefix="studio/vid")`
  - `EngineCall(engine, workflow, output_kind: "image"|"video", model_family, meta={}, dropped_refs=[])`
  - 协议 `ImageEngine`（`name`、`family`、`caps`、`build(req) -> EngineCall`）、`VideoEngine`（另有 `frames_for(seconds) -> int`）
- Produces（runner）：`ComfyRunner(client, *, poll_interval=1.0, use_ws=True)`：
  - `prepare(workflow) -> dict`：深拷贝并把所有 `@upload:` 字符串换成上传后的名字
  - `run(call, *, run_dir, cancel: threading.Event, on_progress=None, timeout=3600.0) -> list[Path]`：在 `run_dir` 写 `workflow.api.json` 和 `call.json`，返回下载到 `run_dir` 的产物；取消时 interrupt + 清队列 + free 后抛 `JobCanceled`；超时同样收摊后抛 `ComfyError(retryable=True)`
  - `cancel_current()`、`free()`（都吞掉 `ComfyError`）
- Produces（测试）：`FakeComfy`（真实 HTTP+WS 的假 ComfyUI）和 conftest fixture `fake_comfy`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写假 ComfyUI**

创建空文件 `backend/tests/fakes/__init__.py`。

`backend/tests/fakes/fake_comfy.py`：

```python
"""测试用的假 ComfyUI：真实 HTTP + WebSocket 服务，跑在后台线程。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import socket
import threading
import time
import uuid

import uvicorn
from fastapi import FastAPI, File, Form, Request, UploadFile, WebSocket
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, Response


def _free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]


class FakeComfy:
    def __init__(self):
        self.prompts: dict[str, dict] = {}
        self.uploads: dict[str, bytes] = {}
        self.calls: list[str] = []
        self.reject: dict | None = None        # /prompt 返回 400 时的 body
        self.exec_error: dict | None = None    # history 里放 execution_error
        self.hold = False                      # True：任务一直不完成，直到 finish_all()
        self.progress_steps = 3
        self.output_name = "out_00001_.png"
        self.output_kind = "images"
        self.output_bytes = b"fake-output"
        self.object_info: dict = {}
        self.events: list[str] = []
        self.port = _free_port()
        self.url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}"
        self.app = self._build()
        self._server: uvicorn.Server | None = None
        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None

    def finish_all(self) -> None:
        for p in self.prompts.values():
            p["done"] = True

    def last_workflow(self) -> dict:
        return list(self.prompts.values())[-1]["workflow"]

    def _build(self) -> FastAPI:
        app = FastAPI()

        @app.get("/system_stats")
        def system_stats():
            self.calls.append("system_stats")
            return {"system": {"ram_free": 32 * 2**30},
                    "devices": [{"name": "fake", "vram_free": 12 * 2**30,
                                 "vram_total": 16 * 2**30}]}

        @app.get("/object_info")
        def object_info():
            return self.object_info

        @app.post("/prompt")
        async def prompt(req: Request):
            body = await req.json()
            self.calls.append("prompt")
            if self.reject is not None:
                return JSONResponse(self.reject, status_code=400)
            pid = uuid.uuid4().hex
            self.prompts[pid] = {"workflow": body["prompt"], "done": not self.hold}
            for i in range(1, self.progress_steps + 1):
                self.events.append(json.dumps({"type": "progress", "data": {
                    "value": i, "max": self.progress_steps, "prompt_id": pid, "node": "8"}}))
            return {"prompt_id": pid, "number": len(self.prompts), "node_errors": {}}

        @app.get("/history/{pid}")
        def history(pid: str):
            p = self.prompts.get(pid)
            if not p or not p["done"]:
                return {}
            if self.exec_error is not None:
                return {pid: {"outputs": {}, "status": {
                    "status_str": "error", "completed": False,
                    "messages": [["execution_start", {}], ["execution_error", self.exec_error]]}}}
            out = {self.output_kind: [{"filename": self.output_name, "subfolder": "studio",
                                       "type": "output"}]}
            return {pid: {"outputs": {"9": out}, "status": {
                "status_str": "success", "completed": True, "messages": []}}}

        @app.get("/view")
        def view(filename: str, subfolder: str = "", type: str = "output"):
            self.calls.append(f"view:{subfolder}/{filename}")
            return Response(self.output_bytes, media_type="application/octet-stream")

        @app.post("/upload/image")
        async def upload(image: UploadFile = File(...), overwrite: str = Form("false"),
                         type: str = Form("input")):
            self.uploads[image.filename] = await image.read()
            self.calls.append(f"upload:{image.filename}")
            return {"name": image.filename, "subfolder": "", "type": "input"}

        @app.post("/interrupt")
        def interrupt():
            self.calls.append("interrupt")
            return {}

        @app.post("/queue")
        def queue():
            self.calls.append("clear_queue")
            return {}

        @app.post("/free")
        def free():
            self.calls.append("free")
            return {}

        @app.websocket("/ws")
        async def ws(sock: WebSocket):
            await sock.accept()
            sent = len(self.events)  # 只推连上之后产生的事件
            try:
                while True:
                    while sent < len(self.events):
                        await sock.send_text(self.events[sent])
                        sent += 1
                    await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
            except Exception:
                pass

        return app

    def start(self) -> None:
        config = uvicorn.Config(self.app, host="127.0.0.1", port=self.port, log_level="warning",
                                timeout_graceful_shutdown=1)
        self._server = uvicorn.Server(config)
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._server.run, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()
        end = time.monotonic() + 10
        while not self._server.started:
            if time.monotonic() > end:
                raise RuntimeError("FakeComfy 启动超时")
            time.sleep(0.02)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        if self._server:
            self._server.should_exit = True
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join(5)
```

`backend/tests/test_comfy_runner.py`：

```python
import threading

import pytest

from backend.engines.base import EngineCall, upload_ref
from backend.engines.comfyui.client import ComfyClient
from backend.engines.comfyui.errors import ComfyError
from backend.engines.comfyui.runner import ComfyRunner
from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled


def runner_for(fake, **kw):
    return ComfyRunner(ComfyClient(fake.url, backoff=0, max_retries=2), poll_interval=0.05, **kw)


def make_call(wf=None):
    return EngineCall(engine="zimage",
                      workflow=wf or {"9": {"class_type": "SaveImage", "inputs": {}}},
                      output_kind="image", model_family="zimage", meta={"prompt": "p"})


def test_run_downloads_and_reports_progress(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.hold = True
    threading.Timer(0.5, fake_comfy.finish_all).start()
    progress = []
    files = runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "run",
                                       cancel=threading.Event(),
                                       on_progress=lambda v, m: progress.append((v, m)))
    assert [f.name for f in files] == ["out_00001_.png"]
    assert files[0].read_bytes() == b"fake-output"
    assert (tmp_path / "run" / "workflow.api.json").exists()
    assert (tmp_path / "run" / "call.json").exists()
    assert progress and progress[-1] == (3, 3)
    assert "view:studio/out_00001_.png" in fake_comfy.calls


def test_upload_placeholders_replaced(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    img = tmp_path / "首帧.png"
    img.write_bytes(b"\x89PNG")
    wf = {"1": {"class_type": "LoadImage", "inputs": {"image": upload_ref(img)}},
          "2": {"class_type": "LoadImage", "inputs": {"image": upload_ref(img)}},
          "9": {"class_type": "SaveImage", "inputs": {}}}
    runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(wf), run_dir=tmp_path / "run", cancel=threading.Event())
    sent = fake_comfy.last_workflow()
    name = sent["1"]["inputs"]["image"]
    assert name.startswith("studio_") and name == sent["2"]["inputs"]["image"]
    assert fake_comfy.uploads[name] == b"\x89PNG"
    assert [c for c in fake_comfy.calls if c.startswith("upload:")] == [f"upload:{name}"]


def test_execution_error_raises(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.exec_error = {"node_id": "12", "node_type": "VAEDecode",
                             "exception_message": "CUDA out of memory"}
    with pytest.raises(ComfyError) as ei:
        runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r", cancel=threading.Event())
    assert ei.value.node_id == "12" and not ei.value.retryable


def test_rejected_workflow_reports_node(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.reject = {"error": {"message": "Prompt outputs failed validation"},
                         "node_errors": {"1": {"class_type": "UNETLoader", "errors": [
                             {"message": "Value not in list", "details": "unet_name: 'x' not in []"}]}}}
    with pytest.raises(ComfyError) as ei:
        runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r", cancel=threading.Event())
    assert ei.value.node_type == "UNETLoader"


def test_cancel_interrupts_clears_and_frees(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.hold = True
    cancel = threading.Event()
    threading.Timer(0.3, cancel.set).start()
    with pytest.raises(JobCanceled):
        runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r", cancel=cancel)
    assert {"interrupt", "clear_queue", "free"} <= set(fake_comfy.calls)


def test_timeout_interrupts(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.hold = True
    with pytest.raises(ComfyError, match="超过"):
        runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r",
                                   cancel=threading.Event(), timeout=0.3)
    assert "interrupt" in fake_comfy.calls


def test_works_without_websocket(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    files = runner_for(fake_comfy, use_ws=False).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r",
                                                     cancel=threading.Event())
    assert files[0].exists()


def test_video_outputs_downloaded(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    fake_comfy.output_kind = "images"   # 新版 SaveVideo 也放在 images 下
    fake_comfy.output_name = "clip_00001_.mp4"
    files = runner_for(fake_comfy).run(make_call(), run_dir=tmp_path / "r", cancel=threading.Event())
    assert files[0].suffix == ".mp4"
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 base.py**

`backend/engines/base.py`：

```python
"""引擎接口。build() 只负责把请求拼成调用（ComfyUI 工作流），执行交给 runner。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

UPLOAD_PREFIX = "@upload:"


def upload_ref(path: Path) -> str:
    """在工作流里标记一张要上传的本地图片，runner 提交前会换成上传后的名字。"""
    return UPLOAD_PREFIX + str(Path(path).resolve())


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ImageCaps:
    max_refs: int
    sizes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]]
    supports_negative: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VideoCaps:
    max_refs: int
    supports_end_frame: bool
    has_audio: bool
    fps: int
    sizes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]]
    default_seconds: float
    max_seconds: float


def size_for(sizes: dict[str, tuple[int, int]], aspect: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    if aspect not in sizes:
        raise ValueError(f"不支持画幅 {aspect}，可选：{', '.join(sizes)}")
    return sizes[aspect]


@dataclass
class ImageRequest:
    prompt: str
    aspect: str = "9:16"
    seed: int = 0
    negative: str = ""
    refs: list[Path] = field(default_factory=list)
    prefix: str = "studio/img"


@dataclass
class VideoRequest:
    prompt: str
    first_frame: Path
    aspect: str = "9:16"
    seed: int = 0
    seconds: float = 5.0
    negative: str = ""
    end_frame: Path | None = None
    refs: list[Path] = field(default_factory=list)
    prefix: str = "studio/vid"


@dataclass
class EngineCall:
    engine: str
    workflow: dict
    output_kind: Literal["image", "video"]
    model_family: str
    meta: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    dropped_refs: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class ImageEngine(Protocol):
    name: str
    family: str
    caps: ImageCaps

    def build(self, req: ImageRequest) -> EngineCall: ...


class VideoEngine(Protocol):
    name: str
    family: str
    caps: VideoCaps

    def frames_for(self, seconds: float) -> int: ...

    def build(self, req: VideoRequest) -> EngineCall: ...
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 runner.py**

`backend/engines/comfyui/runner.py`：

```python
"""执行 EngineCall：上传参考图 → 提交 → 等结果（WebSocket 推进度）→ 下载产物。

产物通过 /view 下载到 run_dir，不依赖 ComfyUI 的 output 目录在哪里。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import threading
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from websockets.exceptions import ConnectionClosed
from websockets.sync.client import connect as ws_connect

from backend.engines.base import UPLOAD_PREFIX, EngineCall
from backend.engines.comfyui.client import ComfyClient
from backend.engines.comfyui.errors import ComfyError, execution_error
from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled

OUTPUT_KEYS = ("images", "gifs", "videos", "audio")


class ComfyRunner:
    def __init__(self, client: ComfyClient, *, poll_interval: float = 1.0, use_ws: bool = True):
        self.client = client
        self.poll_interval = poll_interval
        self.use_ws = use_ws

    def prepare(self, workflow: dict) -> dict:
        wf = json.loads(json.dumps(workflow))
        for node in wf.values():
            inputs = node.get("inputs") or {}
            for key, val in list(inputs.items()):
                if isinstance(val, str) and val.startswith(UPLOAD_PREFIX):
                    inputs[key] = self.client.upload_image(Path(val[len(UPLOAD_PREFIX):]))
        return wf

    def run(self, call: EngineCall, *, run_dir: Path, cancel: threading.Event,
            on_progress: Callable[[int, int], None] | None = None,
            timeout: float = 3600.0) -> list[Path]:
        run_dir = Path(run_dir)
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        wf = self.prepare(call.workflow)
        (run_dir / "workflow.api.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(wf, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        (run_dir / "call.json").write_text(json.dumps(
            {"engine": call.engine, "output_kind": call.output_kind,
             "model_family": call.model_family, "meta": call.meta,
             "dropped_refs": call.dropped_refs},
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
        ws = self._open_ws()
        try:
            pid = self.client.queue_prompt(wf)
            entry = self._wait(pid, ws, cancel, on_progress, timeout)
        finally:
            if ws is not None:
                try:
                    ws.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        err = execution_error(entry)
        if err is not None:
            raise err
        return self._download(entry, run_dir)

    def cancel_current(self) -> None:
        for fn in (self.client.interrupt, self.client.clear_queue, self.client.free):
            try:
                fn()
            except ComfyError:
                pass

    def free(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.client.free()
        except ComfyError:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------
    def _open_ws(self):
        if not self.use_ws:
            return None
        try:
            return ws_connect(self.client.ws_url, open_timeout=5, max_size=None, proxy=None)
        except TypeError:  # 旧版 websockets 没有 proxy 参数
            try:
                return ws_connect(self.client.ws_url, open_timeout=5, max_size=None)
            except Exception:
                return None
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _wait(self, pid: str, ws, cancel: threading.Event,
              on_progress: Callable[[int, int], None] | None, timeout: float) -> dict:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        next_check = 0.0
        while True:
            if cancel.is_set():
                self.cancel_current()
                raise JobCanceled()
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                self.cancel_current()
                raise ComfyError(f"ComfyUI 任务 {pid} 超过 {timeout:.0f} 秒没完成，已中断",
                                 retryable=True)
            if ws is not None:
                try:
                    msg = ws.recv(timeout=self.poll_interval)
                    if isinstance(msg, str):
                        self._handle(msg, pid, on_progress)
                except TimeoutError:
                    pass
                except ConnectionClosed:
                    ws = None
            else:
                time.sleep(self.poll_interval)
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= next_check:
                next_check = now + self.poll_interval
                entry = self.client.history(pid)
                if entry is not None:
                    return entry

    @staticmethod
    def _handle(raw: str, pid: str, on_progress: Callable[[int, int], None] | None) -> None:
        try:
            msg = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            return
        if msg.get("type") != "progress" or on_progress is None:
            return
        data = msg.get("data") or {}
        if data.get("prompt_id") in (pid, None):
            on_progress(int(data.get("value", 0)), int(data.get("max", 0)))

    def _download(self, entry: dict, run_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
        paths: list[Path] = []
        for out in (entry.get("outputs") or {}).values():
            for key in OUTPUT_KEYS:
                for f in out.get(key) or []:
                    if not isinstance(f, dict) or "filename" not in f or f.get("type") == "temp":
                        continue
                    dst = run_dir / f["filename"]
                    dst.write_bytes(self.client.view(f["filename"], f.get("subfolder", ""),
                                                     f.get("type", "output")))
                    paths.append(dst)
        if not paths:
            raise ComfyError("ComfyUI 任务完成了，但没有产出文件（检查工作流里的保存节点）")
        return paths
```

### Task 8: 工作流模板与四个 ComfyUI 引擎

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/engines/comfyui/template.py`, `backend/engines/comfyui/engines.py`, `backend/engines/registry.py`
- Create: `workflows/zimage.json`, `workflows/zimage.map.yaml`, `workflows/h3_image.json`, `workflows/h3_image.map.yaml`, `workflows/h3_video.json`, `workflows/h3_video.map.yaml`, `workflows/wan22_i2v.json`, `workflows/wan22_i2v.map.yaml`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_engines.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `Config`、`EngineCfg`；Task 7 `base.py` 全部
- Produces:
  - `WorkflowTemplate(name, workflow, params: dict[str, list[tuple[node, input]]], defaults: dict, model_params: list[str])`：`load(workflows_dir, name)`（校验 map 指向的节点和输入都存在，否则抛 `ValueError`）、`class_types() -> set[str]`、`model_files(overrides=None) -> list[str]`、`render(values) -> dict`（缺参数抛 `KeyError`）
  - 引擎类（都有 `name`、`family`、`caps`、`template_name`、`tpl`、`overrides`）：`ZImageEngine`（family `zimage`）、`H3ImageEngine`（`h3`）、`H3VideoEngine`（`h3`，`frames_for`）、`Wan22I2VEngine`（`wan22`，`frames_for`）
  - `EngineRegistry(cfg)`：`get(name)`、`image(name)`、`video(name)`（未启用、不存在或类型不对抛 `KeyError`）、`names(kind=None) -> list[str]`（排序）、`all() -> dict`
  - 参数优先级：请求里的值 > `config.yaml` 的 `engines.<name>.params` > `*.map.yaml` 的 `defaults`
  - map.yaml 格式：`params: {参数名: [[节点id, 输入名], ...]}`、`defaults: {...}`、`models: [代表模型文件的参数名, ...]`

工作流内容移植自 `D:\works\ai_videos\vglib\engines.py`（`build_zimage`、`build_minimax_h3_image`、`build_minimax_h3`、`build_wan22`），在 ai_videos 里已实测可用。节点 id 与原代码保持一致。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_engines.py`：

```python
import pytest

from backend.config import Config, EngineCfg
from backend.engines.base import UPLOAD_PREFIX, ImageRequest, VideoRequest
from backend.engines.comfyui.template import WorkflowTemplate
from backend.engines.registry import EngineRegistry


@pytest.fixture
def reg():
    return EngineRegistry(Config())


def test_all_templates_load(reg):
    assert reg.names() == ["h3_image", "h3_video", "wan22_i2v", "zimage"]
    assert reg.names("image") == ["h3_image", "zimage"]
    assert reg.names("video") == ["h3_video", "wan22_i2v"]
    assert "MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo" in reg.get("h3_video").tpl.class_types()


def test_template_rejects_bad_map(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "t.json").write_text('{"1": {"class_type": "X", "inputs": {"a": 1}}}', encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "t.map.yaml").write_text('params:\n  p: [["2", "a"]]\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不存在的节点"):
        WorkflowTemplate.load(tmp_path, "t")
    (tmp_path / "t.map.yaml").write_text('params:\n  p: [["1", "b"]]\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="没有输入"):
        WorkflowTemplate.load(tmp_path, "t")


def test_template_missing_param_raises(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "t.json").write_text('{"1": {"class_type": "X", "inputs": {"a": 1}}}', encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "t.map.yaml").write_text('params:\n  p: [["1", "a"]]\n', encoding="utf-8")
    tpl = WorkflowTemplate.load(tmp_path, "t")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="缺少参数 p"):
        tpl.render({})
    assert tpl.render({"p": 5})["1"]["inputs"]["a"] == 5
    assert tpl.workflow["1"]["inputs"]["a"] == 1  # 模板本身不被改动


def test_zimage_build(reg):
    call = reg.image("zimage").build(ImageRequest(prompt="a cat", negative="blurry", aspect="16:9",
                                                  seed=42, prefix="studio/demo/kf_S01"))
    wf = call.workflow
    assert wf["5"]["inputs"]["text"] == "a cat" and wf["6"]["inputs"]["text"] == "blurry"
    assert (wf["7"]["inputs"]["width"], wf["7"]["inputs"]["height"]) == (1344, 768)
    assert wf["8"]["inputs"]["seed"] == 42
    assert wf["10"]["inputs"]["filename_prefix"] == "studio/demo/kf_S01"
    assert call.model_family == "zimage" and call.output_kind == "image"


def test_unsupported_aspect(reg):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="画幅"):
        reg.image("zimage").build(ImageRequest(prompt="x", aspect="4:3"))


def test_h3_image_refs_and_truncation(reg, tmp_path):
    refs = [tmp_path / f"r{i}.png" for i in range(11)]
    call = reg.image("h3_image").build(ImageRequest(prompt="p", refs=refs, seed=1))
    wf = call.workflow
    assert wf["6"]["inputs"]["ref_images.ref_image_0"] == ["ref_0", 0]
    assert wf["ref_0"]["inputs"]["image"] == UPLOAD_PREFIX + str(refs[0].resolve())
    assert "ref_images.ref_image_8" in wf["6"]["inputs"]
    assert "ref_images.ref_image_9" not in wf["6"]["inputs"]
    assert call.dropped_refs == [str(refs[9]), str(refs[10])]
    assert wf["9"]["inputs"]["steps"] == 20 and "1b" not in wf


def test_h3_turbo_rewires_model():
    reg = EngineRegistry(Config(engines={"h3_image": EngineCfg(params={"turbo": True})}))
    wf = reg.image("h3_image").build(ImageRequest(prompt="p")).workflow
    assert wf["1b"]["class_type"] == "LoraLoaderModelOnly"
    assert wf["7"]["inputs"]["model"] == ["1b", 0] and wf["9"]["inputs"]["model"] == ["1b", 0]
    assert wf["9"]["inputs"]["steps"] == 4


def test_h3_video_frames_and_first_frame(reg, tmp_path):
    eng = reg.video("h3_video")
    assert eng.frames_for(5) == 124 and eng.frames_for(3) == 73 and eng.frames_for(0.1) == 5
    assert all((eng.frames_for(s) - 5) % 17 == 0 for s in (1, 2.5, 4, 7, 10))
    ff = tmp_path / "首帧.png"
    call = eng.build(VideoRequest(prompt="walk\nAudio: rain", first_frame=ff, seconds=5, seed=7,
                                  refs=[tmp_path / "c.png"]))
    wf = call.workflow
    assert wf["5"]["inputs"]["image"] == UPLOAD_PREFIX + str(ff.resolve())
    assert wf["6"]["inputs"]["length"] == 124 and wf["14"]["inputs"]["fps"] == 24.0
    assert wf["6"]["inputs"]["prompt"] == "walk\nAudio: rain"
    assert wf["6"]["inputs"]["ref_images.ref_image_1"] == ["ref_0", 0]
    assert wf["1b"]["class_type"] == "LoraLoaderModelOnly"   # 视频默认 turbo
    assert eng.caps.has_audio and call.model_family == "h3" and call.output_kind == "video"


def test_h3_video_clamps_to_max_seconds(reg, tmp_path):
    eng = reg.video("h3_video")
    wf = eng.build(VideoRequest(prompt="p", first_frame=tmp_path / "f.png", seconds=30)).workflow
    assert wf["6"]["inputs"]["length"] == eng.frames_for(eng.caps.max_seconds)


def test_wan_frames_and_size(reg, tmp_path):
    eng = reg.video("wan22_i2v")
    assert eng.frames_for(5) == 81 and eng.frames_for(1) == 17
    wf = eng.build(VideoRequest(prompt="p", first_frame=tmp_path / "f.png", aspect="9:16",
                                seconds=5, seed=3)).workflow
    assert (wf["12"]["inputs"]["width"], wf["12"]["inputs"]["height"]) == (480, 832)
    assert wf["13"]["inputs"]["noise_seed"] == 3 and wf["14"]["inputs"]["noise_seed"] == 3
    assert wf["12"]["inputs"]["length"] == 81 and wf["16"]["inputs"]["fps"] == 16.0
    assert not eng.caps.has_audio


def test_config_override_and_disabled_engine():
    reg = EngineRegistry(Config(engines={
        "zimage": EngineCfg(params={"unet": "custom.safetensors", "steps": 6}),
        "wan22_i2v": EngineCfg(enabled=False)}))
    z = reg.image("zimage")
    wf = z.build(ImageRequest(prompt="p")).workflow
    assert wf["1"]["inputs"]["unet_name"] == "custom.safetensors"
    assert wf["8"]["inputs"]["steps"] == 6
    assert z.tpl.model_files(z.overrides)[0] == "custom.safetensors"
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="wan22_i2v"):
        reg.video("wan22_i2v")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="不是视频引擎"):
        reg.video("zimage")
```

  "8": {"class_type": "VAELoader", "inputs": {"vae_name": ""}},

  "10": {"class_type": "CLIPTextEncode", "inputs": {"clip": ["7", 0], "text": ""}},

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 template.py**

```python
"""ComfyUI 工作流模板：workflows/<name>.json（API 格式）+ <name>.map.yaml（参数 → 节点输入）。

在 ComfyUI 界面里调好的图，导出 API 格式，再写一份 map，就能接进来。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import yaml


@dataclass
class WorkflowTemplate:
    name: str
    workflow: dict
    params: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]
    defaults: dict
    model_params: list[str]

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, workflows_dir: Path, name: str) -> "WorkflowTemplate":
        wf_path = Path(workflows_dir) / f"{name}.json"
        map_path = Path(workflows_dir) / f"{name}.map.yaml"
        workflow = json.loads(wf_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        m = yaml.safe_load(map_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
        params = {k: [(str(n), str(i)) for n, i in v] for k, v in (m.get("params") or {}).items()}
        for key, targets in params.items():
            for node, inp in targets:
                if node not in workflow:
                    raise ValueError(f"{map_path.name}：参数 {key} 指向不存在的节点 {node}")
                if inp not in (workflow[node].get("inputs") or {}):
                    raise ValueError(f"{map_path.name}：节点 {node} 没有输入 {inp}")
        return cls(name, workflow, params, dict(m.get("defaults") or {}),
                   list(m.get("models") or []))

    def class_types(self) -> set[str]:
        return {n["class_type"] for n in self.workflow.values()}

    def model_files(self, overrides: dict | None = None) -> list[str]:
        vals = {**self.defaults, **(overrides or {})}
        return [str(vals[k]) for k in self.model_params if vals.get(k)]

    def render(self, values: dict) -> dict:
        merged = {**self.defaults, **{k: v for k, v in values.items() if v is not None}}
        wf = copy.deepcopy(self.workflow)
        for key, targets in self.params.items():
            if key not in merged:
                raise KeyError(f"工作流模板 {self.name} 缺少参数 {key}")
            for node, inp in targets:
                wf[node]["inputs"][inp] = merged[key]
        return wf
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 engines.py**

```python
"""第一版四个 ComfyUI 引擎。参数与帧数规则移植自 vglib/engines.py（已实测）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from backend.engines.base import (EngineCall, ImageCaps, ImageRequest, VideoCaps, VideoRequest,
                                  size_for, upload_ref)
from backend.engines.comfyui.template import WorkflowTemplate


class _ComfyEngine:
    name = ""
    family = ""
    template_name = ""

    def __init__(self, tpl: WorkflowTemplate, overrides: dict | None = None):
        self.tpl = tpl
        self.overrides = dict(overrides or {})

    def _render(self, values: dict) -> dict:
        return self.tpl.render({**self.overrides, **values})

    def _setting(self, key: str, default=None):
        return self.overrides.get(key, self.tpl.defaults.get(key, default))


def _add_h3_refs(wf: dict, refs: list[Path], start: int) -> None:
    for i, path in enumerate(refs):
        nid = f"ref_{i}"
        wf[nid] = {"class_type": "LoadImage", "inputs": {"image": upload_ref(path)}}
        wf["6"]["inputs"][f"ref_images.ref_image_{start + i}"] = [nid, 0]


def _add_h3_turbo(wf: dict, lora_name: str) -> None:
    # LoRA 必须同时接到 BasicGuider 和 BasicScheduler，否则 sigma 与模型对不上
    wf["1b"] = {"class_type": "LoraLoaderModelOnly",
                "inputs": {"model": ["1", 0], "strength_model": 1.0, "lora_name": lora_name}}
    wf["7"]["inputs"]["model"] = ["1b", 0]
    wf["9"]["inputs"]["model"] = ["1b", 0]


class ZImageEngine(_ComfyEngine):
    name = "zimage"
    family = "zimage"
    template_name = "zimage"
    caps = ImageCaps(max_refs=0, supports_negative=True,
                     sizes={"9:16": (768, 1344), "16:9": (1344, 768), "1:1": (1024, 1024)})

    def build(self, req: ImageRequest) -> EngineCall:
        w, h = size_for(self.caps.sizes, req.aspect)
        wf = self._render({"prompt": req.prompt, "negative": req.negative, "width": w,
                           "height": h, "seed": req.seed, "prefix": req.prefix})
        return EngineCall(self.name, wf, "image", self.family,
                          meta={"prompt": req.prompt, "negative": req.negative, "seed": req.seed,
                                "size": [w, h]},
                          dropped_refs=[str(p) for p in req.refs])


class H3ImageEngine(_ComfyEngine):
    """H3 ref2va 出 5 帧取第 0 帧。20 步定稿；turbo 4 步只适合探构图（静帧会糊）。"""
    name = "h3_image"
    family = "h3"
    template_name = "h3_image"
    caps = ImageCaps(max_refs=9, supports_negative=False,
                     sizes={"9:16": (768, 1344), "16:9": (1344, 768), "1:1": (960, 960)})

    def build(self, req: ImageRequest) -> EngineCall:
        w, h = size_for(self.caps.sizes, req.aspect)
        turbo = bool(self.overrides.get("turbo", False))
        steps = self.overrides.get("steps") or (4 if turbo else 20)
        keep, dropped = req.refs[: self.caps.max_refs], req.refs[self.caps.max_refs:]
        wf = self._render({"prompt": req.prompt, "width": w // 32 * 32, "height": h // 32 * 32,
                           "seed": req.seed, "steps": steps, "prefix": req.prefix})
        _add_h3_refs(wf, keep, start=0)
        if turbo:
            _add_h3_turbo(wf, self._setting("turbo_lora"))
        return EngineCall(self.name, wf, "image", self.family,
                          meta={"prompt": req.prompt, "seed": req.seed, "size": [w, h],
                                "steps": steps, "turbo": turbo, "refs": [str(p) for p in keep]},
                          dropped_refs=[str(p) for p in dropped])


class H3VideoEngine(_ComfyEngine):
    """H3 参考图 → 视频 + 原生立体声。帧数落在 17k+5 网格（24fps）。"""
    name = "h3_video"
    family = "h3"
    template_name = "h3_video"
    caps = VideoCaps(max_refs=8, supports_end_frame=False, has_audio=True, fps=24,
                     sizes={"9:16": (768, 1344), "16:9": (1344, 768)},
                     default_seconds=5.0, max_seconds=10.0)

    def frames_for(self, seconds: float) -> int:
        n = max(1, round(seconds * self.caps.fps))
        return max(5, n + (5 - n % 17) % 17)

    def build(self, req: VideoRequest) -> EngineCall:
        w, h = size_for(self.caps.sizes, req.aspect)
        turbo = bool(self.overrides.get("turbo", True))
        steps = self.overrides.get("steps") or (4 if turbo else 20)
        frames = self.frames_for(min(req.seconds, self.caps.max_seconds))
        keep, dropped = req.refs[: self.caps.max_refs], req.refs[self.caps.max_refs:]
        wf = self._render({"prompt": req.prompt, "width": w, "height": h, "frames": frames,
                           "fps": float(self.caps.fps), "seed": req.seed, "steps": steps,
                           "prefix": req.prefix, "first_frame": upload_ref(req.first_frame)})
        _add_h3_refs(wf, keep, start=1)
        if turbo:
            _add_h3_turbo(wf, self._setting("turbo_lora"))
        return EngineCall(self.name, wf, "video", self.family,
                          meta={"prompt": req.prompt, "seed": req.seed, "size": [w, h],
                                "frames": frames, "fps": self.caps.fps, "steps": steps,
                                "turbo": turbo, "first_frame": str(req.first_frame),
                                "refs": [str(p) for p in keep]},
                          dropped_refs=[str(p) for p in dropped])


class Wan22I2VEngine(_ComfyEngine):
    """Wan2.2 I2V 14B + lightx2v 4 步。无声，16fps，帧数为 4k+1。"""
    name = "wan22_i2v"
    family = "wan22"
    template_name = "wan22_i2v"
    caps = VideoCaps(max_refs=0, supports_end_frame=False, has_audio=False, fps=16,
                     sizes={"9:16": (480, 832), "16:9": (832, 480)},
                     default_seconds=5.0, max_seconds=5.0)

    def frames_for(self, seconds: float) -> int:
        n = max(1, round(seconds * self.caps.fps))
        return max(17, (n // 4) * 4 + 1)

    def build(self, req: VideoRequest) -> EngineCall:
        w, h = size_for(self.caps.sizes, req.aspect)
        frames = self.frames_for(min(req.seconds, self.caps.max_seconds))
        values = {"prompt": req.prompt, "first_frame": upload_ref(req.first_frame),
                  "width": w, "height": h, "frames": frames, "seed": req.seed,
                  "fps": float(self.caps.fps), "prefix": req.prefix}
        if req.negative:
            values["negative"] = req.negative
        wf = self._render(values)
        return EngineCall(self.name, wf, "video", self.family,
                          meta={"prompt": req.prompt, "seed": req.seed, "size": [w, h],
                                "frames": frames, "fps": self.caps.fps,
                                "first_frame": str(req.first_frame)},
                          dropped_refs=[str(p) for p in req.refs])
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 实现 registry.py**

```python
"""引擎注册表：按 config.yaml 的 engines.<name>.enabled 加载。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from backend.config import Config
from backend.engines.base import ImageCaps, VideoCaps
from backend.engines.comfyui.engines import (H3ImageEngine, H3VideoEngine, Wan22I2VEngine,
                                             ZImageEngine)
from backend.engines.comfyui.template import WorkflowTemplate

ENGINE_CLASSES = {cls.name: cls for cls in (ZImageEngine, H3ImageEngine, H3VideoEngine,
                                            Wan22I2VEngine)}


class EngineRegistry:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Config):
        self._engines: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for name, cls in ENGINE_CLASSES.items():
            ec = cfg.engine(name)
            if not ec.enabled:
                continue
            tpl = WorkflowTemplate.load(cfg.workflows_dir, cls.template_name)
            self._engines[name] = cls(tpl, ec.params)

    def get(self, name: str) -> Any:
        try:
            return self._engines[name]
        except KeyError:
            raise KeyError(f"引擎 {name} 不存在或未启用，可用：{', '.join(self.names())}") from None

    def image(self, name: str) -> Any:
        e = self.get(name)
        if not isinstance(e.caps, ImageCaps):
            raise KeyError(f"{name} 不是图像引擎")
        return e

    def video(self, name: str) -> Any:
        e = self.get(name)
        if not isinstance(e.caps, VideoCaps):
            raise KeyError(f"{name} 不是视频引擎")
        return e

    def names(self, kind: str | None = None) -> list[str]:
        want = {"image": ImageCaps, "video": VideoCaps}.get(kind or "")
        return sorted(n for n, e in self._engines.items() if want is None or isinstance(e.caps, want))

    def all(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return dict(self._engines)
```

### Task 9: ffmpeg：探测、截尾帧、成片合成（含有声/无声混剪）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/engines/ffmpeg.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_ffmpeg.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 无
- Produces:
  - `FFmpegError(RuntimeError)`
  - `parse_transition(raw: str | None) -> tuple[str, float]`（xfade 名称、秒数；`hard_cut` → `("fade", 0.05)`）
  - `build_xfade_graph(durs: list[float], transitions: list[str], fps: int, with_audio: bool) -> tuple[str, float]`（filter_complex 字符串、成片时长；输出标签 `[vout]`，有声时还有 `[aout]`）
  - `build_normalize_cmd(ffmpeg, src, dst, *, width, height, fps, duration, audio: "keep"|"silent"|"none") -> list[str]`
  - `FFmpeg(ffmpeg="ffmpeg", ffprobe="ffprobe")`：属性 `ffmpeg`、`ffprobe`；方法 `run(args) -> str`、`version() -> str`、`video_duration(path) -> float`、`has_audio(path) -> bool`、`last_frame(video, out_png) -> Path`、`assemble(clips, out, *, width, height, fps, transitions, work_dir) -> Path`
  - 合成规则：只要有一个片段有声，无声片段补静音轨，整片保留声音；视频用 xfade、音频用同样时长的 acrossfade，保证音画对齐。`transitions` 长度为片段数 − 1。

合成逻辑移植自 `vglib/core.py` 的 `assemble`（xfade offset 相对已拼接时长计算、留一帧余量），改动：时长用 JSON 输出的 ffprobe 读取；音频从"全部有声才保留"改成"有声就保留、无声补静音"；音频改用 acrossfade 与视频转场对齐。调色（eq/unsharp）留到 M3 后期。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_ffmpeg.py`：

```python
import json
import shutil
import subprocess

import pytest

from backend.engines.ffmpeg import (FFmpeg, FFmpegError, build_normalize_cmd, build_xfade_graph,
                                    parse_transition)

needs_ffmpeg = pytest.mark.skipif(not (shutil.which("ffmpeg") and shutil.which("ffprobe")),
                                  reason="没有 ffmpeg")


def test_parse_transition():
    assert parse_transition("hard_cut") == ("fade", 0.05)
    assert parse_transition(None) == ("fade", 0.05)
    assert parse_transition("dissolve_0.5") == ("fade", 0.5)
    assert parse_transition("white_flash_0.2") == ("fadewhite", 0.2)
    assert parse_transition("black_dip_0.3") == ("fadeblack", 0.3)
    assert parse_transition("dissolve") == ("fade", 0.4)


def test_xfade_offsets_and_audio():
    graph, total = build_xfade_graph([5.0, 5.0, 5.0], ["dissolve_0.5", "hard_cut"], 24,
                                     with_audio=True)
    assert "[0:v][1:v]xfade=transition=fade:duration=0.500:offset=4.458[v0]" in graph
    assert "[v0][2:v]xfade=transition=fade:duration=0.050:offset=9.367[v1]" in graph
    assert "[0:a][1:a]acrossfade=d=0.500[a0]" in graph
    assert "[vout]" in graph and "[aout]" in graph
    assert abs(total - 14.3667) < 1e-3


def test_xfade_without_audio():
    graph, _ = build_xfade_graph([3.0, 3.0], ["hard_cut"], 16, with_audio=False)
    assert "acrossfade" not in graph and "[aout]" not in graph


def test_normalize_cmd_modes():
    kw = dict(width=1080, height=1920, fps=24, duration=5.0)
    keep = build_normalize_cmd("ffmpeg", "a.mp4", "n.mp4", audio="keep", **kw)
    assert "scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=1080:1920,fps=24,setsar=1" in keep
    assert "0:a:0" in keep and "apad" in keep and "-an" not in keep
    silent = build_normalize_cmd("ffmpeg", "a.mp4", "n.mp4", audio="silent", **kw)
    assert "anullsrc=r=48000:cl=stereo" in silent and "1:a:0" in silent
    none = build_normalize_cmd("ffmpeg", "a.mp4", "n.mp4", audio="none", **kw)
    assert "-an" in none


def test_assemble_empty_raises(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(FFmpegError):
        FFmpeg().assemble([], tmp_path / "o.mp4", width=270, height=480, fps=24,
                          transitions=[], work_dir=tmp_path / "w")


def make_clip(path, seconds, audio):
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc=size=320x240:rate=24:duration={seconds}"]
    if audio:
        cmd += ["-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=frequency=440:duration={seconds}"]
    cmd += ["-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p"]
    if audio:
        cmd += ["-c:a", "aac"]
    subprocess.run(cmd + [str(path)], check=True, capture_output=True)
    return path


@needs_ffmpeg
def test_assemble_mixed_audio_keeps_sound(tmp_path):
    ff = FFmpeg()
    d = tmp_path / "素材 目录"
    d.mkdir()
    clips = [make_clip(d / "一.mp4", 1.5, True), make_clip(d / "二.mp4", 1.5, False),
             make_clip(d / "三.mp4", 1.5, True)]
    out = tmp_path / "成片" / "第1集.mp4"
    ff.assemble(clips, out, width=270, height=480, fps=24,
                transitions=["dissolve_0.5", "hard_cut"], work_dir=tmp_path / "work")
    assert out.exists() and ff.has_audio(out)
    v = ff.video_duration(out)
    assert 3.6 < v < 4.2  # 4.5 - 0.5 - 0.05 - 两帧余量 ≈ 3.87
    info = json.loads(ff.run([ff.ffprobe, "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "a:0",
                              "-show_entries", "stream=duration", "-of", "json", str(out)]))
    assert abs(float(info["streams"][0]["duration"]) - v) < 0.15


@needs_ffmpeg
def test_single_clip_and_last_frame(tmp_path):
    ff = FFmpeg()
    clip = make_clip(tmp_path / "a.mp4", 1.0, False)
    out = ff.assemble([clip], tmp_path / "o.mp4", width=270, height=480, fps=24,
                      transitions=[], work_dir=tmp_path / "w")
    assert out.exists() and not ff.has_audio(out)
    png = ff.last_frame(clip, tmp_path / "尾帧.png")
    assert png.stat().st_size > 0
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 ffmpeg.py**

```python
"""ffmpeg 封装：探测、截尾帧、成片合成。

合成移植自 vglib/core.assemble：xfade 的 offset 相对"已拼接内容的时长"计算并留一帧余量，
否则误差逐段累积，offset 越过末尾会让 xfade 静默截断。改动见计划 Task 9。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

XFADE = {"dissolve": "fade", "white_flash": "fadewhite", "black_dip": "fadeblack",
         "fade_out": "fade"}


class FFmpegError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def parse_transition(raw: str | None) -> tuple[str, float]:
    raw = raw or "hard_cut"
    if raw.startswith("hard_cut"):
        return "fade", 0.05
    name, _, num = raw.rpartition("_")
    try:
        dur = float(num)
    except ValueError:
        name, dur = raw, 0.4
    return XFADE.get(name, "fade"), dur


def build_xfade_graph(durs: list[float], transitions: list[str], fps: int,
                      with_audio: bool) -> tuple[str, float]:
    eps = 1.0 / max(fps, 1)
    filters: list[str] = []
    last_v, last_a = "0:v", "0:a"
    acc = durs[0]
    for i in range(len(durs) - 1):
        kind, dur = parse_transition(transitions[i] if i < len(transitions) else None)
        dur = max(min(dur, acc - 2 * eps, durs[i + 1] - 2 * eps), eps)
        offset = max(acc - dur - eps, 0.0)
        filters.append(f"[{last_v}][{i + 1}:v]xfade=transition={kind}:duration={dur:.3f}"
                       f":offset={offset:.3f}[v{i}]")
        last_v = f"v{i}"
        if with_audio:
            filters.append(f"[{last_a}][{i + 1}:a]acrossfade=d={dur:.3f}[a{i}]")
            last_a = f"a{i}"
        acc = offset + durs[i + 1]
    fade_out = max(acc - 0.8, 0.0)
    filters.append(f"[{last_v}]fade=t=in:st=0:d=0.6,fade=t=out:st={fade_out:.3f}:d=0.8[vout]")
    if with_audio:
        filters.append(f"[{last_a}]afade=t=out:st={fade_out:.3f}:d=0.8[aout]")
    return ";".join(filters), acc


def build_normalize_cmd(ffmpeg: str, src, dst, *, width: int, height: int, fps: int,
                        duration: float, audio: Literal["keep", "silent", "none"]) -> list[str]:
    vf = (f"scale={width}:{height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,"
          f"crop={width}:{height},fps={fps},setsar=1")
    cmd = [ffmpeg, "-y", "-i", str(src)]
    if audio == "silent":
        cmd += ["-f", "lavfi", "-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-i", "anullsrc=r=48000:cl=stereo"]
    cmd += ["-t", f"{duration:.3f}", "-vf", vf, "-map", "0:v:0"]
    if audio == "keep":
        cmd += ["-map", "0:a:0", "-af", "apad"]
    elif audio == "silent":
        cmd += ["-map", "1:a:0"]
    cmd += ["-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", "17", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p"]
    if audio == "none":
        cmd += ["-an"]
    else:
        cmd += ["-c:a", "aac", "-ar", "48000", "-ac", "2", "-b:a", "192k"]
    return cmd + [str(dst)]


class FFmpeg:
    def __init__(self, ffmpeg: str = "ffmpeg", ffprobe: str = "ffprobe"):
        self.ffmpeg = ffmpeg
        self.ffprobe = ffprobe

    def run(self, args: list[str]) -> str:
        try:
            p = subprocess.run([str(a) for a in args], capture_output=True, text=True,
                               encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except FileNotFoundError as e:
            raise FFmpegError(f"找不到 {args[0]}：{e}") from e
        if p.returncode != 0:
            raise FFmpegError(f"{Path(str(args[0])).name} 失败：\n{p.stderr[-1500:]}")
        return p.stdout

    def version(self) -> str:
        return self.run([self.ffmpeg, "-version"])

    def video_duration(self, path: Path) -> float:
        """视频流的真实时长。不能用容器时长：音轨常比画面长几十毫秒，会让 xfade 越界。"""
        out = self.run([self.ffprobe, "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "v:0", "-show_entries",
                        "stream=duration,nb_frames,r_frame_rate:format=duration", "-of", "json",
                        str(path)])
        d = json.loads(out or "{}")
        st = (d.get("streams") or [{}])[0]
        try:
            nb = int(st.get("nb_frames"))
            num, den = str(st.get("r_frame_rate", "0/1")).split("/")
            fps = float(num) / float(den)
            if nb > 0 and fps > 0:
                return nb / fps
        except (TypeError, ValueError, ZeroDivisionError):
            pass
        for v in (st.get("duration"), (d.get("format") or {}).get("duration")):
            try:
                if float(v) > 0:
                    return float(v)
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                pass
        raise FFmpegError(f"读不出视频时长：{path}")

    def has_audio(self, path: Path) -> bool:
        out = self.run([self.ffprobe, "-v", "error", "-select_streams", "a", "-show_entries",
                        "stream=codec_name", "-of", "csv=p=0", str(path)])
        return bool(out.strip())

    def last_frame(self, video: Path, out_png: Path) -> Path:
        out_png = Path(out_png)
        out_png.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.run([self.ffmpeg, "-y", "-sseof", "-1", "-i", str(video), "-update", "1",
                  "-q:v", "2", str(out_png)])
        if not out_png.exists():
            raise FFmpegError(f"没截到尾帧：{video}")
        return out_png

    def assemble(self, clips: list[Path], out: Path, *, width: int, height: int, fps: int,
                 transitions: list[str], work_dir: Path) -> Path:
        if not clips:
            raise FFmpegError("没有片段可合成")
        out, work_dir = Path(out), Path(work_dir)
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        flags = [self.has_audio(c) for c in clips]
        any_audio = any(flags)
        norm: list[Path] = []
        durs: list[float] = []
        for i, (clip, has) in enumerate(zip(clips, flags)):
            dst = work_dir / f"n{i:03d}.mp4"
            mode = "keep" if has else ("silent" if any_audio else "none")
            self.run(build_normalize_cmd(self.ffmpeg, clip, dst, width=width, height=height,
                                         fps=fps, duration=self.video_duration(clip), audio=mode))
            norm.append(dst)
            durs.append(self.video_duration(dst))
        if len(norm) == 1:
            shutil.copyfile(norm[0], out)
            return out
        graph, _ = build_xfade_graph(durs, transitions, fps, any_audio)
        cmd = [self.ffmpeg, "-y"]
        for p in norm:
            cmd += ["-i", str(p)]
        cmd += ["-filter_complex", graph, "-map", "[vout]"]
        if any_audio:
            cmd += ["-map", "[aout]", "-c:a", "aac", "-b:a", "192k", "-shortest"]
        cmd += ["-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "medium", "-crf", "18", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
                "-r", str(fps), "-movflags", "+faststart", str(out)]
        self.run(cmd)
        return out
```

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/llm/__init__.py`, `backend/llm/base.py`, `backend/llm/jsonutil.py`, `backend/llm/schemas.py`, `backend/llm/prompts.py`
- Create: `backend/prompts/script_structure.md`, `backend/prompts/script_visual.md`, `backend/prompts/storyboard.md`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_llm_utils.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 无
- Produces:
  - `LLMResult(text, model=None, usage=None, seconds=0.0)`；`LLMError(RuntimeError)`；协议 `LLMProvider`（`id`、`complete(system, messages, *, json_mode=True) -> LLMResult`）
  - `extract_json(raw: str) -> Any`（去掉 `<think>…</think>`、代码块和前后废话，容忍尾随逗号；失败抛 `LLMError`）
  - 输出结构（Pydantic）：
    - `ScriptStructure(title, characters: list[CharacterOut(name, description)], locations: list[LocationOut(name, time_of_day, atmosphere)], scenes: list[SceneBlockOut(location, text)])`：scene 的 location 必须在 locations 里
    - `ScriptVisual(style: StyleOut(anchor_text, palette, lighting, texture, character_rules, mood_keywords, negative), characters: list[CharacterVisual(name, visual_prompt, core_features)], locations: list[LocationVisual(name, visual_prompt)])`
    - `StoryboardOut(shots: list[ShotOut])`；`ShotOut(shot_size, camera_move, action, cast: list[str], dialogue: list[DialogueOut(speaker, zh, start, end)], image_prompt, motion_prompt, audio_prompt, duration, transition_out)`
    - `TRANSITIONS`（五种转场的元组）
  - `PromptLibrary(builtin_dir=BUILTIN_DIR, override_dir=None)`：`render(name, **ctx) -> (system, user)`；模板用一行 `=== user ===` 分隔 system 和 user；项目目录 `projects/<slug>/prompts/<name>.md` 优先于内置
  - 模板变量：
    - `script_structure`：`project_title`、`target_seconds`、`source_text`
    - `script_visual`：`project_title`、`characters[{name, description}]`、`locations[{name, time_of_day, atmosphere}]`、`excerpt`
    - `storyboard`：`anchor`、`characters[{name, visual_prompt}]`、`location`（`{name, time_of_day, atmosphere, visual_prompt}` 或 None）、`text`、`count`、`shot_seconds`、`max_seconds`、`prev_action`

```python
import jinja2
import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from backend.llm.base import LLMError
from backend.llm.jsonutil import extract_json
from backend.llm.prompts import PromptLibrary
from backend.llm.schemas import ScriptStructure, ShotOut

WORDS = " ".join(["cinematic"] * 90)


def test_extract_json_variants():
    assert extract_json('好的：\n```json\n{"a": 1}\n```') == {"a": 1}
    assert extract_json('<think>先想想 {"x": 0}</think>{"a": [1, 2,],}') == {"a": [1, 2]}
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="没有返回 JSON"):
        extract_json("对不起，我做不到")
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="JSON 解析失败"):
        extract_json('{"a": }')


def test_script_structure_location_must_exist():
    ok = {"title": "t", "characters": [{"name": "林", "description": "d"}],
          "locations": [{"name": "直播间"}], "scenes": [{"location": "直播间", "text": "x"}]}
    assert ScriptStructure.model_validate(ok).scenes[0].location == "直播间"
    bad = {**ok, "scenes": [{"location": "天台", "text": "x"}]}
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="天台"):
        ScriptStructure.model_validate(bad)


def shot(**kw):
    base = {"shot_size": "近景", "camera_move": "static", "action": "a", "image_prompt": WORDS,
            "motion_prompt": "m", "duration": 5}
    return {**base, **kw}


def test_shot_rules():
    assert ShotOut.model_validate(shot()).transition_out == "hard_cut"
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="80–150"):
        ShotOut.model_validate(shot(image_prompt="too short"))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="same as before"):
        ShotOut.model_validate(shot(image_prompt=WORDS + " same as before"))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ShotOut.model_validate(shot(dialogue=[{"speaker": "林", "zh": "这句台词实在是太长太长了超过了十六个字",
                                              "start": 0.5, "end": 2}]))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="duration"):
        ShotOut.model_validate(shot(dialogue=[{"speaker": "林", "zh": "你好", "start": 0.5, "end": 6}]))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ShotOut.model_validate(shot(transition_out="spin"))


def test_render_builtin_storyboard():
    lib = PromptLibrary()
    system, user = lib.render("storyboard", anchor="ANCHOR", count=3, shot_seconds=5,
                              max_seconds=10, text="剧本段落", prev_action="",
                              characters=[{"name": "林", "visual_prompt": "LOOK"}],
                              location={"name": "直播间", "time_of_day": "夜", "atmosphere": "压抑",
                                        "visual_prompt": "ROOM"})
    assert "正好 3 个镜头" in user and "LOOK" in user and "ROOM" in user and "ANCHOR" in user
    assert "image_prompt" in system and "=== user ===" not in system + user


def test_render_all_builtin_templates():
    lib = PromptLibrary()
    s, u = lib.render("script_structure", project_title="提词员", target_seconds=90,
                      source_text="梗概")
    assert "梗概" in u and "90" in u and "JSON" in s
    s, u = lib.render("script_visual", project_title="提词员",
                      characters=[{"name": "林", "description": "主播"}],
                      locations=[{"name": "直播间", "time_of_day": "夜", "atmosphere": "压抑"}],
                      excerpt="节选")
    assert "林" in u and "直播间" in u and "anchor_text" in s


def test_project_override_and_strict_variables(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "storyboard.md").write_text("SYS\n=== user ===\nUSER {{ count }}", encoding="utf-8")
    lib = PromptLibrary(override_dir=tmp_path)
    assert lib.render("storyboard", count=3) == ("SYS", "USER 3")
    with pytest.raises(jinja2.UndefinedError):
        lib.render("storyboard")
```

```python
"""LLM provider 的公共类型。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol


@dataclass
class LLMResult:
    text: str
    model: str | None = None
    usage: dict | None = None
    seconds: float = 0.0


class LLMError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class LLMProvider(Protocol):
    id: str

    def complete(self, system: str, messages: list[dict], *, json_mode: bool = True) -> LLMResult: ...
```

`backend/llm/jsonutil.py`：

```python
"""从模型输出里抠出 JSON 对象。移植自 vglib/script.extract_json，另外去掉推理模型的 <think> 段。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import Any

from backend.llm.base import LLMError

_THINK = re.compile(r"<think>.*?</think>", re.S | re.I)
_TRAILING_COMMA = re.compile(r",\s*([}\]])")


def extract_json(raw: str) -> Any:
    text = _THINK.sub("", raw or "")
    s, e = text.find("{"), text.rfind("}")
    if s < 0 or e <= s:
        raise LLMError("模型没有返回 JSON。原始输出前 400 字：\n" + text[:400])
    blob = text[s:e + 1]
    try:
        return json.loads(blob)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        pass
    try:
        return json.loads(_TRAILING_COMMA.sub(r"\1", blob))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as ex:
        raise LLMError(f"JSON 解析失败：{ex}\n原始输出前 600 字：\n{blob[:600]}") from ex
```

`backend/llm/prompts.py`：

```python
"""提示词模板（Jinja2）。项目目录下的同名模板优先于内置模板。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, StrictUndefined

BUILTIN_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "prompts"
SPLIT = "=== user ==="


class PromptLibrary:
    def __init__(self, builtin_dir: Path = BUILTIN_DIR, override_dir: Path | None = None):
        dirs = [str(d) for d in (override_dir, builtin_dir) if d is not None and Path(d).is_dir()]
        self.env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(dirs), undefined=StrictUndefined,
                               autoescape=False, keep_trailing_newline=True)

    def render(self, name: str, **ctx) -> tuple[str, str]:
        text = self.env.get_template(f"{name}.md").render(**ctx)
        if SPLIT not in text:
            raise ValueError(f"提示词模板 {name}.md 缺少分隔行 {SPLIT}")
        system, user = text.split(SPLIT, 1)
        return system.strip(), user.strip()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 schemas.py**

```python
"""各 LLM 任务的输出结构。校验失败的错误信息会原样喂回模型让它改。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator

TRANSITIONS = ("hard_cut", "dissolve_0.5", "white_flash_0.2", "black_dip_0.3", "fade_out_1.0")
Transition = Literal["hard_cut", "dissolve_0.5", "white_flash_0.2", "black_dip_0.3", "fade_out_1.0"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 剧本结构
class CharacterOut(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1)
    description: str = ""


class LocationOut(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(min_length=1)
    time_of_day: str = ""
    atmosphere: str = ""


class SceneBlockOut(BaseModel):
    location: str
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)


class ScriptStructure(BaseModel):
    title: str = ""
    characters: list[CharacterOut] = Field(default_factory=list)
    locations: list[LocationOut] = Field(min_length=1)
    scenes: list[SceneBlockOut] = Field(min_length=1)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _scene_locations_exist(self):
        names = {loc.name for loc in self.locations}
        for sc in self.scenes:
            if sc.location not in names:
                raise ValueError(f"场景 {sc.location!r} 不在 locations 列表里，可用：{sorted(names)}")
        return self


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 视觉设定
class StyleOut(BaseModel):
    anchor_text: str = Field(min_length=40)
    palette: str = ""
    lighting: str = ""
    texture: str = ""
    character_rules: str = ""
    mood_keywords: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    negative: str = ""


class CharacterVisual(BaseModel):
    name: str
    visual_prompt: str = Field(min_length=20)
    core_features: str = ""


class LocationVisual(BaseModel):
    name: str
    visual_prompt: str = Field(min_length=20)


class ScriptVisual(BaseModel):
    style: StyleOut
    characters: list[CharacterVisual] = Field(default_factory=list)
    locations: list[LocationVisual] = Field(default_factory=list)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 分镜
class DialogueOut(BaseModel):
    speaker: str
    zh: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=16)
    start: float = Field(ge=0)
    end: float

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _order(self):
        if self.end <= self.start:
            raise ValueError(f"台词「{self.zh}」的 end 必须大于 start")
        return self


class ShotOut(BaseModel):
    shot_size: str = ""
    camera_move: str = ""
    action: str
    cast: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    dialogue: list[DialogueOut] = Field(default_factory=list)
    image_prompt: str
    motion_prompt: str
    audio_prompt: str = ""
    duration: float = Field(default=5.0, ge=2, le=10)
    transition_out: Transition = "hard_cut"

    @field_validator("image_prompt")
    @classmethod
    def _image_prompt_rules(cls, v: str) -> str:
        words = len(v.split())
        if words < 60 or words > 180:
            raise ValueError(f"image_prompt 要写 80–150 个英文单词，现在是 {words} 个")
        if "same as" in v.lower():
            raise ValueError("image_prompt 里不能写 same as before / same as previous，必须完整复述外貌与场景")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _dialogue_within_duration(self):
        for d in self.dialogue:
            if d.end > self.duration:
                raise ValueError(f"台词「{d.zh}」的 end={d.end} 超过了镜头 duration={self.duration}")
        return self


class StoryboardOut(BaseModel):
    shots: list[ShotOut] = Field(min_length=1)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 写三个提示词模板**

`backend/prompts/script_structure.md`：

```
你是 AI 漫剧的编剧。把用户给的故事素材整理成一集约 {{ target_seconds|int }} 秒、9:16 竖屏、仿真人风格的漫剧剧本结构。

要求：
- 列出本集出场的主要角色（会反复出现、需要保持长相一致的人），路人不列。
- 列出场景（地点 + 时段），同一地点不同时段算不同场景。
- 按时间顺序把剧情切成剧本段落，每段只发生在一个场景里，写清楚发生了什么、谁说了什么（中文）。
- 前 3 秒要有冲突或悬念；段落数量与时长相称，大约每 10–20 秒一段。

只输出一个 JSON 对象，不要 markdown 代码块，不要解释：
{"title": "本集标题", "characters": [{"name": "角色名", "description": "身份、性格、与其他人的关系（中文）"}], "locations": [{"name": "场景名", "time_of_day": "白天/夜晚/黄昏…", "atmosphere": "氛围（中文）"}], "scenes": [{"location": "必须是 locations 里的某个 name", "text": "这一段的剧情与对白（中文）"}]}
=== user ===
项目：{{ project_title }}
目标时长：{{ target_seconds|int }} 秒

素材：
{{ source_text }}
```

`backend/prompts/script_visual.md`：

```
你是 AI 漫剧的美术指导。根据剧本素材，为整部剧定一套统一的视觉风格，并给每个角色、每个场景写英文外观描述，用于文生图提示词。

要求：
- style.anchor_text：80–120 个英文单词的一致性锚，写清画风（仿真人写实）、镜头与胶片质感、整体色调、光线倾向、皮肤与材质质感。它会被拼到每一个画面提示词前面。
- style 的 palette / lighting / texture / character_rules 用中文简述；mood_keywords 是 3–6 个英文关键词；negative 是英文负面词。
- characters：每个角色一条，name 必须和给你的角色名完全一致，一个都不能少。visual_prompt 是 60–120 个英文单词：年龄、脸型、发型发色、体型、服装及材质、一个标志性细节。只写外观，不写动作和表情。core_features 用中文写 2–4 个最不能变的特征。
- locations：每个场景一条，name 必须和给你的场景名完全一致，一个都不能少。visual_prompt 是 50–100 个英文单词，写空间结构、至少三个具体物件、光源、色调；画面里不要出现人。

只输出一个 JSON 对象，不要 markdown 代码块，不要解释：
{"style": {"anchor_text": "...", "palette": "...", "lighting": "...", "texture": "...", "character_rules": "...", "mood_keywords": ["..."], "negative": "..."}, "characters": [{"name": "...", "visual_prompt": "...", "core_features": "..."}], "locations": [{"name": "...", "visual_prompt": "..."}]}
=== user ===
项目：{{ project_title }}

角色：
{% for c in characters %}- {{ c.name }}：{{ c.description }}
{% endfor %}
场景：
{% for l in locations %}- {{ l.name }}（{{ l.time_of_day }}）：{{ l.atmosphere }}
{% endfor %}
剧本素材节选：
{{ excerpt }}
```

`backend/prompts/storyboard.md`：

```
你是 AI 漫剧的分镜师。把给你的一段剧本拆成正好 {{ count }} 个镜头，画面是 9:16 竖屏、仿真人风格。

每个镜头的要求：
- 只拍一个清楚的动作或状态，不做跨镜头调度；相邻两镜要换景别或换机位。
- shot_size 只用：大特写、特写、近景、中景、全景。
- duration 是秒数，默认 {{ shot_seconds }}，最长 {{ max_seconds }}；台词多的镜头可以长一些。
- image_prompt：80–150 个英文单词，按这个顺序写：
  1. 景别与焦段（如 medium close-up, 85mm）、机位高度与角度
  2. 主体：是谁、姿态、动作瞬间、表情、服装材质
  3. 环境：至少三个看得见的具体物件及其位置、材质
  4. 光线：主光方向、软硬、色温，辅光或轮廓光
  5. 色彩与质感：主色调、对比、空气感、景深
  6. 风格锚点
  出场角色的外貌描述必须从下面的角色表里原样抄进来，每个镜头都抄一遍；禁止写 "same as before" 或 "same as previous shot"。不要堆 beautiful、amazing、cinematic 这类空词，只写画面里看得见的东西。
- motion_prompt：30–60 个英文单词，写主体在这几秒里的微动作、镜头运动（static / slow dolly in / handheld drift / pan left / orbit）和结束时的状态。
- audio_prompt：20–40 个英文单词，写环境声、动作音效、有无音乐；有台词时写明谁在说、什么语气。
- dialogue：只在有人说话时写。zh 是中文台词，每句不超过 16 个字；start / end 是相对本镜开头的秒数，第一句不早于 0.4 秒，最后一句结束后留 0.4 秒，end 不能超过 duration。
- cast：出场角色名，只能从角色表里选；没有人出场就写空数组。
- transition_out 只能是：hard_cut、dissolve_0.5、white_flash_0.2、black_dip_0.3、fade_out_1.0。

只输出一个 JSON 对象，不要 markdown 代码块，不要解释：
{"shots": [{"shot_size": "近景", "camera_move": "slow dolly in", "action": "这一镜发生了什么（中文）", "cast": ["角色名"], "dialogue": [{"speaker": "角色名", "zh": "台词", "start": 0.6, "end": 2.4}], "image_prompt": "...", "motion_prompt": "...", "audio_prompt": "...", "duration": {{ shot_seconds }}, "transition_out": "hard_cut"}]}
=== user ===
风格锚（每个 image_prompt 的风格部分要和它一致）：
{{ anchor or "（暂无）" }}

角色表（外貌描述原样抄进 image_prompt）：
{% for c in characters %}- {{ c.name }}：{{ c.visual_prompt }}
{% else %}（本集没有固定角色）
{% endfor %}
场景：{% if location %}{{ location.name }}（{{ location.time_of_day }}，{{ location.atmosphere }}）
场景外观：{{ location.visual_prompt }}{% else %}未指定{% endif %}

{% if prev_action %}上一镜结束时：{{ prev_action }}
{% endif %}这一段剧本：
{{ text }}

请拆成正好 {{ count }} 个镜头。
```

### Task 11: LLM provider 与网关（校验重试、manual、LM Studio 互斥）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/llm/providers.py`, `backend/llm/gateway.py`, `backend/llm/selftest.py`
- Create: `backend/tests/helpers.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_llm_gateway.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `LLMCfg`、`ProviderCfg`；Task 4 `NeedsInput`；Task 10 `LLMResult`、`LLMError`、`extract_json`
- Produces:
  - `OpenAIProvider(cfg: ProviderCfg, *, transport=None)`：POST `{base_url}/chat/completions`
  - `ClaudeProvider(cfg: ProviderCfg, *, run=subprocess.run, which=shutil.which)`：`use_sdk` 时先走 Claude Agent SDK，任何异常都退回 `claude -p --output-format json --max-turns 1 --system-prompt-file <tmp>`，用户消息走 stdin，工作目录是临时目录
  - `flatten_messages(messages) -> str`
  - `build_providers(cfg: LLMCfg) -> dict[str, LLMProvider]`（manual 不建 provider）
  - `parse_output(text, schema, check=None)`
  - `LLMGateway(cfg: LLMCfg, providers, *, gpu_guard=None, run=subprocess.run)`：
    `generate(task, system, user, schema, *, check=None, log=None, manual=None, key=None) -> schema 实例`
    - 解析或校验（含 `check`）失败时，把错误信息附在对话后面重试，最多再试 2 次；仍失败抛 `LLMError`，信息里带最后一次的原始输出
    - provider 是 manual 时：`manual[key]` 没有就抛 `NeedsInput({"key", "task", "system", "user", "schema"})`；有但不合格也抛 `NeedsInput`，并带上 `error`
    - provider 的 `local_gpu` 为真时，在 `gpu_guard()` 里调用，结束后（仍在 guard 内）执行 `unload_cmd`
  - `backend/tests/helpers.py`：`FakeProvider(texts)`（按顺序返回预置文本，记录每次调用的 `system`、`messages`）

```python
"""测试用的假对象。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from backend.llm.base import LLMResult


class FakeProvider:
    def __init__(self, texts, provider_id: str = "fake"):
        self.id = provider_id
        self.texts = list(texts)
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def complete(self, system, messages, *, json_mode=True):
        self.calls.append({"system": system, "messages": list(messages)})
        if not self.texts:
            raise AssertionError("FakeProvider 没有更多预置回复")
        t = self.texts.pop(0)
        if not isinstance(t, str):
            t = json.dumps(t, ensure_ascii=False)
        return LLMResult(text=t, model="fake", usage=None, seconds=0.0)
```

`backend/tests/test_llm_gateway.py`：

```python
import json
import subprocess
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest
from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.config import LLMCfg, ProviderCfg
from backend.llm.base import LLMError
from backend.llm.gateway import LLMGateway
from backend.llm.providers import ClaudeProvider, OpenAIProvider, build_providers
from backend.queue.errors import NeedsInput
from backend.tests.helpers import FakeProvider


class Out(BaseModel):
    n: int


def gw(texts, kind="openai", **pkw):
    prov = FakeProvider(texts)
    cfg = LLMCfg(default="p", providers=[ProviderCfg(id="p", kind=kind, base_url="http://x/v1", **pkw)])
    return LLMGateway(cfg, {"p": prov}), prov


def test_valid_first_try():
    g, p = gw(['{"n": 1}'])
    assert g.generate("t", "sys", "u", Out).n == 1
    assert len(p.calls) == 1 and p.calls[0]["system"] == "sys"
    assert p.calls[0]["messages"] == [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}]


def test_retry_with_error_feedback():
    g, p = gw(["不是 JSON", '{"n": 2}'])
    assert g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out).n == 2
    msgs = p.calls[1]["messages"]
    assert msgs[1] == {"role": "assistant", "content": "不是 JSON"}
    assert "不符合要求" in msgs[2]["content"]


def test_check_failure_triggers_retry():
    g, p = gw(['{"n": 1}', '{"n": 3}'])

    def check(o):
        if o.n != 3:
            raise ValueError("需要 n=3")

    assert g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out, check=check).n == 3
    assert "需要 n=3" in p.calls[1]["messages"][2]["content"]


def test_gives_up_after_three_and_keeps_raw():
    g, _ = gw(["坏1", "坏2", "坏3"])
    with pytest.raises(LLMError) as ei:
        g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out)
    assert "3 次" in str(ei.value) and "坏3" in str(ei.value)


def test_manual_without_result_needs_input():
    g, _ = gw([], kind="manual")
    with pytest.raises(NeedsInput) as ei:
        g.generate("t", "sys", "user", Out, key="k1")
    r = ei.value.request
    assert r["key"] == "k1" and r["system"] == "sys" and r["user"] == "user"
    assert "properties" in r["schema"]


def test_manual_with_result_and_invalid_result():
    g, _ = gw([], kind="manual")
    assert g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out, manual={"k1": '{"n": 5}'}, key="k1").n == 5
    with pytest.raises(NeedsInput) as ei:
        g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out, manual={"k1": "乱写"}, key="k1")
    assert ei.value.request["error"]


def test_local_gpu_uses_guard_and_unloads():
    events = []

    @contextmanager
    def guard():
        events.append("enter")
        yield
        events.append("exit")

    def fake_run(cmd, **kw):
        events.append(("run", tuple(cmd)))
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, 0, "", "")

    cfg = LLMCfg(default="p", providers=[ProviderCfg(id="p", kind="openai", base_url="http://x/v1",
                                                     local_gpu=True,
                                                     unload_cmd=["lms", "unload", "--all"])])
    g = LLMGateway(cfg, {"p": FakeProvider(['{"n": 1}'])}, gpu_guard=guard, run=fake_run)
    g.generate("t", "s", "u", Out)
    assert events == ["enter", ("run", ("lms", "unload", "--all")), "exit"]


def test_openai_provider_request_shape():
    seen = {}

    def handler(req):
        seen["url"] = str(req.url)
        seen["body"] = json.loads(req.read())
        seen["auth"] = req.headers.get("authorization")
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"model": "m1", "usage": {"total_tokens": 9},
                                         "choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"n": 1}'}}]})

    cfg = ProviderCfg(id="ds", kind="openai", base_url="http://llm.test/v1", model="m1")
    res = OpenAIProvider(cfg, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)).complete(
        "SYS", [{"role": "user", "content": "U"}])
    assert seen["url"] == "http://llm.test/v1/chat/completions"
    assert seen["body"]["messages"][0] == {"role": "system", "content": "SYS"}
    assert seen["body"]["model"] == "m1" and seen["auth"] is None
    assert res.text == '{"n": 1}' and res.usage == {"total_tokens": 9}


def test_openai_provider_http_error():
    cfg = ProviderCfg(id="ds", kind="openai", base_url="http://llm.test/v1")
    p = OpenAIProvider(cfg, transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r: httpx.Response(500, text="炸了")))
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="500"):
        p.complete("s", [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}])


class FakeRun:
    def __init__(self, stdout, returncode=0, stderr=""):
        self.stdout, self.returncode, self.stderr = stdout, returncode, stderr

    def __call__(self, cmd, **kw):
        self.cmd, self.kw = cmd, kw
        self.system = Path(cmd[cmd.index("--system-prompt-file") + 1]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, self.returncode, self.stdout, self.stderr)


def claude(run, use_sdk=False):
    cfg = ProviderCfg(id="claude", kind="claude", use_sdk=use_sdk)
    return ClaudeProvider(cfg, run=run, which=lambda name: "C:/fake/claude.exe")


def test_claude_cli_path():
    run = FakeRun(json.dumps({"type": "result", "subtype": "success", "is_error": False,
                              "result": '{"n": 1}', "usage": {"output_tokens": 3}}))
    res = claude(run).complete("系统提示", [{"role": "user", "content": "用户消息"}])
    assert res.text == '{"n": 1}'
    assert run.cmd[:4] == ["C:/fake/claude.exe", "-p", "--output-format", "json"]
    assert run.kw["input"] == "用户消息" and run.system == "系统提示"


def test_claude_cli_errors():
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="报错"):
        claude(FakeRun(json.dumps({"type": "result", "is_error": True, "result": "额度用完"}))).complete(
            "s", [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}])
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="不是 JSON"):
        claude(FakeRun("oops")).complete("s", [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}])
    p = ClaudeProvider(ProviderCfg(id="c", kind="claude", use_sdk=False), which=lambda n: None)
    with pytest.raises(LLMError, match="找不到 claude"):
        p.complete("s", [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}])


def test_claude_sdk_failure_falls_back_to_cli():
    class Broken(ClaudeProvider):
        async def _sdk(self, system, prompt):
            raise RuntimeError("sdk 坏了")

    run = FakeRun(json.dumps({"type": "result", "subtype": "success", "result": "ok"}))
    p = Broken(ProviderCfg(id="c", kind="claude", use_sdk=True), run=run,
               which=lambda n: "C:/fake/claude.exe")
    assert p.complete("s", [{"role": "user", "content": "u"}]).text == "ok"


def test_build_providers_skips_manual():
    cfg = LLMCfg(providers=[ProviderCfg(id="a", kind="openai", base_url="http://x/v1"),
                            ProviderCfg(id="b", kind="claude"), ProviderCfg(id="m", kind="manual")])
    assert sorted(build_providers(cfg)) == ["a", "b"]
```
