- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 providers.py**

```python
"""LLM provider：OpenAI 兼容接口（LM Studio / DeepSeek / Agnes…）、本机 Claude Code。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

import httpx

from backend.config import LLMCfg, ProviderCfg
from backend.llm.base import LLMError, LLMProvider, LLMResult

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def flatten_messages(messages: list[dict]) -> str:
    if len(messages) == 1:
        return messages[0]["content"]
    label = {"user": "【用户】", "assistant": "【助手】"}
    return "\n\n".join(f"{label.get(m['role'], m['role'])}\n{m['content']}" for m in messages)


class OpenAIProvider:
    def __init__(self, cfg: ProviderCfg, *, transport: httpx.BaseTransport | None = None):
        if not cfg.base_url:
            raise ValueError(f"LLM provider {cfg.id} 缺少 base_url")
        self.id = cfg.id
        self.cfg = cfg
        headers = {}
        key = cfg.api_key()
        if key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {key}"
        self._http = httpx.Client(base_url=cfg.base_url.rstrip("/"), timeout=cfg.timeout,
                                  trust_env=False, headers=headers, transport=transport)

    def complete(self, system: str, messages: list[dict], *, json_mode: bool = True) -> LLMResult:
        body: dict = {"messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}, *messages],
                      "temperature": 0.7, "stream": False}
        if self.cfg.model:
            body["model"] = self.cfg.model
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        last: Exception | None = None
        for attempt in range(3):
            try:
                r = self._http.post("/chat/completions", json=body)
                break
            except httpx.TransportError as e:  # LM Studio 加载模型时会重置连接
                last = e
                time.sleep(2)
        else:
            raise LLMError(f"连不上 {self.id}（{self.cfg.base_url}）：{last}")
        if r.status_code >= 400:
            raise LLMError(f"{self.id} 返回 HTTP {r.status_code}：{r.text[:500]}")
        d = r.json()
        text = d["choices"][0]["message"].get("content") or ""
        return LLMResult(text=text, model=d.get("model"), usage=d.get("usage"),
                         seconds=time.monotonic() - t0)


class ClaudeProvider:
    """走本机 Claude Code 订阅，不需要 API key。"""

    def __init__(self, cfg: ProviderCfg, *, run: Callable = subprocess.run,
                 which: Callable[[str], str | None] = shutil.which):
        self.id = cfg.id
        self.cfg = cfg
        self._run = run
        self._which = which

    def complete(self, system: str, messages: list[dict], *, json_mode: bool = True) -> LLMResult:
        prompt = flatten_messages(messages)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        if self.cfg.use_sdk:
            try:
                text = asyncio.run(self._sdk(system, prompt))
                return LLMResult(text=text, model=self.cfg.model, seconds=time.monotonic() - t0)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Claude Agent SDK 调用失败，退回 claude -p：%s", e)
        return self._cli(system, prompt, t0)

    async def _sdk(self, system: str, prompt: str) -> str:
        from claude_agent_sdk import ClaudeAgentOptions, ResultMessage, query

        opts = ClaudeAgentOptions(system_prompt=system, max_turns=1, allowed_tools=[],
                                  model=self.cfg.model, cwd=tempfile.gettempdir())
        result = None
        async for msg in query(prompt=prompt, options=opts):
            if isinstance(msg, ResultMessage):
                if msg.is_error:
                    raise LLMError(f"Claude Agent SDK 报错：{msg.result}")
                result = msg.result
        if not result:
            raise LLMError("Claude Agent SDK 没有返回结果")
        return result

    def _cli(self, system: str, prompt: str, t0: float) -> LLMResult:
        exe = self._which("claude")
        if not exe:
            raise LLMError("找不到 claude 命令（Claude Code CLI），装好后再试，或换个 provider")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            sp = Path(tmp) / "system.md"
            sp.write_text(system, encoding="utf-8")
            cmd = [exe, "-p", "--output-format", "json", "--max-turns", "1",
                   "--system-prompt-file", str(sp)]
            if self.cfg.model:
                cmd += ["--model", self.cfg.model]
            if exe.lower().endswith((".cmd", ".bat")):
                cmd = ["cmd", "/c", *cmd]
            p = self._run(cmd, input=prompt, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
                          errors="replace", timeout=self.cfg.timeout, cwd=tmp)
        if p.returncode != 0 and not (p.stdout or "").strip():
            raise LLMError(f"claude -p 失败（退出码 {p.returncode}）：{(p.stderr or '')[-800:]}")
        try:
            d = json.loads(p.stdout)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            raise LLMError(f"claude -p 输出不是 JSON：{(p.stdout or '')[:500]}") from None
        if isinstance(d, list):
            d = next((x for x in reversed(d) if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("type") == "result"), {})
        if d.get("is_error") or d.get("subtype") not in (None, "success"):
            raise LLMError(f"claude -p 报错：{d.get('result') or d.get('subtype')}")
        return LLMResult(text=d.get("result") or "", model=self.cfg.model, usage=d.get("usage"),
                         seconds=time.monotonic() - t0)


def build_providers(cfg: LLMCfg) -> dict[str, LLMProvider]:
    out: dict[str, LLMProvider] = {}
    for p in cfg.providers:
        if p.kind == "openai":
            out[p.id] = OpenAIProvider(p)
        elif p.kind == "claude":
            out[p.id] = ClaudeProvider(p)
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 gateway.py**

```python
"""LLM 网关：按任务路由 provider，解析并校验 JSON，不合格就带着错误重试。

本机 GPU 上的 provider（LM Studio）在 gpu_guard 里调用：先等 GPU 任务让出显卡，
调用结束后卸载模型，再把显卡还给 ComfyUI。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import subprocess
from collections.abc import Callable
from contextlib import AbstractContextManager, nullcontext
from typing import Any, TypeVar

from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.config import LLMCfg, ProviderCfg
from backend.llm.base import LLMError, LLMProvider
from backend.llm.jsonutil import extract_json
from backend.queue.errors import NeedsInput

T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
MAX_RETRIES = 2
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _short(e: Exception, n: int = 800) -> str:
    s = str(e).strip()
    return s if len(s) <= n else s[:n] + "…"


def parse_output(text: str, schema: type[T], check: Callable[[T], None] | None = None) -> T:
    obj = schema.model_validate(extract_json(text))
    if check is not None:
        check(obj)
    return obj


class LLMGateway:
    def __init__(self, cfg: LLMCfg, providers: dict[str, LLMProvider], *,
                 gpu_guard: Callable[[], AbstractContextManager] | None = None,
                 run: Callable[..., Any] = subprocess.run):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.providers = providers
        self.gpu_guard = gpu_guard
        self._run = run

    def generate(self, task: str, system: str, user: str, schema: type[T], *,
                 check: Callable[[T], None] | None = None,
                 log: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
                 manual: dict | None = None, key: str | None = None) -> T:
        emit = log or (lambda m: None)
        key = key or task
        pcfg = self.cfg.provider_for(task)
        if pcfg.kind == "manual":
            return self._manual(task, key, system, user, schema, check, manual or {})
        provider = self.providers.get(pcfg.id)
        if provider is None:
            raise LLMError(f"LLM provider {pcfg.id} 没有初始化")
        guard = self.gpu_guard() if (pcfg.local_gpu and self.gpu_guard) else nullcontext()
        with guard:
            try:
                return self._loop(provider, pcfg, system, user, schema, check, emit)
            finally:
                if pcfg.local_gpu and pcfg.unload_cmd:
                    self._unload(pcfg, emit)

    def _loop(self, provider: LLMProvider, pcfg: ProviderCfg, system: str, user: str,
              schema: type[T], check, emit) -> T:
        messages = [{"role": "user", "content": user}]
        last_err, last_raw = "", ""
        for attempt in range(1 + MAX_RETRIES):
            res = provider.complete(system, messages, json_mode=True)
            emit(f"LLM {pcfg.id}（{res.model or pcfg.model or '-'}）第 {attempt + 1} 次，"
                 f"{res.seconds:.1f}s，usage={res.usage}")
            try:
                return parse_output(res.text, schema, check)
            except (LLMError, ValueError) as e:
                last_err, last_raw = _short(e), res.text
                emit(f"输出不合格：{last_err}")
                messages = messages + [
                    {"role": "assistant", "content": res.text[:8000]},
                    {"role": "user", "content": f"上面的输出不符合要求：{last_err}\n"
                                                "请修正后只输出一个符合格式的 JSON 对象，不要任何其他文字。"}]
        raise LLMError(f"{1 + MAX_RETRIES} 次输出都不合格：{last_err}\n"
                       f"最后一次原始输出：\n{last_raw[:3000]}")

    def _manual(self, task: str, key: str, system: str, user: str, schema: type[T], check,
                manual: dict) -> T:
        request = {"key": key, "task": task, "system": system, "user": user,
                   "schema": schema.model_json_schema()}
        text = manual.get(key)
        if text is None:
            raise NeedsInput(request)
        try:
            return parse_output(text, schema, check)
        except (LLMError, ValueError) as e:
            raise NeedsInput({**request, "error": _short(e)}) from None

    def _unload(self, pcfg: ProviderCfg, emit) -> None:
        try:
            p = self._run(pcfg.unload_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
                          errors="replace", timeout=60)
            emit(f"已卸载 {pcfg.id} 的模型（退出码 {p.returncode}）")
        except Exception as e:
            emit(f"卸载 {pcfg.id} 的模型失败：{e}")
```

```python
"""真实环境自检：uv run python -m backend.llm.selftest <provider_id> [--no-sdk]"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys

from pydantic import BaseModel

from backend.config import load_config
from backend.llm.gateway import LLMGateway
from backend.llm.providers import build_providers


class Pong(BaseModel):
    ok: bool
    word: str


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
    if not argv:
        print("用法：python -m backend.llm.selftest <provider_id> [--no-sdk]")
        return 2
    cfg = load_config()
    pcfg = cfg.llm.provider(argv[0])
    if "--no-sdk" in argv:
        pcfg.use_sdk = False
    cfg.llm.routes["selftest"] = pcfg.id
    gw = LLMGateway(cfg.llm, build_providers(cfg.llm))
    out = gw.generate("selftest", "你是接口自检程序，只输出 JSON。",
                      '输出 {"ok": true, "word": "你好"}，不要任何其他文字。', Pong, log=print)
    print(f"通过：{out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `Services(cfg, db, media, engines, llm, ffmpeg, comfy=None, comfy_client=None)`，方法 `prompts(slug) -> PromptLibrary`（项目目录 `<data>/<slug>/prompts/` 覆盖内置）
  - `backend.pipeline.common.load_episode(s, episode_id) -> (Episode, Project)`（找不到抛 `JobError`）
  - `backend.pipeline.common.continue_chain(ctx, episode_id, payload) -> list[int]`：payload 里有 `then` 时调用 `backend.pipeline.runs.submit_chain`（Task 15 实现）提交后续阶段
  - 任务类型 `script`（CPU）：payload `{episode_id, force?, manual?, then?, options?}`，返回 `{"structure": "done"|"skipped", "visual": "done"|"skipped"}`
    - 结构：LLM 路由 `script`，manual key `script_structure`；按名字新增/更新角色与场景，整体替换本集剧本段落（旧镜头的 `scene_block_id` 变为空），`stage_hashes["structure"]`，`status = "scripted"`
    - 视觉：LLM 路由 `script`，manual key `script_visual`；每个角色、场景都必须覆盖到，缺了就带错误重试；写入 `visual_prompt` / `core_features`，创建或更新项目的 StyleBible，`stage_hashes["visual"]`
  - 测试工具（`helpers.py`）：`FakeCtx(services, *, manager=None, job_id=1)`（`logs`、`progress_calls`、`cancel_event`）、`make_gateway(texts, kind="openai") -> (LLMGateway, FakeProvider)`、`make_services(tmp_path, db, *, llm=None, comfy=None, ffmpeg=None, cfg=None) -> Services`、`seed_episode(db, *, slug="demo", source=..., target=20.0, image_engine="zimage", video_engine="h3_video") -> (project_id, episode_id)`

在 `backend/tests/helpers.py` 末尾追加：

```python
import threading

from backend.config import Config, LLMCfg, ProviderCfg, ServerCfg
from backend.domain.media import MediaStore
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Project
from backend.engines.ffmpeg import FFmpeg
from backend.engines.registry import EngineRegistry
from backend.llm.gateway import LLMGateway
from backend.queue.errors import JobCanceled
from backend.services import Services


class FakeCtx:
    def __init__(self, services, *, manager=None, job_id: int = 1):
        self.services = services
        self.manager = manager
        self.job_id = job_id
        self.logs: list[str] = []
        self.progress_calls: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        self.cancel_event = threading.Event()

    def log(self, msg):
        self.logs.append(msg)

    def progress(self, value, maximum):
        self.progress_calls.append((value, maximum))

    def check_canceled(self):
        if self.cancel_event.is_set():
            raise JobCanceled()


def make_gateway(texts, kind: str = "openai"):
    prov = FakeProvider(texts)
    cfg = LLMCfg(default="fake", providers=[ProviderCfg(id="fake", kind=kind,
                                                        base_url="http://x/v1")])
    return LLMGateway(cfg, {"fake": prov}), prov


def make_services(tmp_path, db, *, llm=None, comfy=None, ffmpeg=None, cfg=None) -> Services:
    cfg = cfg or Config(server=ServerCfg(data_dir=tmp_path / "data"))
    return Services(cfg=cfg, db=db, media=MediaStore(cfg.data_dir), engines=EngineRegistry(cfg),
                    llm=llm, ffmpeg=ffmpeg or FFmpeg(), comfy=comfy)


def seed_episode(db, *, slug="demo", source="林夏在直播间发现提词器被人改了。", target=20.0,
                 image_engine="zimage", video_engine="h3_video"):
    with db.session() as s:
        p = Project(slug=slug, title="提词员", image_engine=image_engine,
                    video_engine=video_engine)
        s.add(p)
        s.flush()
        ep = Episode(project_id=p.id, number=1, source_text=source, target_seconds=target)
        s.add(ep)
        s.flush()
        return p.id, ep.id
```

`backend/tests/test_script.py`：

```python
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.models import Character, Episode, Location, Project, SceneBlock
from backend.pipeline.script import run_script
from backend.queue.errors import JobError, NeedsInput
from backend.tests.helpers import FakeCtx, make_gateway, make_services, seed_episode

STRUCTURE = {
    "title": "被改的提词器",
    "characters": [{"name": "林夏", "description": "新闻主播"}, {"name": "老周", "description": "导播"}],
    "locations": [{"name": "直播间", "time_of_day": "夜晚", "atmosphere": "压抑"},
                  {"name": "走廊", "time_of_day": "夜晚", "atmosphere": "冷清"}],
    "scenes": [{"location": "直播间", "text": "林夏照着提词器念，发现词被改了。"},
               {"location": "走廊", "text": "林夏追上老周质问。"}],
}
LOOK_LIN = ("a woman in her late twenties with an oval face and shoulder length black hair, "
            "slim build, charcoal wool blazer over a white silk blouse, small silver ear cuff")
LOOK_ZHOU = ("a man in his fifties with a square face, receding grey hair, heavy build, "
             "navy cardigan over a checked shirt, black headset around his neck")
VISUAL = {
    "style": {"anchor_text": "photorealistic live action short drama, shot on ARRI Alexa, "
                             "Kodak 2383 print look, cool teal shadows, warm skin tones",
              "palette": "青橙", "mood_keywords": ["tense", "night"], "negative": "cartoon"},
    "characters": [{"name": "林夏", "visual_prompt": LOOK_LIN, "core_features": "齐肩黑发、银耳骨夹"},
                   {"name": "老周", "visual_prompt": LOOK_ZHOU, "core_features": "灰发、耳麦"}],
    "locations": [{"name": "直播间", "visual_prompt": "a cramped live news studio with a ring light, "
                                                    "a teleprompter monitor and a glass desk"},
                  {"name": "走廊", "visual_prompt": "a narrow office corridor with flickering "
                                                   "fluorescent tubes and grey carpet"}],
}


def setup(tmp_path, db, texts, kind="openai", **seed):
    gw, prov = make_gateway(texts, kind=kind)
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, llm=gw)
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db, **seed)
    return FakeCtx(sv), ep_id, prov


def test_script_creates_blocks_characters_and_style(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [STRUCTURE, VISUAL])
    assert run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id}) == {"structure": "done", "visual": "done"}
    with db.session() as s:
        ep = s.get(Episode, ep_id)
        assert ep.title == "被改的提词器" and ep.status == "scripted"
        blocks = ep.scene_blocks
        assert [b.text for b in blocks] == [STRUCTURE["scenes"][0]["text"], STRUCTURE["scenes"][1]["text"]]
        loc_names = {l.id: l.name for l in s.scalars(select(Location))}
        assert [loc_names[b.location_id] for b in blocks] == ["直播间", "走廊"]
        lin = s.scalar(select(Character).where(Character.name == "林夏"))
        assert lin.visual_prompt == LOOK_LIN and lin.core_features == "齐肩黑发、银耳骨夹"
        proj = s.get(Project, ep.project_id)
        assert proj.style_bible.anchor_text.startswith("photorealistic")
        assert proj.style_bible.mood_keywords == ["tense", "night"]
        assert set(ep.stage_hashes) == {"structure", "visual"}
    assert "提词器" in prov.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"]


def test_script_skips_when_unchanged(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [STRUCTURE, VISUAL])
    run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    assert run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id}) == {"structure": "skipped", "visual": "skipped"}
    assert len(prov.calls) == 2
    assert any("跳过" in line for line in ctx.logs)


def test_force_reruns_and_replaces_blocks(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [STRUCTURE, VISUAL, STRUCTURE, VISUAL])
    run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id, "force": True})
    assert len(prov.calls) == 4
    with db.session() as s:
        assert len(s.scalars(select(SceneBlock)).all()) == 2
        assert len(s.scalars(select(Character)).all()) == 2


def test_visual_missing_character_retries(tmp_path, db):
    partial = {**VISUAL, "characters": VISUAL["characters"][:1]}
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [STRUCTURE, partial, VISUAL])
    run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    assert len(prov.calls) == 3
    assert "缺少角色：老周" in prov.calls[2]["messages"][2]["content"]


def test_source_text_required(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, _ = setup(tmp_path, db, [], source="  ")
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="原文"):
        run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})


def test_manual_provider_waits_for_input(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, _ = setup(tmp_path, db, [], kind="manual")
    with pytest.raises(NeedsInput) as ei:
        run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    assert ei.value.request["key"] == "script_structure"
    import json
    with pytest.raises(NeedsInput) as ei:
        run_script(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id,
                         "manual": {"script_structure": json.dumps(STRUCTURE, ensure_ascii=False)}})
    assert ei.value.request["key"] == "script_visual"
```

```python
"""服务容器：处理器和 API 通过它拿到数据库、媒体、引擎、LLM、ffmpeg、ComfyUI。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from backend.config import Config
from backend.domain.db import Database
from backend.domain.media import MediaStore
from backend.engines.ffmpeg import FFmpeg
from backend.engines.registry import EngineRegistry
from backend.llm.prompts import BUILTIN_DIR, PromptLibrary


@dataclass
class Services:
    cfg: Config
    db: Database
    media: MediaStore
    engines: EngineRegistry
    llm: Any                 # LLMGateway
    ffmpeg: FFmpeg
    comfy: Any = None        # ComfyRunner
    comfy_client: Any = None  # ComfyClient

    def prompts(self, slug: str) -> PromptLibrary:
        return PromptLibrary(BUILTIN_DIR, self.media.project_dir(slug) / "prompts")
```

`backend/pipeline/common.py`：

```python
"""各阶段共用的小工具。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.domain.models import Episode, Project
from backend.queue.errors import JobError


def load_episode(s: Session, episode_id: int) -> tuple[Episode, Project]:
    ep = s.get(Episode, episode_id)
    if ep is None:
        raise JobError(f"找不到剧集 {episode_id}")
    return ep, ep.project


def continue_chain(ctx, episode_id: int, payload: dict) -> list[int]:
    """payload 带 then（后续阶段）时，本阶段完成后接着提交。"""
    then = payload.get("then") or []
    if not then:
        return []
    from backend.pipeline.runs import RunOptions, submit_chain

    opts = RunOptions(**(payload.get("options") or {}))
    sv = ctx.services
    ids = submit_chain(ctx.manager, sv.db, sv.engines, episode_id, then, opts)
    ctx.log(f"已提交后续任务：{', '.join(map(str, ids))}")
    return ids
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 pipeline/script.py**

```python
"""剧本阶段：结构（段落、角色、场景）→ 视觉（风格锚、角色与场景外观）。

每一步记录输入指纹（渲染后提示词的哈希），输入没变就跳过，中途失败可以断点续跑。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.hashing import input_hash
from backend.domain.models import Character, Location, SceneBlock, StyleBible
from backend.llm.schemas import ScriptStructure, ScriptVisual
from backend.pipeline.common import continue_chain, load_episode
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS


@HANDLERS.register("script")
def run_script(ctx, payload: dict) -> dict:
    episode_id = int(payload["episode_id"])
    force = bool(payload.get("force"))
    manual = payload.get("manual") or {}
    structure = _structure(ctx, episode_id, force, manual)
    visual = _visual(ctx, episode_id, force, manual)
    continue_chain(ctx, episode_id, payload)
    return {"structure": structure, "visual": visual}


def _structure(ctx, episode_id: int, force: bool, manual: dict) -> str:
    sv = ctx.services
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        if not ep.source_text.strip():
            raise JobError("这一集还没有原文或梗概，先填写 source_text")
        system, user = sv.prompts(proj.slug).render(
            "script_structure", project_title=proj.title, target_seconds=ep.target_seconds,
            source_text=ep.source_text)
        fp = input_hash("structure", system, user)
        if not force and (ep.stage_hashes or {}).get("structure") == fp and ep.scene_blocks:
            ctx.log("剧本结构：输入没变，跳过")
            return "skipped"
    out = sv.llm.generate("script", system, user, ScriptStructure, log=ctx.log, manual=manual,
                          key="script_structure")
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        for c in out.characters:
            ch = s.scalar(select(Character).where(Character.project_id == proj.id,
                                                  Character.name == c.name))
            if ch is None:
                ch = Character(project_id=proj.id, name=c.name)
                s.add(ch)
            if c.description:
                ch.description = c.description
        locs: dict[str, Location] = {}
        for lo in out.locations:
            loc = s.scalar(select(Location).where(Location.project_id == proj.id,
                                                  Location.name == lo.name))
            if loc is None:
                loc = Location(project_id=proj.id, name=lo.name)
                s.add(loc)
            loc.time_of_day = lo.time_of_day or loc.time_of_day
            loc.atmosphere = lo.atmosphere or loc.atmosphere
            locs[lo.name] = loc
        s.flush()
        ep.scene_blocks.clear()  # 旧段落删掉；镜头的 scene_block_id 由外键置空
        s.flush()
        for i, sc in enumerate(out.scenes):
            ep.scene_blocks.append(SceneBlock(order=i, location_id=locs[sc.location].id,
                                              text=sc.text))
        if not ep.title:
            ep.title = out.title
        ep.stage_hashes = {**(ep.stage_hashes or {}), "structure": fp}
        ep.status = "scripted"
    ctx.log(f"剧本结构：{len(out.scenes)} 段，角色 {len(out.characters)} 个，"
            f"场景 {len(out.locations)} 个")
    return "done"


def _visual(ctx, episode_id: int, force: bool, manual: dict) -> str:
    sv = ctx.services
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        chars = [{"name": c.name, "description": c.description} for c in proj.characters]
        locs = [{"name": l.name, "time_of_day": l.time_of_day, "atmosphere": l.atmosphere}
                for l in proj.locations]
        system, user = sv.prompts(proj.slug).render(
            "script_visual", project_title=proj.title, characters=chars, locations=locs,
            excerpt=ep.source_text[:4000])
        fp = input_hash("visual", system, user)
        if not force and (ep.stage_hashes or {}).get("visual") == fp and proj.style_bible_id:
            ctx.log("视觉设定：输入没变，跳过")
            return "skipped"
    char_names = {c["name"] for c in chars}
    loc_names = {l["name"] for l in locs}

    def check(out: ScriptVisual) -> None:
        missing_c = char_names - {c.name for c in out.characters}
        missing_l = loc_names - {l.name for l in out.locations}
        if missing_c:
            raise ValueError(f"缺少角色：{'、'.join(sorted(missing_c))}")
        if missing_l:
            raise ValueError(f"缺少场景：{'、'.join(sorted(missing_l))}")

    out = sv.llm.generate("script", system, user, ScriptVisual, check=check, log=ctx.log,
                          manual=manual, key="script_visual")
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        by_char = {c.name: c for c in proj.characters}
        for cv in out.characters:
            if cv.name in by_char:
                by_char[cv.name].visual_prompt = cv.visual_prompt
                by_char[cv.name].core_features = cv.core_features
        by_loc = {l.name: l for l in proj.locations}
        for lv in out.locations:
            if lv.name in by_loc:
                by_loc[lv.name].visual_prompt = lv.visual_prompt
        sb = proj.style_bible
        if sb is None:
            sb = StyleBible()
            s.add(sb)
            proj.style_bible = sb
        st = out.style
        sb.anchor_text, sb.palette, sb.lighting = st.anchor_text, st.palette, st.lighting
        sb.texture, sb.character_rules = st.texture, st.character_rules
        sb.mood_keywords, sb.negative = list(st.mood_keywords), st.negative
        ep.stage_hashes = {**(ep.stage_hashes or {}), "visual": fp}
    ctx.log(f"视觉设定：风格锚 {len(out.style.anchor_text.split())} 词，"
            f"角色 {len(out.characters)}，场景 {len(out.locations)}")
    return "done"
```

### Task 13: 分镜阶段（镜头预算、按段落生成、数量与角色校验）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/pipeline/storyboard.py`
- Modify: `backend/pipeline/__init__.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_storyboard.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 `renumber_shots`、`input_hash`；Task 8 `EngineRegistry.video(...).caps`；Task 10 `StoryboardOut`；Task 12 `load_episode`、`continue_chain`、测试工具
- Produces:
  - `shot_budget(target_seconds, shot_seconds) -> int`（至少 1）
  - `allocate_shots(total, weights) -> list[int]`（每段至少 1；按权重最大余数分配；段数多于总数时每段 1）
  - 任务类型 `storyboard`（CPU）：payload `{episode_id, force?, scene_block_ids?, manual?, then?, options?}`，返回 `{"blocks_done", "blocks_skipped"}`
    - 单镜时长 = 项目视频引擎的 `caps.default_seconds`；镜头总数 = `shot_budget(target_seconds, 单镜时长)`，按段落字数分配
    - 每段一次 LLM 调用（路由 `storyboard`，manual key `storyboard:<block_id>`），`check` 要求镜头数正好等于分配数、cast 只用已知角色名；不合格带错误重试
    - LLM 成功后才删除该段旧镜头并写入新镜头（失败不动数据库）；duration 截到引擎 `max_seconds`；cast 存 `[{character_id, variation_id: None}]`
    - 有段落重新生成时，按段落顺序重排全集镜头并重新编号 S01…；重跑整集（未指定 scene_block_ids）会删除不属于任何段落的旧镜头
    - `stage_hashes["storyboard:<block_id>"]`；`status = "storyboarded"`

```python
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.models import Character, Episode, Location, Project, SceneBlock, Shot, StyleBible
from backend.llm.base import LLMError
from backend.pipeline.storyboard import allocate_shots, run_storyboard, shot_budget
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.tests.helpers import FakeCtx, make_gateway, make_services, seed_episode

LOOK = ("a woman in her late twenties with an oval face and shoulder length black hair, "
        "slim build, charcoal wool blazer over a white silk blouse, small silver ear cuff")


def shots_json(n, cast=("林夏",), duration=5):
    img = "medium close-up, 85mm, eye level. " + LOOK + " " + " ".join(["detail"] * 50)
    return {"shots": [{
        "shot_size": "近景", "camera_move": "static", "action": f"动作{i}", "cast": list(cast),
        "dialogue": ([{"speaker": "林夏", "zh": "词被改了", "start": 0.5, "end": 2.0}] if i == 0 else []),
        "image_prompt": img, "motion_prompt": "she looks up slowly", "audio_prompt": "quiet hum",
        "duration": duration, "transition_out": "hard_cut"} for i in range(n)]}


def seed_scripted(db, **kw):
    proj_id, ep_id = seed_episode(db, target=20.0, **kw)
    with db.session() as s:
        proj = s.get(Project, proj_id)
        proj.style_bible = StyleBible(anchor_text="ANCHOR photorealistic drama")
        s.add(Character(project_id=proj_id, name="林夏", visual_prompt=LOOK))
        loc = Location(project_id=proj_id, name="直播间", time_of_day="夜晚", visual_prompt="ROOM")
        s.add(loc)
        s.flush()
        ep = s.get(Episode, ep_id)
        ep.scene_blocks.append(SceneBlock(order=0, location_id=loc.id, text="甲" * 50))
        ep.scene_blocks.append(SceneBlock(order=1, location_id=loc.id, text="乙" * 50))
    return ep_id


def setup(tmp_path, db, texts, **kw):
    gw, prov = make_gateway(texts)
    ctx = FakeCtx(make_services(tmp_path, db, llm=gw))
    return ctx, seed_scripted(db, **kw), prov


def test_budget_and_allocation():
    assert shot_budget(20, 5) == 4 and shot_budget(2, 5) == 1
    assert allocate_shots(6, [1, 1, 1]) == [2, 2, 2]
    assert allocate_shots(5, [3, 1]) == [3, 2]
    assert allocate_shots(2, [1, 1, 1]) == [1, 1, 1]
    assert sum(allocate_shots(24, [120, 30, 300, 55])) == 24


def test_storyboard_creates_numbered_shots(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2), shots_json(2)])
    assert run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id}) == {"blocks_done": 2, "blocks_skipped": 0}
    with db.session() as s:
        shots = s.get(Episode, ep_id).shots
        assert [x.code for x in shots] == ["S01", "S02", "S03", "S04"]
        lin_id = s.scalar(select(Character.id))
        assert shots[0].cast == [{"character_id": lin_id, "variation_id": None}]
        assert shots[0].dialogue[0]["zh"] == "词被改了"
        blocks = s.get(Episode, ep_id).scene_blocks
        assert [x.scene_block_id for x in shots] == [blocks[0].id] * 2 + [blocks[1].id] * 2
        assert s.get(Episode, ep_id).status == "storyboarded"
    user = prov.calls[0]["messages"][0]["content"]
    assert "正好 2 个镜头" in user and LOOK in user and "ROOM" in user and "ANCHOR" in user
    assert "上一镜结束时：动作1" in prov.calls[1]["messages"][0]["content"]


def test_skip_unchanged(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2), shots_json(2)])
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    assert run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id}) == {"blocks_done": 0, "blocks_skipped": 2}
    assert len(prov.calls) == 2


def test_wrong_count_fails_and_keeps_db_clean(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2), shots_json(2),
                                            shots_json(3), shots_json(1), shots_json(3)])
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    with db.session() as s:
        before = [x.id for x in s.get(Episode, ep_id).shots]
    with pytest.raises(LLMError) as ei:
        run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id, "force": True})
    assert "需要正好 2 个镜头" in str(ei.value) and "动作2" in str(ei.value)
    with db.session() as s:
        assert [x.id for x in s.get(Episode, ep_id).shots] == before


def test_unknown_cast_retries(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2, cast=("路人甲",)), shots_json(2),
                                            shots_json(2)])
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    assert "未知角色" in prov.calls[1]["messages"][2]["content"]


def test_duration_clamped_to_engine_max(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, _ = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2, duration=8), shots_json(2, duration=8)],
                          video_engine="wan22_i2v")
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    with db.session() as s:
        assert {x.duration for x in s.get(Episode, ep_id).shots} == {5.0}


def test_regenerate_single_block_keeps_others(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, ep_id, prov = setup(tmp_path, db, [shots_json(2), shots_json(2), shots_json(2)])
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
    with db.session() as s:
        ep = s.get(Episode, ep_id)
        b2 = ep.scene_blocks[1].id
        first_two = [x.id for x in ep.shots[:2]]
    run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id, "scene_block_ids": [b2], "force": True})
    with db.session() as s:
        shots = s.get(Episode, ep_id).shots
        assert [x.id for x in shots[:2]] == first_two
        assert [x.code for x in shots] == ["S01", "S02", "S03", "S04"]
    assert len(prov.calls) == 3


def test_no_blocks_raises(tmp_path, db):
    gw, _ = make_gateway([])
    ctx = FakeCtx(make_services(tmp_path, db, llm=gw))
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db)
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="script"):
        run_storyboard(ctx, {"episode_id": ep_id})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 storyboard.py**

```python
"""分镜阶段：按目标时长和引擎甜区算镜头预算，按剧本段落分配，逐段让 LLM 出镜头。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select

from backend.domain.hashing import input_hash
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots
from backend.llm.schemas import StoryboardOut
from backend.pipeline.common import continue_chain, load_episode
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS


def shot_budget(target_seconds: float, shot_seconds: float) -> int:
    return max(1, round(target_seconds / max(shot_seconds, 0.1)))


def allocate_shots(total: int, weights: list[float]) -> list[int]:
    n = len(weights)
    if n == 0:
        return []
    remaining = max(total, n) - n  # 每段先保底 1 个
    w = [max(float(x), 0.0) for x in weights]
    wsum = sum(w)
    shares = [remaining * x / wsum for x in w] if wsum > 0 else [remaining / n] * n
    floors = [int(x) for x in shares]
    rest = remaining - sum(floors)
    order = sorted(range(n), key=lambda i: (-(shares[i] - floors[i]), i))
    for i in order[:rest]:
        floors[i] += 1
    return [1 + f for f in floors]


def _last_action(db, block_id: int) -> str:
    with db.session() as s:
        sh = s.scalar(select(Shot).where(Shot.scene_block_id == block_id)
                      .order_by(Shot.order.desc(), Shot.id.desc()))
        return sh.action if sh else ""


@HANDLERS.register("storyboard")
def run_storyboard(ctx, payload: dict) -> dict:
    sv = ctx.services
    episode_id = int(payload["episode_id"])
    force = bool(payload.get("force"))
    manual = payload.get("manual") or {}
    only = set(payload.get("scene_block_ids") or [])
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        blocks = list(ep.scene_blocks)
        if not blocks:
            raise JobError("这一集还没有剧本段落，先跑 script")
        try:
            caps = sv.engines.video(proj.video_engine).caps
        except KeyError as e:
            raise JobError(str(e)) from None
        shot_seconds, max_seconds = caps.default_seconds, caps.max_seconds
        counts = allocate_shots(shot_budget(ep.target_seconds, shot_seconds),
                                [len(b.text) for b in blocks])
        anchor = proj.style_bible.anchor_text if proj.style_bible else ""
        characters = [{"name": c.name, "visual_prompt": c.visual_prompt or c.description}
                      for c in proj.characters]
        char_ids = {c.name: c.id for c in proj.characters}
        locs = {l.id: {"name": l.name, "time_of_day": l.time_of_day, "atmosphere": l.atmosphere,
                       "visual_prompt": l.visual_prompt} for l in proj.locations}
        plan = [{"id": b.id, "order": b.order, "text": b.text, "location": locs.get(b.location_id),
                 "count": n} for b, n in zip(blocks, counts)]
        prompts = sv.prompts(proj.slug)

    done = skipped = 0
    prev_action = ""
    for blk in plan:
        if only and blk["id"] not in only:
            prev_action = _last_action(sv.db, blk["id"]) or prev_action
            continue
        ctx.check_canceled()
        n = blk["count"]
        system, user = prompts.render(
            "storyboard", anchor=anchor, characters=characters, location=blk["location"],
            text=blk["text"], count=n, shot_seconds=shot_seconds, max_seconds=max_seconds,
            prev_action=prev_action)
        fp = input_hash("storyboard", system, user)
        key = f"storyboard:{blk['id']}"
        with sv.db.session() as s:
            ep = s.get(Episode, episode_id)
            existing = s.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Shot)
                                .where(Shot.scene_block_id == blk["id"]))
            unchanged = (ep.stage_hashes or {}).get(key) == fp and existing > 0
        if unchanged and not force:
            ctx.log(f"段落 {blk['order'] + 1}：输入没变，跳过")
            skipped += 1
            prev_action = _last_action(sv.db, blk["id"]) or prev_action
            continue

        def check(out: StoryboardOut, n: int = n) -> None:
            if len(out.shots) != n:
                raise ValueError(f"需要正好 {n} 个镜头，实际给了 {len(out.shots)} 个")
            unknown = {c for sh in out.shots for c in sh.cast} - set(char_ids)
            if unknown:
                raise ValueError(f"cast 里有未知角色 {sorted(unknown)}，只能用：{sorted(char_ids)}")

        out = sv.llm.generate("storyboard", system, user, StoryboardOut, check=check,
                              log=ctx.log, manual=manual, key=key)
        with sv.db.session() as s:
            s.execute(delete(Shot).where(Shot.scene_block_id == blk["id"]))
            for j, sh in enumerate(out.shots):
                s.add(Shot(
                    episode_id=episode_id, scene_block_id=blk["id"], order=blk["order"] * 1000 + j,
                    shot_size=sh.shot_size, camera_move=sh.camera_move, action=sh.action,
                    dialogue=[d.model_dump() for d in sh.dialogue],
                    cast=[{"character_id": char_ids[c], "variation_id": None} for c in sh.cast],
                    image_prompt=sh.image_prompt, motion_prompt=sh.motion_prompt,
                    audio_prompt=sh.audio_prompt, duration=min(sh.duration, max_seconds),
                    transition_out=sh.transition_out))
            ep = s.get(Episode, episode_id)
            ep.stage_hashes = {**(ep.stage_hashes or {}), key: fp}
        done += 1
        prev_action = out.shots[-1].action
        ctx.log(f"段落 {blk['order'] + 1}：{n} 个镜头")

    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep = s.get(Episode, episode_id)
        if done:
            if not only:
                s.execute(delete(Shot).where(Shot.episode_id == episode_id,
                                             Shot.scene_block_id.is_(None)))
            block_order = {b.id: b.order for b in ep.scene_blocks}
            shots = list(s.scalars(select(Shot).where(Shot.episode_id == episode_id)))
            shots.sort(key=lambda x: (block_order.get(x.scene_block_id, 10**6), x.order, x.id))
            renumber_shots(s, episode_id, [x.id for x in shots])
        ep.status = "storyboarded"
    continue_chain(ctx, episode_id, payload)
    return {"blocks_done": done, "blocks_skipped": skipped}
```

### Task 14: 首帧与视频阶段（提示词拼装、输入哈希、候选自动选定）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/pipeline/prompting.py`, `backend/pipeline/keyframes.py`, `backend/pipeline/videos.py`
- Modify: `backend/pipeline/__init__.py`, `backend/tests/helpers.py`（追加 `FakeRunner`）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_keyframes_videos.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 槽位函数、`input_hash`、`MediaStore`；Task 7 `ImageRequest`、`VideoRequest`；Task 8 引擎；Task 12 `Services`、`load_episode`、测试工具
- Produces（`prompting.py`，API 的过期判定也用它）：
  - `compose_image_prompt(image_prompt, anchor) -> str`：风格锚放最前，已包含就不重复
  - `compose_video_prompt(motion_prompt, image_prompt, audio_prompt, dialogue, *, anchor, has_audio) -> str`：motion 为空时用 image_prompt；有声引擎追加 `\nAudio: ...`，台词不在 audio_prompt 里时补 `<speaker> says in Mandarin Chinese: "<zh>"`
  - `join_negative(*parts) -> str`
  - `shot_seed(base, shot_id, n) -> int`（n 为该槽位已有候选数，保证每次重跑换种子）
  - `keyframe_input_hash(shot, project, style, engine_name) -> str`
  - `video_input_hash(shot, project, style, first_frame_candidate_id, engine_name, has_audio) -> str`
- Produces（任务类型，均为 GPU）：
  - `image.keyframe`：payload `{shot_id}`；用 `shot.image_engine or project.image_engine`；产物复制到 `media/first_frame/`，进 `("shot", id, "first_frame")` 槽位；返回 `{"candidate_ids": [id], "file": rel}`
  - `video.shot`：payload `{shot_id}`；需要已选定首帧（否则 `JobError`，信息含"还没有选定首帧"）；用 `shot.video_engine or project.video_engine`；产物进 `media/video/` 和 `("shot", id, "video")` 槽位
  - 两者都把 `run_dir/workflow.api.json` 的相对路径记进候选 `params["workflow"]`，并通过 `ctx.progress` 报进度
- 测试工具：`FakeRunner(fail=None)`（记录 `calls: list[EngineCall]`，按 `output_kind` 写出 `.png` / `.mp4`）

M1 不做参考图打包、尾帧和续接（属于 M2）：`refs` 为空，`use_end_frame`、`continue_from_prev` 暂不生效。

- [ ] **Step 1: 补测试工具**

在 `backend/tests/helpers.py` 末尾追加：

```python
class FakeRunner:
    """代替 ComfyRunner：不连 ComfyUI，直接写一个产物文件。"""

    def __init__(self, fail: Exception | None = None):
        self.calls = []
        self.fail = fail

    def run(self, call, *, run_dir, cancel, on_progress=None, timeout=3600.0):
        self.calls.append(call)
        if self.fail:
            raise self.fail
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (run_dir / "workflow.api.json").write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
        ext = ".png" if call.output_kind == "image" else ".mp4"
        f = run_dir / f"out_{len(self.calls)}{ext}"
        f.write_bytes(b"x")
        if on_progress:
            on_progress(1, 1)
        return [f]
```

`backend/tests/test_keyframes_videos.py`：

```python
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.domain.models import Episode, Project, Shot, StyleBible
from backend.domain.slots import selected_candidate, slots_for
from backend.engines.base import upload_ref
from backend.pipeline.keyframes import run_keyframe
from backend.pipeline.prompting import (compose_image_prompt, compose_video_prompt, join_negative,
                                        shot_seed)
from backend.pipeline.videos import run_video
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.tests.helpers import FakeCtx, FakeRunner, make_services, seed_episode

IMG = "medium close-up, 85mm. a woman reads a teleprompter in a dark studio"


def test_prompt_helpers():
    assert compose_image_prompt("shot", "ANCHOR") == "ANCHOR shot"
    assert compose_image_prompt("ANCHOR shot", "ANCHOR") == "ANCHOR shot"
    assert compose_image_prompt("shot", "") == "shot"
    dlg = [{"speaker": "林夏", "zh": "词被改了", "start": 0.5, "end": 2}]
    v = compose_video_prompt("she looks up", "img", "quiet hum", dlg, anchor="A", has_audio=True)
    assert v == 'A she looks up\nAudio: quiet hum 林夏 says in Mandarin Chinese: "词被改了"'
    assert compose_video_prompt("", "img", "hum", dlg, anchor="", has_audio=False) == "img"
    assert join_negative(" a ", "", None, "b") == "a, b"
    assert len({shot_seed(12345, 3, n) for n in range(4)}) == 4


def setup(tmp_path, db, video_engine="h3_video"):
    runner = FakeRunner()
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, comfy=runner)
    proj_id, ep_id = seed_episode(db, video_engine=video_engine)
    with db.session() as s:
        proj = s.get(Project, proj_id)
        proj.style_bible = StyleBible(anchor_text="ANCHOR", negative="cartoon")
        proj.negative = "blurry"
        sh = Shot(episode_id=ep_id, order=0, code="S01", image_prompt=IMG,
                  motion_prompt="she looks up slowly", audio_prompt="quiet hum",
                  dialogue=[{"speaker": "林夏", "zh": "词被改了", "start": 0.5, "end": 2.0}])
        s.add(sh)
        s.flush()
        shot_id = sh.id
    return FakeCtx(sv, job_id=11), runner, shot_id


def test_keyframe_creates_selected_candidate(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, runner, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db)
    res = run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    call = runner.calls[0]
    assert call.workflow["5"]["inputs"]["text"] == "ANCHOR " + IMG
    assert call.workflow["6"]["inputs"]["text"] == "blurry, cartoon"
    assert call.workflow["10"]["inputs"]["filename_prefix"] == "studio/demo/kf_S01"
    with db.session() as s:
        c = selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame")
        assert c.id == res["candidate_ids"][0] and c.engine == "zimage" and c.job_id == 11
        assert c.file_path.startswith("demo/media/first_frame/")
        assert c.params["workflow"] == "demo/runs/11/workflow.api.json"
        assert ctx.services.media.abs(c.file_path).exists()
    assert ctx.progress_calls == [(1, 1)]


def test_second_keyframe_keeps_selection_and_changes_seed(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, runner, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db)
    first = run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})["candidate_ids"][0]
    run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    assert runner.calls[0].meta["seed"] != runner.calls[1].meta["seed"]
    with db.session() as s:
        assert selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame").id == first
        assert len(slots_for(s, "shot", shot_id)[0].candidates) == 2


def test_rerun_after_prompt_change_selects_new(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, runner, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db)
    run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    with db.session() as s:
        s.get(Shot, shot_id).image_prompt = IMG + ", rain on the window"
    new = run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})["candidate_ids"][0]
    with db.session() as s:
        assert selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame").id == new


def test_video_requires_first_frame(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, _, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db)
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="还没有选定首帧"):
        run_video(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})


def test_h3_video_prompt_has_audio_and_uses_selected_frame(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, runner, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db)
    run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    res = run_video(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    call = runner.calls[-1]
    prompt = call.workflow["6"]["inputs"]["prompt"]
    assert prompt.startswith("ANCHOR she looks up slowly\nAudio: quiet hum")
    assert "词被改了" in prompt
    with db.session() as s:
        ff = selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame")
        assert call.workflow["5"]["inputs"]["image"] == upload_ref(ctx.services.media.abs(ff.file_path))
        v = selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot_id, "video")
        assert v.id == res["candidate_ids"][0] and v.file_path.endswith(".mp4")


def test_wan_video_has_no_audio_section(tmp_path, db):
    ctx, runner, shot_id = setup(tmp_path, db, video_engine="wan22_i2v")
    run_keyframe(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    run_video(ctx, {"shot_id": shot_id})
    assert "Audio:" not in runner.calls[-1].workflow["9"]["inputs"]["text"]
    assert runner.calls[-1].model_family == "wan22"


def test_missing_comfy_raises(tmp_path, db):
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, comfy=None)
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db)
    with db.session() as s:
        sh = Shot(episode_id=ep_id, order=0, code="S01", image_prompt=IMG)
        s.add(sh)
        s.flush()
        sid = sh.id
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="ComfyUI"):
        run_keyframe(FakeCtx(sv), {"shot_id": sid})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 prompting.py**

```python
"""把镜头字段拼成最终提示词，并算输入哈希（用于判断候选是否过期）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from backend.domain.hashing import input_hash


def compose_image_prompt(image_prompt: str, anchor: str) -> str:
    p, a = (image_prompt or "").strip(), (anchor or "").strip()
    return p if not a or a in p else f"{a} {p}"


def compose_video_prompt(motion_prompt: str, image_prompt: str, audio_prompt: str,
                         dialogue: list[dict], *, anchor: str, has_audio: bool) -> str:
    text = compose_image_prompt((motion_prompt or "").strip() or image_prompt, anchor)
    if not has_audio:
        return text
    audio = (audio_prompt or "").strip()
    lines = [f'{d.get("speaker", "")} says in Mandarin Chinese: "{d["zh"]}"'
             for d in dialogue or [] if d.get("zh") and d["zh"] not in audio]
    parts = [p for p in (audio, " ".join(lines)) if p]
    return text + ("\nAudio: " + " ".join(parts) if parts else "")


def join_negative(*parts: str | None) -> str:
    return ", ".join(p.strip() for p in parts if p and p.strip())


def shot_seed(base: int, shot_id: int, n: int) -> int:
    return (int(base) + shot_id * 1009 + n * 7919) % 2**32


def _anchor(style) -> str:
    return style.anchor_text if style else ""


def _negative(project, style) -> str:
    return join_negative(project.negative, style.negative if style else "")


def keyframe_input_hash(shot, project, style, engine_name: str) -> str:
    return input_hash("first_frame", compose_image_prompt(shot.image_prompt, _anchor(style)),
                      _negative(project, style), engine_name, project.aspect)


def video_input_hash(shot, project, style, first_frame_candidate_id: int, engine_name: str,
                     has_audio: bool) -> str:
    prompt = compose_video_prompt(shot.motion_prompt, shot.image_prompt, shot.audio_prompt,
                                  shot.dialogue, anchor=_anchor(style), has_audio=has_audio)
    return input_hash("video", prompt, first_frame_candidate_id, engine_name, project.aspect,
                      round(float(shot.duration), 2))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 keyframes.py**

```python
"""首帧阶段：每个任务给一个镜头出一张首帧候选。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil

from backend.domain.models import Shot
from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot
from backend.engines.base import ImageRequest
from backend.pipeline.common import load_episode
from backend.pipeline.prompting import (compose_image_prompt, join_negative, keyframe_input_hash,
                                        shot_seed)
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS


def load_shot(s, shot_id: int):
    shot = s.get(Shot, shot_id)
    if shot is None:
        raise JobError(f"找不到镜头 {shot_id}")
    ep, proj = load_episode(s, shot.episode_id)
    return shot, ep, proj, proj.style_bible


def run_comfy(ctx, call, slug: str):
    sv = ctx.services
    if sv.comfy is None:
        raise JobError("ComfyUI 未配置")
    for ref in call.dropped_refs:
        ctx.log(f"超出引擎参考图上限，已丢弃：{ref}")
    run_dir = sv.media.run_dir(slug, ctx.job_id)
    files = sv.comfy.run(call, run_dir=run_dir, cancel=ctx.cancel_event, on_progress=ctx.progress)
    return files, run_dir


@HANDLERS.register("image.keyframe", gpu=True)
def run_keyframe(ctx, payload: dict) -> dict:
    sv = ctx.services
    shot_id = int(payload["shot_id"])
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        shot, ep, proj, style = load_shot(s, shot_id)
        engine_name = shot.image_engine or proj.image_engine
        try:
            engine = sv.engines.image(engine_name)
        except KeyError as e:
            raise JobError(str(e)) from None
        anchor = style.anchor_text if style else ""
        prompt = compose_image_prompt(shot.image_prompt, anchor)
        negative = join_negative(proj.negative, style.negative if style else "")
        h = keyframe_input_hash(shot, proj, style, engine_name)
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot.id, "first_frame")
        seed = shot_seed(proj.seed, shot.id, len(slot.candidates))
        slug, code, aspect = proj.slug, shot.code, proj.aspect
    if not shot.image_prompt.strip():
        raise JobError(f"镜头 {code} 没有 image_prompt")
    call = engine.build(ImageRequest(prompt=prompt, negative=negative, aspect=aspect, seed=seed,
                                     prefix=f"studio/{slug}/kf_{code}"))
    ctx.log(f"首帧 {code} · {engine_name} · seed {seed}")
    files, run_dir = run_comfy(ctx, call, slug)
    dst = sv.media.new_media_path(slug, "first_frame", files[0].suffix)
    shutil.copyfile(files[0], dst)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame")
        c = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path=sv.media.rel(dst), engine=engine_name,
                          params={**call.meta, "workflow": sv.media.rel(run_dir / "workflow.api.json")},
                          input_hash=h, job_id=ctx.job_id)
        cid = c.id
    return {"candidate_ids": [cid], "file": sv.media.rel(dst)}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 实现 videos.py**

```python
"""视频阶段：用选定的首帧逐镜出视频。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil

from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot, selected_candidate
from backend.engines.base import VideoRequest
from backend.pipeline.keyframes import load_shot, run_comfy
from backend.pipeline.prompting import (compose_video_prompt, join_negative, shot_seed,
                                        video_input_hash)
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS

VIDEO_EXTS = (".mp4", ".webm", ".mov", ".mkv")


@HANDLERS.register("video.shot", gpu=True)
def run_video(ctx, payload: dict) -> dict:
    sv = ctx.services
    shot_id = int(payload["shot_id"])
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        shot, ep, proj, style = load_shot(s, shot_id)
        ff = selected_candidate(s, "shot", shot.id, "first_frame")
        if ff is None:
            raise JobError(f"镜头 {shot.code} 还没有选定首帧，先跑 keyframes")
        engine_name = shot.video_engine or proj.video_engine
        try:
            engine = sv.engines.video(engine_name)
        except KeyError as e:
            raise JobError(str(e)) from None
        has_audio = engine.caps.has_audio
        prompt = compose_video_prompt(shot.motion_prompt, shot.image_prompt, shot.audio_prompt,
                                      shot.dialogue, anchor=style.anchor_text if style else "",
                                      has_audio=has_audio)
        negative = join_negative(proj.negative, style.negative if style else "")
        h = video_input_hash(shot, proj, style, ff.id, engine_name, has_audio)
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot.id, "video")
        seed = shot_seed(proj.seed, shot.id, len(slot.candidates))
        seconds = max(1.0, min(float(shot.duration), engine.caps.max_seconds))
        first = sv.media.abs(ff.file_path)
        slug, code, aspect = proj.slug, shot.code, proj.aspect
    call = engine.build(VideoRequest(prompt=prompt, first_frame=first, aspect=aspect, seed=seed,
                                     seconds=seconds, negative=negative,
                                     prefix=f"studio/{slug}/clip_{code}"))
    ctx.log(f"视频 {code} · {engine_name} · {seconds:.1f}s · seed {seed}"
            f"{'（原生有声）' if has_audio else '（无声）'}")
    files, run_dir = run_comfy(ctx, call, slug)
    video = next((f for f in files if f.suffix.lower() in VIDEO_EXTS), None)
    if video is None:
        raise JobError(f"镜头 {code} 没有产出视频文件：{[f.name for f in files]}")
    dst = sv.media.new_media_path(slug, "video", video.suffix)
    shutil.copyfile(video, dst)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot_id, "video")
        c = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path=sv.media.rel(dst), engine=engine_name,
                          params={**call.meta, "first_frame_candidate_id": ff.id,
                                  "workflow": sv.media.rel(run_dir / "workflow.api.json")},
                          input_hash=h, job_id=ctx.job_id)
        cid = c.id
    return {"candidate_ids": [cid], "file": sv.media.rel(dst)}
```

### Task 15: 合成导出与阶段编排（submit_chain）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/pipeline/export.py`, `backend/pipeline/runs.py`
- Modify: `backend/pipeline/__init__.py`, `backend/tests/helpers.py`（追加 `FakeFFmpeg`）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_export_runs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 `JobManager`；Task 9 `FFmpeg.assemble` 签名；Task 12–14 全部
- Produces:
  - 任务类型 `export.episode`（CPU）：payload `{episode_id, allow_missing?}`；按镜头顺序取选定视频，缺了就报 `JobError`（列出镜头编号），`allow_missing` 时跳过；9:16 → 1080×1920，16:9 → 1920×1080，24fps；输出 `<data>/<slug>/exports/ep01_9x16_<时间>.mp4`，写 `Export` 行，`status = "exported"`；返回 `{"export_id", "file"}`
  - `OUTPUT_SIZES = {"9:16": (1080, 1920), "16:9": (1920, 1080)}`
  - `STAGES = ("script", "storyboard", "keyframes", "videos", "export")`
  - `RunOptions(shots: list[str] | None = None, candidates: int = 1, force: bool = False, priority: int = 0)`
  - `submit_chain(manager, db, engines, episode_id, stages, opts, depends_on=None) -> list[int]`：
    - 第一个阶段是 script / storyboard 时，只提交这一个任务，其余阶段放进 payload 的 `then`（连同 `options`），由它完成后通过 `continue_chain` 接着提交
    - keyframes：每个选中镜头提交 `candidates` 个 `image.keyframe`（model_family = 图像引擎族）
    - videos：每个选中镜头一个 `video.shot`，依赖该镜头本次提交的所有首帧任务
    - export：一个 `export.episode`，依赖本次提交的所有视频任务（没有视频任务时依赖首帧任务）
    - `shots` 按镜头编号（不分大小写）或 id 匹配，找不到抛 `JobError`；没有镜头时 keyframes / videos 抛 `JobError`；阶段名非法或 script/storyboard 不在最前抛 `JobError`
- 测试工具：`FakeFFmpeg`（记录 `assemble` 参数并写出输出文件）

- [ ] **Step 1: 补测试工具**

在 `backend/tests/helpers.py` 末尾追加：

```python
from pathlib import Path


class FakeFFmpeg:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def assemble(self, clips, out, *, width, height, fps, transitions, work_dir):
        self.calls.append({"clips": list(clips), "out": Path(out), "width": width,
                           "height": height, "fps": fps, "transitions": list(transitions)})
        Path(out).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(out).write_bytes(b"mp4")
        return Path(out)
```

```python
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

import backend.pipeline  # noqa: F401  注册全部处理器
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Export, Shot
from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot
from backend.pipeline.export import run_export
from backend.pipeline.runs import RunOptions, submit_chain

from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS
from backend.queue.manager import JobManager
from backend.tests.helpers import FakeCtx, FakeFFmpeg, make_gateway, make_services, seed_episode


def seed_shots(db, n=3, **kw):
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db, **kw)
    ids = []
    with db.session() as s:
        for i in range(n):
            sh = Shot(episode_id=ep_id, order=i, code=f"S{i + 1:02d}",
                      transition_out="dissolve_0.5" if i == 0 else "hard_cut")
            s.add(sh)
            s.flush()
            ids.append(sh.id)
    return ep_id, ids


def add_video(sv, shot_id, name):
    p = sv.media.new_media_path("demo", "video", "mp4")
    p.write_bytes(b"v")
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        add_candidate(s, get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot_id, "video"), file_path=sv.media.rel(p),
                      engine="h3_video", params={}, input_hash=name, job_id=None)
    return p


def test_export_assembles_selected_videos_in_order(tmp_path, db):
    ff = FakeFFmpeg()
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, ffmpeg=ff)
    ep_id, ids = seed_shots(db)
    paths = [add_video(sv, sid, f"h{i}") for i, sid in enumerate(ids)]
    res = run_export(FakeCtx(sv, job_id=5), {"episode_id": ep_id})
    call = ff.calls[0]
    assert call["clips"] == paths
    assert (call["width"], call["height"], call["fps"]) == (1080, 1920, 24)
    assert call["transitions"] == ["dissolve_0.5", "hard_cut"]
    assert res["file"].startswith("demo/exports/ep01_9x16_") and res["file"].endswith(".mp4")
    with db.session() as s:
        assert s.get(Export, res["export_id"]).variant == "9x16"
        assert s.get(Episode, ep_id).status == "exported"


def test_export_reports_missing_videos(tmp_path, db):
    ff = FakeFFmpeg()
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, ffmpeg=ff)
    ep_id, ids = seed_shots(db)
    add_video(sv, ids[0], "a")
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="S02, S03"):
        run_export(FakeCtx(sv), {"episode_id": ep_id})
    run_export(FakeCtx(sv), {"episode_id": ep_id, "allow_missing": True})
    assert len(ff.calls[0]["clips"]) == 1 and ff.calls[0]["transitions"] == []


@pytest.fixture
def manager(db):
    return JobManager(db, HANDLERS, poll=0.02)  # 不启动 worker，只看提交了什么


def jobs(db):
    from backend.domain.models import Job
    with db.session() as s:
        return [(j.id, j.type, j.payload, j.depends_on, j.model_family, j.gpu)
                for j in s.scalars(select(Job).order_by(Job.id))]


def test_chain_keyframes_videos_export(tmp_path, db, manager):
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db)
    ep_id, ids = seed_shots(db, n=2)
    out = submit_chain(manager, db, sv.engines, ep_id, ["keyframes", "videos", "export"],
                       RunOptions(candidates=2))
    rows = jobs(db)
    assert [r[1] for r in rows] == ["image.keyframe"] * 4 + ["video.shot"] * 2 + ["export.episode"]
    kf = {sid: [r[0] for r in rows if r[1] == "image.keyframe" and r[2]["shot_id"] == sid] for sid in ids}
    vids = [r for r in rows if r[1] == "video.shot"]
    assert vids[0][3] == kf[ids[0]] and vids[1][3] == kf[ids[1]]
    assert rows[-1][3] == [v[0] for v in vids]
    assert rows[0][4] == "zimage" and vids[0][4] == "h3" and rows[0][5] is True
    assert out == [r[0] for r in rows]


def test_chain_shot_filter(tmp_path, db, manager):

    submit_chain(manager, db, sv.engines, ep_id, ["keyframes"], RunOptions(shots=["s02", str(ids[2])]))
    assert [r[2]["shot_id"] for r in jobs(db)] == [ids[1], ids[2]]
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="S09"):
        submit_chain(manager, db, sv.engines, ep_id, ["keyframes"], RunOptions(shots=["S09"]))


def test_chain_validation(tmp_path, db, manager):
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db)
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db)
    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="分镜"):

    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="未知阶段"):

    with pytest.raises(JobError, match="最前面"):

def test_chain_starting_with_script_defers_the_rest(tmp_path, db, manager):
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db)
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db)
    [jid] = submit_chain(manager, db, sv.engines, ep_id, list(("script", "storyboard", "keyframes")),
                         RunOptions(force=True, candidates=2))
    (_, jtype, payload, _, _, gpu), = jobs(db)
    assert jtype == "script" and gpu is False
    assert payload["then"] == ["storyboard", "keyframes"]
    assert payload["options"]["candidates"] == 2 and payload["force"] is True


def test_script_handler_continues_chain(tmp_path, db, manager):
    from backend.tests.test_script import STRUCTURE, VISUAL
    gw, _ = make_gateway([STRUCTURE, VISUAL])
    sv = make_services(tmp_path, db, llm=gw)
    manager.services = sv
    _, ep_id = seed_episode(db)

               {"episode_id": ep_id, "then": ["storyboard", "keyframes"],
                "options": {"candidates": 1}})
    (_, jtype, payload, _, _, _), = jobs(db)
    assert jtype == "storyboard" and payload["then"] == ["keyframes"]
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 export.py**

```python
"""合成导出：按镜头顺序把选定视频拼成成片。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from datetime import datetime

from backend.domain.models import Episode, Export
from backend.domain.slots import selected_candidate
from backend.pipeline.common import load_episode
from backend.queue.errors import JobError
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS

OUTPUT_SIZES = {"9:16": (1080, 1920), "16:9": (1920, 1080)}
OUTPUT_FPS = 24


@HANDLERS.register("export.episode")
def run_export(ctx, payload: dict) -> dict:
    sv = ctx.services
    episode_id = int(payload["episode_id"])
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        ep, proj = load_episode(s, episode_id)
        if not ep.shots:
            raise JobError("这一集还没有镜头")
        clips, transitions, missing = [], [], []
        for sh in ep.shots:
            c = selected_candidate(s, "shot", sh.id, "video")
            if c is None:
                missing.append(sh.code)
                continue
            clips.append(sv.media.abs(c.file_path))
            transitions.append(sh.transition_out)
        slug, number, aspect = proj.slug, ep.number, proj.aspect
    if missing and not payload.get("allow_missing"):
        raise JobError(f"以下镜头还没有选定视频：{', '.join(missing)}")
    if not clips:
        raise JobError("没有可合成的视频")
    if aspect not in OUTPUT_SIZES:
        raise JobError(f"不支持的画幅 {aspect}")
    width, height = OUTPUT_SIZES[aspect]
    variant = aspect.replace(":", "x")
    out = sv.media.export_path(slug, f"ep{number:02d}_{variant}_{datetime.now():%Y%m%d_%H%M%S}.mp4")
    work = sv.media.run_dir(slug, ctx.job_id) / "work"
    ctx.log(f"合成 {len(clips)} 个镜头 → {width}x{height}@{OUTPUT_FPS}")
    sv.ffmpeg.assemble(clips, out, width=width, height=height, fps=OUTPUT_FPS,
                       transitions=transitions[:-1], work_dir=work)
    shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
    rel = sv.media.rel(out)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        e = Export(episode_id=episode_id, variant=variant, file_path=rel,
                   settings={"width": width, "height": height, "fps": OUTPUT_FPS,
                             "clips": len(clips), "missing": missing})
        s.add(e)
        s.get(Episode, episode_id).status = "exported"
        s.flush()
        export_id = e.id
    return {"export_id": export_id, "file": rel}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 runs.py**

```python
"""把"跑某几个阶段"拆成任务并串好依赖。API、CLI 和阶段处理器（then 链）都用它。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

from backend.domain.db import Database
from backend.domain.models import Episode
from backend.queue.errors import JobError

STAGES = ("script", "storyboard", "keyframes", "videos", "export")
LLM_STAGES = ("script", "storyboard")


@dataclass
class RunOptions:
    shots: list[str] | None = None
    candidates: int = 1
    force: bool = False
    priority: int = 0


def _pick_shots(shots, wanted: list[str] | None):
    if not wanted:
        return list(shots)
    by_code = {sh.code.upper(): sh for sh in shots}
    by_id = {str(sh.id): sh for sh in shots}
    out = []
    for tok in wanted:
        t = str(tok).strip()
        sh = by_code.get(t.upper()) or by_id.get(t)
        if sh is None:
            raise JobError(f"找不到镜头：{t}")
        if sh not in out:
            out.append(sh)
    return out


def _family(engines, kind: str, name: str) -> str:
    try:
        return (engines.image(name) if kind == "image" else engines.video(name)).family
    except KeyError as e:
        raise JobError(str(e)) from None


def submit_chain(manager, db: Database, engines, episode_id: int, stages: list[str],
                 opts: RunOptions, depends_on: list[int] | None = None) -> list[int]:
    stages = list(stages)
    bad = [x for x in stages if x not in STAGES]
    if bad:
        raise JobError(f"未知阶段：{', '.join(bad)}，可选：{', '.join(STAGES)}")
    if not stages:
        return []
    if any(x in LLM_STAGES for x in stages[1:]) and stages[0] not in LLM_STAGES:
        raise JobError("script / storyboard 必须排在最前面")
    deps = list(depends_on or [])
    with db.session() as s:
        ep = s.get(Episode, episode_id)
        if ep is None:
            raise LookupError(f"找不到剧集 {episode_id}")
        proj = ep.project
        common = {"project_id": proj.id, "priority": opts.priority}
        picked = [] if stages[0] in LLM_STAGES else [
            (sh.id, sh.image_engine or proj.image_engine, sh.video_engine or proj.video_engine)
            for sh in _pick_shots(ep.shots, opts.shots)]

    first = stages[0]
    if first in LLM_STAGES:
        payload: dict = {"episode_id": episode_id, "force": opts.force}
        if stages[1:]:
            payload["then"] = stages[1:]
            payload["options"] = asdict(opts)
        return [manager.submit(first, payload, owner_type="episode", owner_id=episode_id,
                               depends_on=deps, **common)]

    if ("keyframes" in stages or "videos" in stages) and not picked:
        raise JobError("这一集还没有分镜，先跑 storyboard")
    ids: list[int] = []
    kf: dict[int, list[int]] = {}
    vids: list[int] = []
    if "keyframes" in stages:
        for sid, image_engine, _ in picked:
            fam = _family(engines, "image", image_engine)
            kf[sid] = [manager.submit("image.keyframe", {"shot_id": sid}, owner_type="shot",
                                      owner_id=sid, model_family=fam, depends_on=deps, **common)
                       for _ in range(max(1, opts.candidates))]
            ids += kf[sid]
    if "videos" in stages:
        for sid, _, video_engine in picked:
            fam = _family(engines, "video", video_engine)
            jid = manager.submit("video.shot", {"shot_id": sid}, owner_type="shot", owner_id=sid,
                                 model_family=fam, depends_on=deps + kf.get(sid, []), **common)
            vids.append(jid)
            ids.append(jid)
    if "export" in stages:
        upstream = vids or [j for js in kf.values() for j in js]
        ids.append(manager.submit("export.episode", {"episode_id": episode_id},
                                  owner_type="episode", owner_id=episode_id,
                                  depends_on=deps + upstream, **common))
    return ids
```

  - `views.candidate_out(c) -> CandidateOut`（`url = "/media/" + quote(file_path)`）、`views.shot_detail(s, sv, shot, proj, style) -> ShotDetail`（含每个槽位的 `stale`）、`views.episode_detail(s, sv, ep) -> EpisodeDetail`

```python
def test_create_project_with_chinese_title(api):
    r = api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "提词员 第一季"})
    assert r.status_code == 201, r.text
    p = r.json()
    assert p["slug"] == "提词员-第一季" and p["aspect"] == "9:16"
    assert p["image_engine"] == "zimage" and p["video_engine"] == "h3_video"
    assert (api.app.state.services.media.root / "提词员-第一季").is_dir()
    assert [x["slug"] for x in api.get("/api/projects").json()] == ["提词员-第一季"]
    assert api.get("/api/projects/提词员-第一季").json()["title"] == "提词员 第一季"


def test_project_validation(api):
    assert api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "a", "slug": "demo"}).status_code == 201
    assert api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "b", "slug": "demo"}).status_code == 409
    r = api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "c", "video_engine": "sora"})
    assert r.status_code == 400 and "sora" in r.json()["detail"]
    assert api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "d", "slug": "a/b"}).status_code == 400
    assert api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "e", "aspect": "4:3"}).status_code == 422
    assert api.get("/api/projects/nope").status_code == 404


def test_patch_and_delete_project(api):
    api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "demo"})
    r = api.patch("/api/projects/demo", json={"video_engine": "wan22_i2v", "seed": 7})
    assert r.json()["video_engine"] == "wan22_i2v" and r.json()["seed"] == 7
    assert api.patch("/api/projects/demo", json={"image_engine": "h3_video"}).status_code == 400
    assert api.delete("/api/projects/demo").status_code == 204
    assert api.get("/api/projects/demo").status_code == 404


def test_episodes_crud(api):
    api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "demo"})
    e1 = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"source_text": "梗概", "target_seconds": 30}).json()
    e2 = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"title": "第二集"}).json()
    assert (e1["number"], e2["number"]) == (1, 2)
    assert api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"number": 2}).status_code == 409
    d = api.get("/api/projects/demo/episodes/by-number/1").json()
    assert d["id"] == e1["id"] and d["shots"] == [] and d["scene_blocks"] == []
    r = api.patch(f"/api/episodes/{e1['id']}", json={"title": "改名"})
    assert r.json()["title"] == "改名" and r.json()["source_text"] == "梗概"
    assert [e["number"] for e in api.get("/api/projects/demo/episodes").json()] == [1, 2]
    assert api.delete(f"/api/episodes/{e2['id']}").status_code == 204
    assert api.get(f"/api/episodes/{e2['id']}").status_code == 404


def test_assets_and_style(api):
    from backend.domain.models import Character, Episode, Location, Project, SceneBlock

    api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "demo"})
    ep = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"source_text": "x"}).json()
    db = api.app.state.services.db
    with db.session() as s:
        pid = s.query(Project).one().id
        s.add(Character(project_id=pid, name="林夏"))
        loc = Location(project_id=pid, name="直播间")
        s.add(loc)
        s.flush()
        s.get(Episode, ep["id"]).scene_blocks.append(SceneBlock(order=0, text="段落", location_id=loc.id))
    ch = api.get("/api/projects/demo/characters").json()[0]
    assert api.patch(f"/api/characters/{ch['id']}", json={"visual_prompt": "LOOK"}).json()["visual_prompt"] == "LOOK"
    loc = api.get("/api/projects/demo/locations").json()[0]
    assert api.patch(f"/api/locations/{loc['id']}", json={"time_of_day": "夜"}).json()["time_of_day"] == "夜"
    assert api.get("/api/projects/demo/style").json() is None
    st = api.put("/api/projects/demo/style", json={"anchor_text": "ANCHOR", "mood_keywords": ["tense"]}).json()
    assert st["anchor_text"] == "ANCHOR" and api.get("/api/projects/demo/style").json()["mood_keywords"] == ["tense"]
    blk = api.get(f"/api/episodes/{ep['id']}").json()["scene_blocks"][0]
    assert api.patch(f"/api/scene-blocks/{blk['id']}", json={"text": "改过"}).json()["text"] == "改过"
```

在 `backend/services.py` 末尾追加：

```python
def build_services(cfg: Config, db: Database, manager: Any) -> Services:
    from backend.engines.comfyui.client import ComfyClient
    from backend.engines.comfyui.runner import ComfyRunner
    from backend.llm.gateway import LLMGateway
    from backend.llm.providers import build_providers

    client = ComfyClient(cfg.comfyui.url, timeout=cfg.comfyui.timeout,
                         max_retries=cfg.comfyui.max_retries)
    runner = ComfyRunner(client)
    llm = LLMGateway(cfg.llm, build_providers(cfg.llm), gpu_guard=manager.gpu_exclusive)
    # 换模型族、或本机 LLM 要用显卡时，先让 ComfyUI 把模型卸掉
    manager.on_family_switch = lambda old, new: runner.free()
    manager.on_gpu_handover = runner.free
    return Services(cfg=cfg, db=db, media=MediaStore(cfg.data_dir), engines=EngineRegistry(cfg),
                    llm=llm, ffmpeg=FFmpeg(cfg.ffmpeg.ffmpeg, cfg.ffmpeg.ffprobe),
                    comfy=runner, comfy_client=client)
```

```python
"""HTTP API 的输入输出模型。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from backend.llm.schemas import Transition

Aspect = Literal["9:16", "16:9"]
Stage = Literal["script", "storyboard", "keyframes", "videos", "export", "all"]


class ORM(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)


class ProjectIn(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    slug: str | None = None
    aspect: Aspect = "9:16"
    image_engine: str = "zimage"
    video_engine: str = "h3_video"
    seed: int = 12345
    negative: str = ""


class ProjectPatch(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = Field(None, min_length=1, max_length=200)
    aspect: Aspect | None = None
    image_engine: str | None = None
    video_engine: str | None = None
    seed: int | None = None
    negative: str | None = None


class ProjectOut(ORM):
    id: int
    slug: str
    title: str
    aspect: str
    image_engine: str
    video_engine: str
    seed: int
    negative: str
    style_bible_id: int | None
    created_at: datetime


class StyleIO(ORM):
    anchor_text: str = ""
    palette: str = ""
    lighting: str = ""
    texture: str = ""
    character_rules: str = ""
    mood_keywords: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    negative: str = ""


class CharacterOut(ORM):
    id: int
    project_id: int
    name: str
    description: str
    visual_prompt: str
    core_features: str
    voice: str | None


class CharacterPatch(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = None
    description: str | None = None
    visual_prompt: str | None = None
    core_features: str | None = None
    voice: str | None = None


class LocationOut(ORM):
    id: int
    project_id: int
    name: str
    time_of_day: str
    atmosphere: str
    visual_prompt: str


class LocationPatch(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = None
    time_of_day: str | None = None
    atmosphere: str | None = None
    visual_prompt: str | None = None


class EpisodeIn(BaseModel):
    title: str = ""
    source_text: str = ""
    target_seconds: float = Field(60.0, gt=0, le=3600)
    number: int | None = Field(None, ge=1)


class EpisodePatch(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = None
    source_text: str | None = None
    target_seconds: float | None = Field(None, gt=0, le=3600)


class EpisodeOut(ORM):
    id: int
    project_id: int
    number: int
    title: str
    source_text: str
    target_seconds: float
    status: str


class SceneBlockOut(ORM):
    id: int
    order: int
    location_id: int | None
    text: str


class SceneBlockPatch(BaseModel):
    text: str | None = None
    location_id: int | None = None


class CandidateOut(ORM):
    id: int
    slot_id: int
    file_path: str
    url: str = ""
    engine: str
    params: dict
    input_hash: str
    job_id: int | None
    note: str
    rating: int | None
    created_at: datetime


class SlotOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    kind: str
    selected_candidate_id: int | None
    stale: bool = False
    candidates: list[CandidateOut] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ShotFields(BaseModel):
    shot_size: str | None = None
    camera_move: str | None = None
    action: str | None = None
    dialogue: list[dict] | None = None
    cast: list[dict] | None = None
    props: list | None = None
    image_prompt: str | None = None
    motion_prompt: str | None = None
    audio_prompt: str | None = None
    image_engine: str | None = None
    video_engine: str | None = None
    duration: float | None = Field(None, gt=0, le=30)
    use_end_frame: bool | None = None
    continue_from_prev: bool | None = None
    transition_out: Transition | None = None


class ShotIn(ShotFields):
    after_shot_id: int | None = None


class ShotOut(ORM):
    id: int
    episode_id: int
    scene_block_id: int | None
    order: int
    code: str
    shot_size: str
    camera_move: str
    action: str
    dialogue: list
    cast: list
    props: list
    image_prompt: str
    motion_prompt: str
    audio_prompt: str
    image_engine: str | None
    video_engine: str | None
    duration: float
    use_end_frame: bool
    continue_from_prev: bool
    transition_out: str


class ShotDetail(ShotOut):
    slots: list[SlotOut] = Field(default_factory=list)


class EpisodeDetail(EpisodeOut):
    scene_blocks: list[SceneBlockOut] = Field(default_factory=list)
    shots: list[ShotDetail] = Field(default_factory=list)


class ReorderIn(BaseModel):

class RunIn(BaseModel):
    stage: Stage

    candidates: int = Field(1, ge=1, le=4)

    priority: int = 0


class RunOut(BaseModel):
```
