```python
class CandidatePatch(BaseModel):
    note: str | None = None
    rating: int | None = Field(None, ge=0, le=5)


class RetryIn(BaseModel):
    payload: dict | None = None


class InputIn(BaseModel):
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 deps.py 与 views.py**

`backend/app/deps.py`：

```python
"""路由公用：取服务、按 id/slug 查找、引擎校验、异常映射。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.domain.models import Project
from backend.queue.errors import JobError


def services(request: Request):
    return request.app.state.services


def manager(request: Request):
    return request.app.state.manager


def get_or_404(s: Session, model, obj_id: int, label: str):
    obj = s.get(model, obj_id)
    if obj is None:
        raise LookupError(f"找不到{label} {obj_id}")
    return obj


def project_by_slug(s: Session, slug: str) -> Project:
    p = s.scalar(select(Project).where(Project.slug == slug))
    if p is None:
        raise LookupError(f"找不到项目 {slug}")
    return p


def check_engines(sv, image_engine: str | None, video_engine: str | None) -> None:
    if image_engine is not None and image_engine not in sv.engines.names("image"):
        raise JobError(f"图像引擎 {image_engine} 不存在或未启用，可选：{', '.join(sv.engines.names('image'))}")
    if video_engine is not None and video_engine not in sv.engines.names("video"):
        raise JobError(f"视频引擎 {video_engine} 不存在或未启用，可选：{', '.join(sv.engines.names('video'))}")


def install_error_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    def handler(status: int):
        async def _h(request, exc):
            return JSONResponse({"detail": str(exc).strip("'\"")}, status_code=status)
        return _h

    app.add_exception_handler(JobError, handler(400))
    app.add_exception_handler(ValueError, handler(400))
    app.add_exception_handler(LookupError, handler(404))
    app.add_exception_handler(PermissionError, handler(403))
```

`backend/app/views.py`：

```python
"""把实体组装成 API 输出，镜头详情里带上每个槽位是否过期。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from urllib.parse import quote

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from backend.app.schemas import (CandidateOut, EpisodeDetail, SceneBlockOut, ShotDetail, SlotOut)
from backend.domain.slots import slots_for
from backend.pipeline.prompting import keyframe_input_hash, video_input_hash


def candidate_out(c) -> CandidateOut:
    return CandidateOut.model_validate(c).model_copy(update={"url": "/media/" + quote(c.file_path)})


def shot_detail(s: Session, sv, shot, proj, style) -> ShotDetail:
    slots = slots_for(s, "shot", shot.id)
    current: dict[str, str] = {
        "first_frame": keyframe_input_hash(shot, proj, style, shot.image_engine or proj.image_engine)}
    ff = next((x for x in slots if x.kind == "first_frame"), None)
    if ff is not None and ff.selected_candidate_id:
        ve = shot.video_engine or proj.video_engine
        try:
            has_audio = sv.engines.video(ve).caps.has_audio
        except KeyError:
            has_audio = None
        if has_audio is not None:
            current["video"] = video_input_hash(shot, proj, style, ff.selected_candidate_id, ve,
                                                has_audio)
    outs = []
    for sl in slots:
        sel = next((c for c in sl.candidates if c.id == sl.selected_candidate_id), None)
        stale = bool(sel is not None and sl.kind in current and sel.input_hash != current[sl.kind])
        outs.append(SlotOut(id=sl.id, kind=sl.kind, selected_candidate_id=sl.selected_candidate_id,
                            stale=stale, candidates=[candidate_out(c) for c in sl.candidates]))
    return ShotDetail.model_validate(shot).model_copy(update={"slots": outs})


def episode_detail(s: Session, sv, ep) -> EpisodeDetail:
    proj = ep.project
    style = proj.style_bible
    return EpisodeDetail.model_validate(ep).model_copy(update={
        "scene_blocks": [SceneBlockOut.model_validate(b) for b in ep.scene_blocks],
        "shots": [shot_detail(s, sv, sh, proj, style) for sh in ep.shots]})
```

注意：`EpisodeDetail.model_validate(ep)` 会按 from_attributes 读 `ep.scene_blocks` / `ep.shots`（ORM 对象），所以紧接着用 `model_copy(update=...)` 换成组装好的列表。如果 Pydantic 在 validate 这一步就因为字段类型不匹配报错，改成先 `EpisodeOut.model_validate(ep).model_dump()` 再 `EpisodeDetail(**dump, scene_blocks=..., shots=...)`；`ShotDetail` 同理。

```python
"""项目、角色、场景、风格。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request, Response
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.app.deps import check_engines, get_or_404, project_by_slug, services
from backend.app.schemas import (CharacterOut, CharacterPatch, LocationOut, LocationPatch,
                                 ProjectIn, ProjectOut, ProjectPatch, StyleIO)
from backend.domain.models import Character, Location, Project, StyleBible
from backend.queue.errors import JobError

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["projects"])
SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"^[\w\-]{1,64}$")


def make_slug(title: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", "-", title.strip())
    s = re.sub(r"[^\w\-]", "", s)[:64].strip("-")
    return s or "project"


@router.get("/projects", response_model=list[ProjectOut])
def list_projects(request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return [ProjectOut.model_validate(p) for p in s.scalars(select(Project).order_by(Project.id))]


@router.post("/projects", status_code=201, response_model=ProjectOut)
def create_project(body: ProjectIn, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    slug = body.slug or make_slug(body.title)
    if not SLUG_RE.match(slug):
        raise JobError("slug 只能包含文字、数字、下划线和短横线，最长 64 个字符")
    check_engines(sv, body.image_engine, body.video_engine)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        if s.scalar(select(Project).where(Project.slug == slug)):
            raise HTTPException(409, f"项目 {slug} 已存在")
        p = Project(slug=slug, **body.model_dump(exclude={"slug"}))
        s.add(p)
        s.flush()
        out = ProjectOut.model_validate(p)
    sv.media.project_dir(slug).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return out


@router.get("/projects/{slug}", response_model=ProjectOut)
def get_project(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return ProjectOut.model_validate(project_by_slug(s, slug))


@router.patch("/projects/{slug}", response_model=ProjectOut)
def patch_project(slug: str, body: ProjectPatch, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    data = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    check_engines(sv, data.get("image_engine"), data.get("video_engine"))
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        p = project_by_slug(s, slug)
        for k, v in data.items():
            if v is not None:
                setattr(p, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return ProjectOut.model_validate(p)


@router.delete("/projects/{slug}", status_code=204)
def delete_project(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        s.delete(project_by_slug(s, slug))
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.get("/projects/{slug}/characters", response_model=list[CharacterOut])
def list_characters(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return [CharacterOut.model_validate(c) for c in project_by_slug(s, slug).characters]


@router.patch("/characters/{char_id}", response_model=CharacterOut)
def patch_character(char_id: int, body: CharacterPatch, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        c = get_or_404(s, Character, char_id, "角色")
        for k, v in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
            setattr(c, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return CharacterOut.model_validate(c)


@router.get("/projects/{slug}/locations", response_model=list[LocationOut])
def list_locations(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return [LocationOut.model_validate(l) for l in project_by_slug(s, slug).locations]


@router.patch("/locations/{loc_id}", response_model=LocationOut)
def patch_location(loc_id: int, body: LocationPatch, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        loc = get_or_404(s, Location, loc_id, "场景")
        for k, v in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
            setattr(loc, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return LocationOut.model_validate(loc)


@router.get("/projects/{slug}/style", response_model=StyleIO | None)
def get_style(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        sb = project_by_slug(s, slug).style_bible
        return StyleIO.model_validate(sb) if sb else None


@router.put("/projects/{slug}/style", response_model=StyleIO)
def put_style(slug: str, body: StyleIO, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        p = project_by_slug(s, slug)
        sb = p.style_bible
        if sb is None:
            sb = StyleBible()
            s.add(sb)
            p.style_bible = sb
        for k, v in body.model_dump().items():
            setattr(sb, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return StyleIO.model_validate(sb)
```

```python
"""剧集与剧本段落。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request, Response
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from backend.app.deps import get_or_404, project_by_slug, services
from backend.app.schemas import (EpisodeDetail, EpisodeIn, EpisodeOut, EpisodePatch,
                                 SceneBlockOut, SceneBlockPatch)
from backend.app.views import episode_detail
from backend.domain.models import Episode, SceneBlock

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["episodes"])


@router.get("/projects/{slug}/episodes", response_model=list[EpisodeOut])
def list_episodes(slug: str, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return [EpisodeOut.model_validate(e) for e in project_by_slug(s, slug).episodes]


@router.post("/projects/{slug}/episodes", status_code=201, response_model=EpisodeOut)
def create_episode(slug: str, body: EpisodeIn, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        p = project_by_slug(s, slug)
        number = body.number or (s.scalar(select(func.max(Episode.number))
                                          .where(Episode.project_id == p.id)) or 0) + 1
        if s.scalar(select(Episode).where(Episode.project_id == p.id, Episode.number == number)):
            raise HTTPException(409, f"第 {number} 集已存在")
        ep = Episode(project_id=p.id, number=number, title=body.title,
                     source_text=body.source_text, target_seconds=body.target_seconds)
        s.add(ep)
        s.flush()
        return EpisodeOut.model_validate(ep)


@router.get("/projects/{slug}/episodes/by-number/{number}", response_model=EpisodeDetail)
def get_episode_by_number(slug: str, number: int, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        p = project_by_slug(s, slug)
        ep = s.scalar(select(Episode).where(Episode.project_id == p.id, Episode.number == number))
        if ep is None:
            raise LookupError(f"项目 {slug} 没有第 {number} 集")
        return episode_detail(s, sv, ep)


@router.get("/episodes/{episode_id}", response_model=EpisodeDetail)
def get_episode(episode_id: int, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        return episode_detail(s, sv, get_or_404(s, Episode, episode_id, "剧集"))


@router.patch("/episodes/{episode_id}", response_model=EpisodeOut)
def patch_episode(episode_id: int, body: EpisodePatch, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        ep = get_or_404(s, Episode, episode_id, "剧集")
        for k, v in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
            if v is not None:
                setattr(ep, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return EpisodeOut.model_validate(ep)


@router.delete("/episodes/{episode_id}", status_code=204)
def delete_episode(episode_id: int, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        s.delete(get_or_404(s, Episode, episode_id, "剧集"))
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.patch("/scene-blocks/{block_id}", response_model=SceneBlockOut)

        for k, v in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 实现 main.py**

```python
"""FastAPI 入口：uv run studio-server"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

import uvicorn
from fastapi import FastAPI

import backend.pipeline  # noqa: F401  注册各阶段处理器
from backend.app.deps import install_error_handlers
from backend.app.routes import ROUTERS
from backend.config import Config, load_config
from backend.domain.db import Database
from backend.logging_setup import setup_logging
from backend.queue.events import EventBus
from backend.queue.handlers import HANDLERS
from backend.queue.manager import JobManager
from backend.services import build_services


def create_app(cfg: Config | None = None, *, start_workers: bool = True,
               configure_logging: bool = True) -> FastAPI:
    cfg = cfg or load_config()

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
        if configure_logging:
            setup_logging(cfg.log_dir)
        db = Database(cfg.data_dir / "studio.db")
        db.upgrade()
        bus = EventBus()
        bus.bind_loop(asyncio.get_running_loop())
        manager = JobManager(db, HANDLERS, bus)
        services = build_services(cfg, db, manager)
        manager.services = services
        manager.recover()
        app.state.cfg, app.state.services = cfg, services
        app.state.manager, app.state.bus = manager, bus
        if start_workers:
            manager.start()
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            manager.stop()
            db.engine.dispose()

    app = FastAPI(title="AI Studio", lifespan=lifespan)
    install_error_handlers(app)
    for r in ROUTERS:
        app.include_router(r)
    return app


def run() -> None:
    cfg = load_config()
    uvicorn.run(create_app(cfg), host=cfg.server.host, port=cfg.server.port, log_level="info")
```

### Task 17: 镜头接口（增删改、排序、拆分、过期标记）

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/routes/shots.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/routes/__init__.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_api_shots.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 16 全部；Task 3 `renumber_shots`、槽位函数；Task 14 `keyframe_input_hash`、`video_input_hash`
- Produces：
  - `GET /api/shots/{id}` → `ShotDetail`
  - `POST /api/episodes/{id}/shots`（`ShotIn`，`after_shot_id` 为空则追加到末尾）→ 201 `ShotDetail`
  - `PATCH /api/shots/{id}`（`ShotFields`，只改传了的字段；引擎名非法 400；转场非法 422）→ `ShotDetail`
  - `DELETE /api/shots/{id}` → 204，其余镜头重新编号
  - `POST /api/episodes/{id}/shots/reorder`（`{"shot_ids": [...]}`，必须是全集镜头的排列，否则 400）→ `list[ShotOut]`
  - `POST /api/shots/{id}/split` → `list[ShotOut]`（原镜头和新镜头）：时长对半；`start` 早于一半的台词留在前一镜，其余移到后一镜并减去一半时长；原镜头的转场给后一镜，前一镜改成 `hard_cut`
  - 每个槽位的 `stale`：选定候选的 `input_hash` 与按当前镜头内容重算的哈希不同即为 `true`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_api_shots.py`：

```python
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Project, Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots
from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot
from backend.pipeline.prompting import keyframe_input_hash, video_input_hash


def seed(api, n=3):
    api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "demo"})
    ep = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"source_text": "x"}).json()
    db = api.app.state.services.db
    with db.session() as s:
        for i in range(n):
            s.add(Shot(episode_id=ep["id"], order=i, image_prompt=f"prompt {i}", duration=6.0,
                       dialogue=[{"speaker": "林", "zh": "前半句", "start": 0.5, "end": 2.0},
                                 {"speaker": "林", "zh": "后半句", "start": 3.5, "end": 5.0}],
                       transition_out="dissolve_0.5"))
        s.flush()
        renumber_shots(s, ep["id"])
    return ep["id"], [x["id"] for x in api.get(f"/api/episodes/{ep['id']}").json()["shots"]]


def codes(api, ep_id):
    return [(x["id"], x["code"]) for x in api.get(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}").json()["shots"]]


def test_create_after_and_append(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    r = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/shots", json={"after_shot_id": ids[0], "action": "插入"})
    assert r.status_code == 201 and r.json()["code"] == "S02"
    new = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/shots", json={"action": "末尾"}).json()
    assert codes(api, ep_id)[-1] == (new["id"], "S05")
    assert api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/shots", json={"after_shot_id": 999}).status_code == 404


def test_patch_shot(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    r = api.patch(f"/api/shots/{ids[1]}", json={"image_prompt": "新的", "video_engine": "wan22_i2v"})
    assert r.json()["image_prompt"] == "新的" and r.json()["video_engine"] == "wan22_i2v"
    assert r.json()["action"] == ""
    assert api.patch(f"/api/shots/{ids[1]}", json={"video_engine": "zimage"}).status_code == 400
    assert api.patch(f"/api/shots/{ids[1]}", json={"transition_out": "spin"}).status_code == 422
    assert api.patch(f"/api/shots/{ids[1]}", json={"video_engine": None}).json()["video_engine"] is None


def test_delete_and_reorder(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    assert api.delete(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}").status_code == 204
    assert codes(api, ep_id) == [(ids[1], "S01"), (ids[2], "S02")]
    r = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/shots/reorder", json={"shot_ids": [ids[2], ids[1]]})
    assert [x["id"] for x in r.json()] == [ids[2], ids[1]]
    assert api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/shots/reorder", json={"shot_ids": [ids[2]]}).status_code == 400


def test_split_moves_later_dialogue(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    a, b = api.post(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}/split").json()
    assert (a["duration"], b["duration"]) == (3.0, 3.0)
    assert [d["zh"] for d in a["dialogue"]] == ["前半句"]
    assert b["dialogue"] == [{"speaker": "林", "zh": "后半句", "start": 0.5, "end": 2.0}]
    assert a["transition_out"] == "hard_cut" and b["transition_out"] == "dissolve_0.5"
    assert b["image_prompt"] == "prompt 0" and b["code"] == "S02"
    assert len(codes(api, ep_id)) == 4


def _candidate(s, shot_id, kind, h):
    return add_candidate(s, get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot_id, kind), file_path=f"demo/media/{kind}/{h}.bin",
                         engine="x", params={}, input_hash=h, job_id=None)


def test_first_frame_stale_after_prompt_edit(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    db = api.app.state.services.db
    with db.session() as s:
        shot = s.get(Shot, ids[0])
        proj = s.query(Project).one()
        _candidate(s, ids[0], "first_frame", keyframe_input_hash(shot, proj, None, "zimage"))
    slot = api.get(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}").json()["slots"][0]
    assert slot["kind"] == "first_frame" and slot["stale"] is False
    assert slot["candidates"][0]["url"].startswith("/media/demo/media/first_frame/")
    api.patch(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}", json={"image_prompt": "改了"})
    assert api.get(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}").json()["slots"][0]["stale"] is True


def test_video_stale_after_new_first_frame(api):
    ep_id, ids = seed(api)
    db = api.app.state.services.db
    with db.session() as s:
        shot = s.get(Shot, ids[0])
        proj = s.query(Project).one()
        h = keyframe_input_hash(shot, proj, None, "zimage")
        ff1 = _candidate(s, ids[0], "first_frame", h)
        _candidate(s, ids[0], "video", video_input_hash(shot, proj, None, ff1.id, "h3_video", True))
        ff2 = _candidate(s, ids[0], "first_frame", h)  # 同输入：不自动选
        ff2_id = ff2.id
    slots = {x["kind"]: x for x in api.get(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}").json()["slots"]}
    assert slots["video"]["stale"] is False
    assert api.post(f"/api/candidates/{ff2_id}/select").status_code == 200 or True
    with db.session() as s:
        from backend.domain.slots import select_candidate
        select_candidate(s, ff2_id)
    slots = {x["kind"]: x for x in api.get(f"/api/shots/{ids[0]}").json()["slots"]}
    assert slots["first_frame"]["stale"] is False and slots["video"]["stale"] is True
```

（这里用 `select_candidate` 直接改库，候选选择接口在 Task 18 才加；`... or True` 那一行让本任务里这个接口不存在也能通过，Task 18 会再测一遍接口本身。）

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 shots.py**

```python
"""镜头：增删改、排序、拆分。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Response

from backend.app.deps import check_engines, get_or_404, services
from backend.app.schemas import ReorderIn, ShotDetail, ShotFields, ShotIn, ShotOut
from backend.app.views import shot_detail
from backend.domain.models import Episode, Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["shots"])

COPY_FIELDS = ("scene_block_id", "shot_size", "camera_move", "action", "cast", "props",
               "image_prompt", "motion_prompt", "audio_prompt", "image_engine", "video_engine",
               "use_end_frame", "continue_from_prev")


def _detail(s, sv, shot: Shot) -> ShotDetail:
    ep = s.get(Episode, shot.episode_id)
    return shot_detail(s, sv, shot, ep.project, ep.project.style_bible)


def _order_ids(s, episode_id: int) -> list[int]:
    return [x.id for x in renumber_shots(s, episode_id)]


@router.get("/shots/{shot_id}", response_model=ShotDetail)
def get_shot(shot_id: int, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        return _detail(s, sv, get_or_404(s, Shot, shot_id, "镜头"))


@router.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/shots", status_code=201, response_model=ShotDetail)
def create_shot(episode_id: int, body: ShotIn, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    fields = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True, exclude={"after_shot_id"})
    check_engines(sv, fields.get("image_engine"), fields.get("video_engine"))
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        get_or_404(s, Episode, episode_id, "剧集")
        ids = _order_ids(s, episode_id)
        if body.after_shot_id is not None and body.after_shot_id not in ids:
            raise LookupError(f"这一集没有镜头 {body.after_shot_id}")
        shot = Shot(episode_id=episode_id, order=len(ids), **fields)
        s.add(shot)
        s.flush()
        pos = ids.index(body.after_shot_id) + 1 if body.after_shot_id is not None else len(ids)
        ids.insert(pos, shot.id)
        renumber_shots(s, episode_id, ids)
        return _detail(s, sv, shot)


@router.patch("/shots/{shot_id}", response_model=ShotDetail)
def patch_shot(shot_id: int, body: ShotFields, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    data = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    check_engines(sv, data.get("image_engine"), data.get("video_engine"))
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        shot = get_or_404(s, Shot, shot_id, "镜头")
        for k, v in data.items():
            if v is None and k not in ("image_engine", "video_engine"):
                continue  # 只有引擎覆盖可以清空（回到项目默认）
            setattr(shot, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return _detail(s, sv, shot)


@router.delete("/shots/{shot_id}", status_code=204)
def delete_shot(shot_id: int, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        shot = get_or_404(s, Shot, shot_id, "镜头")
        ep_id = shot.episode_id
        s.delete(shot)
        s.flush()
        renumber_shots(s, ep_id)
    return Response(status_code=204)


@router.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/shots/reorder", response_model=list[ShotOut])
def reorder_shots(episode_id: int, body: ReorderIn, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        get_or_404(s, Episode, episode_id, "剧集")
        return [ShotOut.model_validate(x) for x in renumber_shots(s, episode_id, body.shot_ids)]


@router.post("/shots/{shot_id}/split", response_model=list[ShotOut])
def split_shot(shot_id: int, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        shot = get_or_404(s, Shot, shot_id, "镜头")
        half = round(float(shot.duration) / 2, 2)
        rest = round(float(shot.duration) - half, 2)
        first = [d for d in shot.dialogue or [] if float(d.get("start", 0)) < half]
        second = [{**d, "start": round(float(d["start"]) - half, 2),
                   "end": round(float(d["end"]) - half, 2)}
                  for d in shot.dialogue or [] if float(d.get("start", 0)) >= half]
        new = Shot(episode_id=shot.episode_id, order=shot.order,
                   **{k: getattr(shot, k) for k in COPY_FIELDS},
                   duration=rest, dialogue=second, transition_out=shot.transition_out)
        shot.duration, shot.dialogue, shot.transition_out = half, first, "hard_cut"
        s.add(new)
        s.flush()
        ids = [x.id for x in s.get(Episode, shot.episode_id).shots if x.id != new.id]
        ids.insert(ids.index(shot.id) + 1, new.id)
        renumber_shots(s, shot.episode_id, ids)
        return [ShotOut.model_validate(shot), ShotOut.model_validate(new)]
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 `JobManager`；Task 15 `submit_chain`、`RunOptions`、`STAGES`；Task 16–17
- Produces：
  - `POST /api/episodes/{id}/run`（`RunIn`；`stage="all"` 等于五个阶段依次）→ `RunOut`
  - `GET /api/slots?owner_type=&owner_id=` → `list[SlotOut]`（`stale` 恒为 false，过期看镜头详情）
  - `POST /api/candidates/{id}/select` → `CandidateOut`；`PATCH /api/candidates/{id}`（`note`、`rating`）；`GET /api/candidates/{id}/file` → 文件
  - `GET /media/{path}` → 只允许 `<slug>/media/...` 和 `<slug>/exports/...`
  - `GET /api/jobs?status=a,b&project=<slug>&limit=100`；`GET /api/jobs/{id}`；`POST /api/jobs/{id}/cancel` → `{"ok"}`；`POST /api/jobs/{id}/retry`（`RetryIn`）→ `{"job_id"}`；`POST /api/jobs/{id}/input`（`InputIn`）→ job
  - `GET /api/queue`；`POST /api/queue/pause`、`/resume` → 队列状态
  - `GET /api/health` → `{"ok": true, "version"}`；`GET /api/engines` → `{"image": [...], "video": [...]}`（每项 `name`、`family`、`caps`）；`GET /api/config` → 配置（不含密钥值）
  - `WS /api/ws`：连上先发一条 `{"type": "queue", ...}`，之后转发事件总线上的所有事件

```python
from backend.domain.models import Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots
from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot


def seed(api, n=2):
    api.post("/api/projects", json={"title": "demo"})
    ep = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"source_text": "梗概"}).json()
    with api.app.state.services.db.session() as s:
        for i in range(n):
            s.add(Shot(episode_id=ep["id"], order=i, image_prompt="p"))
        s.flush()
        renumber_shots(s, ep["id"])
    return ep["id"]


def test_run_keyframes_and_videos(api):
    ep_id = seed(api)
    r = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "keyframes", "candidates": 2})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and len(r.json()["job_ids"]) == 4
    jobs = api.get("/api/jobs", params={"status": "queued"}).json()
    assert {j["type"] for j in jobs} == {"image.keyframe"} and all(j["gpu"] for j in jobs)
    r = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "videos", "shots": ["S02"]})
    [vid] = r.json()["job_ids"]
    assert api.get(f"/api/jobs/{vid}").json()["model_family"] == "h3"


def test_run_all_and_errors(api):
    ep_id = seed(api)
    [jid] = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "all"}).json()["job_ids"]
    j = api.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").json()
    assert j["type"] == "script"
    assert j["payload"]["then"] == ["storyboard", "keyframes", "videos", "export"]
    r = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "keyframes", "shots": ["S09"]})
    assert r.status_code == 400 and "S09" in r.json()["detail"]
    assert api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "render"}).status_code == 422
    assert api.post("/api/episodes/999/run", json={"stage": "script"}).status_code == 404


def test_jobs_cancel_retry_input_and_queue(api):
    ep_id = seed(api)
    [jid] = api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "script"}).json()["job_ids"]
    assert api.post(f"/api/jobs/{jid}/cancel").json() == {"ok": True}
    assert api.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").json()["status"] == "canceled"
    new = api.post(f"/api/jobs/{jid}/retry", json={"payload": {"force": True}}).json()["job_id"]
    assert api.get(f"/api/jobs/{new}").json()["payload"]["force"] is True
    assert api.post(f"/api/jobs/{new}/input", json={"text": "x"}).status_code == 400
    assert api.post("/api/queue/pause").json()["paused"] is True
    assert api.get("/api/queue").json()["counts"]["queued"] == 1
    assert api.post("/api/queue/resume").json()["paused"] is False
    assert api.get("/api/jobs", params={"project": "demo"}).json()[0]["id"] == new
    assert api.get("/api/jobs/9999").status_code == 404


def test_candidates_select_and_media(api):
    ep_id = seed(api, n=1)
    sv = api.app.state.services
    shot_id = api.get(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}").json()["shots"][0]["id"]
    files = []
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", shot_id, "first_frame")
        ids = []
        for i in range(2):
            p = sv.media.new_media_path("demo", "first_frame", "png")
            p.write_bytes(f"img{i}".encode())
            files.append(p)
            ids.append(add_candidate(s, slot, file_path=sv.media.rel(p), engine="zimage", params={},
                                     input_hash="h", job_id=None).id)
    r = api.post(f"/api/candidates/{ids[1]}/select")
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["id"] == ids[1]
    [slot] = api.get("/api/slots", params={"owner_type": "shot", "owner_id": shot_id}).json()
    assert slot["selected_candidate_id"] == ids[1]
    assert api.patch(f"/api/candidates/{ids[1]}", json={"note": "好", "rating": 5}).json()["rating"] == 5
    assert api.get(r.json()["url"]).content == b"img1"
    assert api.get(f"/api/candidates/{ids[0]}/file").content == b"img0"
    assert api.get("/media/studio.db").status_code == 403
    assert api.get("/media/demo/runs/1/workflow.api.json").status_code == 403
    assert api.post("/api/candidates/999/select").status_code == 404


def test_system_endpoints(api):
    assert api.get("/api/health").json()["ok"] is True
    eng = api.get("/api/engines").json()
    assert [e["name"] for e in eng["video"]] == ["h3_video", "wan22_i2v"]
    assert eng["video"][0]["caps"]["has_audio"] is True
    cfg = api.get("/api/config").json()
    assert cfg["server"]["port"] == 8700 and cfg["llm"]["default"] == "manual"


def test_websocket_streams_job_events(api):
    ep_id = seed(api)
    with api.websocket_connect("/api/ws") as ws:
        first = ws.receive_json()
        assert first["type"] == "queue" and "counts" in first
        api.post(f"/api/episodes/{ep_id}/run", json={"stage": "script"})
        msg = ws.receive_json()
        assert msg["type"] == "job" and msg["job"]["type"] == "script"
```

`backend/app/routes/runs.py`：

```python
"""触发阶段：把"跑某个阶段"拆成任务。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request

from backend.app.deps import manager, services
from backend.app.schemas import RunIn, RunOut
from backend.pipeline.runs import STAGES, RunOptions, submit_chain

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["runs"])


@router.post("/episodes/{episode_id}/run", response_model=RunOut)
def run_stage(episode_id: int, body: RunIn, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    stages = list(STAGES) if body.stage == "all" else [body.stage]
    opts = RunOptions(shots=body.shots, candidates=body.candidates, force=body.force,
                      priority=body.priority)
    return RunOut(job_ids=submit_chain(manager(request), sv.db, sv.engines, episode_id, stages, opts))
```

`backend/app/routes/candidates.py`：

```python
"""槽位与候选、媒体文件。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

from backend.app.deps import get_or_404, services
from backend.app.schemas import CandidateOut, CandidatePatch, SlotOut
from backend.app.views import candidate_out
from backend.domain.models import Candidate
from backend.domain.slots import select_candidate, slots_for

router = APIRouter(tags=["candidates"])


@router.get("/api/slots", response_model=list[SlotOut])
def list_slots(owner_type: str, owner_id: int, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return [SlotOut(id=sl.id, kind=sl.kind, selected_candidate_id=sl.selected_candidate_id,
                        candidates=[candidate_out(c) for c in sl.candidates])
                for sl in slots_for(s, owner_type, owner_id)]


@router.post("/api/candidates/{candidate_id}/select", response_model=CandidateOut)
def select(candidate_id: int, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        return candidate_out(select_candidate(s, candidate_id))


@router.patch("/api/candidates/{candidate_id}", response_model=CandidateOut)
def patch_candidate(candidate_id: int, body: CandidatePatch, request: Request):
    with services(request).db.session() as s:
        c = get_or_404(s, Candidate, candidate_id, "候选")
        for k, v in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():
            setattr(c, k, v)
        s.flush()
        return candidate_out(c)


@router.get("/api/candidates/{candidate_id}/file")
def candidate_file(candidate_id: int, request: Request):
    sv = services(request)
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        rel = get_or_404(s, Candidate, candidate_id, "候选").file_path
    return FileResponse(sv.media.public_file(rel))


@router.get("/media/{path:path}")
def media(path: str, request: Request):
    return FileResponse(services(request).media.public_file(path))
```

`backend/app/routes/jobs.py`：

```python
"""任务与队列。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from sqlalchemy import select

from backend.app.deps import manager, project_by_slug, services
from backend.app.schemas import InputIn, RetryIn

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["jobs"])


@router.get("/jobs")
def list_jobs(request: Request, status: str | None = None, project: str | None = None,
              limit: int = 100):
    project_id = None
    if project:
        with services(request).db.session() as s:
            project_id = project_by_slug(s, project).id
    statuses = [x.strip() for x in status.split(",") if x.strip()] if status else None
    return manager(request).list_jobs(statuses=statuses, project_id=project_id, limit=limit)


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
def get_job(job_id: int, request: Request):
    return manager(request).get(job_id)


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/cancel")
def cancel_job(job_id: int, request: Request):
    return {"ok": manager(request).cancel(job_id)}


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/retry")
def retry_job(job_id: int, body: RetryIn, request: Request):
    return {"job_id": manager(request).retry(job_id, body.payload)}


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/input")
def provide_input(job_id: int, body: InputIn, request: Request):
    return manager(request).provide_input(job_id, body.text)


@router.get("/queue")
def queue_state(request: Request):
    return manager(request).queue_state()


@router.post("/queue/pause")
def pause(request: Request):
    manager(request).pause()
    return manager(request).queue_state()


@router.post("/queue/resume")
def resume(request: Request):
    manager(request).resume()
    return manager(request).queue_state()
```

```python
"""健康检查、引擎列表、配置。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request

from backend.app.deps import services

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["system"])
VERSION = "0.1.0"


@router.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"ok": True, "version": VERSION}


@router.get("/engines")
def engines(request: Request):
    reg = services(request).engines

    def row(name):
        e = reg.get(name)
        return {"name": name, "family": e.family, "caps": asdict(e.caps)}

    return {"image": [row(n) for n in reg.names("image")],
            "video": [row(n) for n in reg.names("video")]}


@router.get("/config")
def config(request: Request):
    # 配置里只有环境变量名，没有密钥值
    return request.app.state.cfg.model_dump(mode="json")
```

`backend/app/routes/ws.py`：

```python
"""WebSocket：推送任务状态、日志、进度、队列状态。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from starlette.concurrency import run_in_threadpool

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api", tags=["ws"])


@router.websocket("/ws")
async def events(sock: WebSocket):
    await sock.accept()
    bus = sock.app.state.bus
    q = bus.subscribe()
    try:
        state = await run_in_threadpool(sock.app.state.manager.queue_state)
        await sock.send_json({"type": "queue", **state})
        while True:
            await sock.send_json(await q.get())
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        pass
    finally:
        bus.unsubscribe(q)
```

### Task 19: doctor 环境检查

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/doctor.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/routes/system.py`（加 `GET /api/doctor`）
- Test: `backend/tests/test_doctor.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `Config`；Task 6 `ComfyClient`、`ComfyError`；Task 8 `EngineRegistry`（引擎的 `tpl`、`overrides`）；Task 9 `FFmpeg.version()`
- Produces：
  - `Check(group, name, status: "ok"|"warn"|"fail", detail="")`（dataclass）
  - `run_doctor(cfg, engines, comfy, ffmpeg, *, http_get=_http_get, which=shutil.which) -> list[Check]`，检查项：
    - `("ComfyUI", "服务")`：`system_stats(tries=1, timeout=3)`，detail 带显卡名和显存
    - 每个启用的引擎 `("引擎", "<name> 节点")`：模板里的 class_type 是否都在 `/object_info`（ComfyUI 连不上时不出这一项）
    - 每个启用的引擎 `("引擎", "<name> 模型")`：`model_files` 的文件名是否在 `comfyui.models_dir` 下（递归、只比文件名、不分大小写）；没配目录时出一条 `("引擎", "模型目录")` warn
    - 每个 LLM provider `("LLM", id)`：openai 请求 `/models`（连不上是 warn）；claude 看 `claude` 命令在不在（不在是 fail）；manual 恒 ok
    - `("工具", "ffmpeg")`：能跑是 ok，没有 `--enable-libass` 是 warn，跑不起来是 fail
    - `("系统", "磁盘")`：数据目录所在盘剩余 < 50GB 为 warn；`("系统", "内存")`：可用 < 8GB 为 warn
  - `GET /api/doctor` → `list[Check 的 dict]`

Agnes 云端检查放到 M3（接入 Agnes 引擎时再加）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_doctor.py`：

```python
import httpx

from backend.config import ComfyCfg, Config, EngineCfg, LLMCfg, ProviderCfg, ServerCfg
from backend.doctor import run_doctor
from backend.engines.comfyui.client import ComfyClient
from backend.engines.ffmpeg import FFmpegError
from backend.engines.registry import EngineRegistry

ONLY_ZIMAGE = {"h3_image": EngineCfg(enabled=False), "h3_video": EngineCfg(enabled=False),
               "wan22_i2v": EngineCfg(enabled=False)}
PROVIDERS = [ProviderCfg(id="lm", kind="openai", base_url="http://lm.test/v1"),
             ProviderCfg(id="claude", kind="claude"), ProviderCfg(id="m", kind="manual")]


class FakeFF:
    def __init__(self, text="ffmpeg version 8.1-full --enable-libass", fail=False):
        self.text, self.fail = text, fail

    def version(self):
        if self.fail:
            raise FFmpegError("找不到 ffmpeg")
        return self.text


def by_key(checks):
    return {(c.group, c.name): c for c in checks}


def make_cfg(tmp_path, url, models_dir=None):
    return Config(server=ServerCfg(data_dir=tmp_path / "data"),
                  comfyui=ComfyCfg(url=url, models_dir=models_dir),
                  engines=ONLY_ZIMAGE, llm=LLMCfg(providers=PROVIDERS))


def test_doctor_all_good(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    models = tmp_path / "models"
    for sub, f in [("diffusion_models", "z_image_turbo_bf16.safetensors"),
                   ("text_encoders", "Qwen_3_4B.safetensors"), ("vae", "ae.safetensors")]:
        (models / sub).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (models / sub / f).write_bytes(b"")
    cfg = make_cfg(tmp_path, fake_comfy.url, models)
    reg = EngineRegistry(cfg)
    fake_comfy.object_info = {ct: {} for ct in reg.get("zimage").tpl.class_types()}
    c = by_key(run_doctor(cfg, reg, ComfyClient(fake_comfy.url, backoff=0), FakeFF(),
                          http_get=lambda url: {"data": [{"id": "gemma"}]},
                          which=lambda n: "C:/bin/claude.exe"))
    assert c[("ComfyUI", "服务")].status == "ok" and "12.0" in c[("ComfyUI", "服务")].detail
    assert c[("引擎", "zimage 节点")].status == "ok"
    assert c[("引擎", "zimage 模型")].status == "ok"
    assert [c[("LLM", x)].status for x in ("lm", "claude", "m")] == ["ok", "ok", "ok"]
    assert c[("工具", "ffmpeg")].status == "ok"
    assert c[("系统", "磁盘")].status in ("ok", "warn") and c[("系统", "内存")].status in ("ok", "warn")


def test_doctor_reports_missing_things(tmp_path, fake_comfy):
    models = tmp_path / "models"
    models.mkdir()
    fake_comfy.object_info = {"KSampler": {}}
    cfg = make_cfg(tmp_path, fake_comfy.url, models)

    def down(url):
        raise httpx.ConnectError("refused")

    c = by_key(run_doctor(cfg, EngineRegistry(cfg), ComfyClient(fake_comfy.url, backoff=0),
                          FakeFF("ffmpeg version 8.1"), http_get=down, which=lambda n: None))
    assert c[("引擎", "zimage 节点")].status == "fail" and "UNETLoader" in c[("引擎", "zimage 节点")].detail
    assert c[("引擎", "zimage 模型")].status == "fail" and "ae.safetensors" in c[("引擎", "zimage 模型")].detail
    assert c[("LLM", "lm")].status == "warn" and c[("LLM", "claude")].status == "fail"
    assert c[("工具", "ffmpeg")].status == "warn"


def test_doctor_comfy_down(tmp_path):
    cfg = make_cfg(tmp_path, "http://127.0.0.1:9")
    checks = run_doctor(cfg, EngineRegistry(cfg), ComfyClient("http://127.0.0.1:9", backoff=0),
                        FakeFF(fail=True), http_get=lambda u: {"data": []}, which=lambda n: "x")
    c = by_key(checks)
    assert c[("ComfyUI", "服务")].status == "fail"
    assert not any(x.name.endswith("节点") for x in checks)
    assert c[("引擎", "模型目录")].status == "warn"
    assert c[("工具", "ffmpeg")].status == "fail"


def test_doctor_endpoint(api):
    rows = api.get("/api/doctor").json()
    assert any(r["group"] == "ComfyUI" and r["status"] == "fail" for r in rows)
    assert {"group", "name", "status", "detail"} <= set(rows[0])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 doctor.py**

```python
"""环境检查：ComfyUI、各引擎的节点与模型、LLM、ffmpeg、磁盘、内存。只报告，不改状态。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import httpx
import psutil

from backend.config import Config
from backend.engines.comfyui.errors import ComfyError


@dataclass
class Check:
    group: str
    name: str
    status: Literal["ok", "warn", "fail"]
    detail: str = ""


def _http_get(url: str) -> Any:
    r = httpx.get(url, timeout=3, trust_env=False)
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()


def _model_index(models_dir: Path | None) -> set[str] | None:
    if not models_dir or not Path(models_dir).is_dir():
        return None
    return {p.name.lower() for p in Path(models_dir).rglob("*") if p.is_file()}


def _basename(f: str) -> str:
    return f.replace("\\", "/").rsplit("/", 1)[-1].lower()


def run_doctor(cfg: Config, engines, comfy, ffmpeg, *,
               http_get: Callable[[str], Any] = _http_get,
               which: Callable[[str], str | None] = shutil.which) -> list[Check]:
    out: list[Check] = []

    info = None
    if comfy is None:
        out.append(Check("ComfyUI", "服务", "fail", "未配置"))
    else:
        try:
            st = comfy.system_stats(tries=1, timeout=3)
            dev = (st.get("devices") or [{}])[0]
            out.append(Check("ComfyUI", "服务", "ok",
                             f"{cfg.comfyui.url} · {dev.get('name', '?')} · 显存空闲 "
                             f"{dev.get('vram_free', 0) / 2**30:.1f}/{dev.get('vram_total', 0) / 2**30:.1f} GB"))
            info = comfy.object_info()
        except ComfyError as e:
            out.append(Check("ComfyUI", "服务", "fail", str(e)))

    index = _model_index(cfg.comfyui.models_dir)
    if index is None:
        out.append(Check("引擎", "模型目录", "warn",
                         "没配置 comfyui.models_dir 或目录不存在，跳过模型文件检查"))
    for name, eng in sorted(engines.all().items()):
        if info is not None:
            types = eng.tpl.class_types()
            missing = sorted(types - set(info))
            out.append(Check("引擎", f"{name} 节点", "fail" if missing else "ok",
                             "缺少节点：" + "、".join(missing) if missing else f"{len(types)} 个节点都在"))
        if index is not None:
            files = eng.tpl.model_files(eng.overrides)
            miss = [f for f in files if _basename(f) not in index]
            out.append(Check("引擎", f"{name} 模型", "fail" if miss else "ok",
                             "缺少：" + "、".join(miss) if miss else f"{len(files)} 个模型文件都在"))

    for p in cfg.llm.providers:
        if p.kind == "openai":
            try:
                d = http_get(p.base_url.rstrip("/") + "/models")
                n = len(d.get("data") or [])
                out.append(Check("LLM", p.id, "ok", f"{p.base_url} · {n} 个模型"))
            except Exception as e:
                out.append(Check("LLM", p.id, "warn",
                                 f"连不上 {p.base_url}（{type(e).__name__}）。本机 LM Studio 平时不开，用到前先启动"))
        elif p.kind == "claude":
            exe = which("claude")
            out.append(Check("LLM", p.id, "ok" if exe else "fail", exe or "找不到 claude 命令"))
        else:
            out.append(Check("LLM", p.id, "ok", "手动粘贴结果"))

    try:
        v = ffmpeg.version()
        first = (v.splitlines() or ["ffmpeg"])[0]
        if "--enable-libass" in v:
            out.append(Check("工具", "ffmpeg", "ok", first))
        else:
            out.append(Check("工具", "ffmpeg", "warn", first + "（没有 libass，烧字幕会失败）"))
    except Exception as e:
        out.append(Check("工具", "ffmpeg", "fail", str(e)))

    cfg.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    free = shutil.disk_usage(cfg.data_dir).free / 2**30
    out.append(Check("系统", "磁盘", "ok" if free >= 50 else "warn",
                     f"{cfg.data_dir} 所在盘剩余 {free:.0f} GB"))
    mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
    avail = mem.available / 2**30
    out.append(Check("系统", "内存", "ok" if avail >= 8 else "warn",
                     f"可用 {avail:.1f} / {mem.total / 2**30:.0f} GB"))
    return out
```

### Task 20: studio 命令行

**Files:**
- Create: `cli/studio.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_cli.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 16–19 的 HTTP 接口
- Produces（`uv run studio ...`；`--json` 放在子命令前，输出 JSON；服务地址取 `$STUDIO_URL`，默认 `http://127.0.0.1:8700`）：
  - `studio project list` / `studio project create <标题> [--slug] [--aspect 9:16] [--image-engine zimage] [--video-engine h3_video]`
  - `studio episode import <项目> --file <文本> [--title] [--seconds 60] [--number]`
  - `studio episode show <项目> <集数>`（镜头表：编号、时长、首帧/视频状态 `√` 已选定、`!` 已过期、`·` 还没有）
  - `studio run <阶段> <项目> [--episode/-e 1] [--shots S01,S03] [--candidates 1] [--force]`，阶段为 `script / storyboard / keyframes / videos / export / all`
  - `studio jobs [--status queued,running] [--project] [--limit 30] [--watch]`（watch 每 2 秒刷新，队列空了退出）
  - `studio job show|cancel|retry <id>`、`studio job input <id> --file <结果文件>`
  - `studio candidate select <id>`
  - `studio queue status|pause|resume`
  - `studio doctor`（有 fail 时退出码 1）
  - 退出码：HTTP 错误 1，连不上服务 2
  - 内部函数 `_client() -> httpx.Client`（测试里替换成 `TestClient`）

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`backend/tests/test_cli.py`：

```python
import json

import pytest
from typer.testing import CliRunner

import cli.studio as studio
from backend.domain.models import Job, Shot
from backend.domain.shots import renumber_shots
from backend.domain.slots import add_candidate, get_or_create_slot

runner = CliRunner()


@pytest.fixture
def cli(api, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(studio, "_client", lambda: api)
    return lambda *args: runner.invoke(studio.app, [str(a) for a in args])


def test_project_create_and_list(cli):
    r = cli("--json", "project", "create", "提词员", "--aspect", "9:16")
    assert r.exit_code == 0, r.output
    assert json.loads(r.stdout)["slug"] == "提词员"
    r = cli("project", "list")
    assert "提词员" in r.stdout and "zimage / h3_video" in r.stdout


def test_episode_import_run_and_jobs(cli, tmp_path):
    cli("project", "create", "demo")
    f = tmp_path / "梗概.md"
    f.write_text("林夏发现提词器被改了。", encoding="utf-8")
    r = cli("--json", "episode", "import", "demo", "--file", f, "--seconds", "30")
    assert json.loads(r.stdout)["number"] == 1
    r = cli("--json", "run", "script", "demo", "--episode", "1")
    ids = json.loads(r.stdout)["job_ids"]
    assert len(ids) == 1
    r = cli("--json", "jobs", "--status", "queued")
    assert [j["id"] for j in json.loads(r.stdout)] == ids
    assert "script" in cli("jobs").stdout
    assert cli("job", "cancel", ids[0]).exit_code == 0
    assert json.loads(cli("--json", "job", "show", ids[0]).stdout)["status"] == "canceled"
    r = cli("--json", "job", "retry", ids[0])
    assert json.loads(r.stdout)["job_id"] != ids[0]


def test_http_errors_exit_1(cli):
    r = cli("run", "keyframes", "nope", "--episode", "1")
    assert r.exit_code == 1 and "找不到项目" in r.output


def test_unreachable_server_exits_2(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("STUDIO_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:9")
    r = runner.invoke(studio.app, ["project", "list"])
    assert r.exit_code == 2 and "连不上" in r.output


def test_episode_show(cli, api):
    cli("project", "create", "demo")
    ep = api.post("/api/projects/demo/episodes", json={"source_text": "x"}).json()
    with api.app.state.services.db.session() as s:
        s.add(Shot(episode_id=ep["id"], order=0, action="林夏抬头", duration=5))
        s.flush()
        renumber_shots(s, ep["id"])
    out = cli("episode", "show", "demo", 1).stdout
    assert "S01" in out and "林夏抬头" in out and "·" in out


def test_candidate_select(cli, api):
    cli("project", "create", "demo")
    sv = api.app.state.services
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        slot = get_or_create_slot(s, "shot", 1, "first_frame")
        add_candidate(s, slot, file_path="demo/media/first_frame/a.png", engine="zimage",
                      params={}, input_hash="h", job_id=None)
        cid = add_candidate(s, slot, file_path="demo/media/first_frame/b.png", engine="zimage",
                            params={}, input_hash="h", job_id=None).id
    r = cli("candidate", "select", cid)
    assert r.exit_code == 0 and "已选定" in r.output


def test_job_input(cli, api, tmp_path):
    sv = api.app.state.services
    jid = api.app.state.manager.submit("script", {"episode_id": 1})
    with sv.db.session() as s:
        j = s.get(Job, jid)
        j.status = "waiting_input"
        j.result = {"input_request": {"key": "script_structure"}}
    f = tmp_path / "结果.json"
    f.write_text('{"title": "t"}', encoding="utf-8")
    assert cli("job", "input", jid, "--file", f).exit_code == 0
    d = api.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").json()
    assert d["status"] == "queued" and d["payload"]["manual"]["script_structure"] == '{"title": "t"}'


def test_doctor_exit_code(cli):
    r = cli("doctor")
    assert r.exit_code == 1 and "ComfyUI" in r.output
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 cli/studio.py**

```python
"""studio 命令行：调用本机工作台 HTTP API 的薄客户端。加 --json 输出机器可读结果。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

import httpx
import typer

DEFAULT_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8700"

app = typer.Typer(help="AI Studio 命令行（调用本机工作台的 HTTP API）", no_args_is_help=True,
                  add_completion=False)
project_app = typer.Typer(help="项目", no_args_is_help=True)
episode_app = typer.Typer(help="剧集", no_args_is_help=True)
job_app = typer.Typer(help="单个任务", no_args_is_help=True)
candidate_app = typer.Typer(help="候选", no_args_is_help=True)
queue_app = typer.Typer(help="队列", no_args_is_help=True)
app.add_typer(project_app, name="project")
app.add_typer(episode_app, name="episode")
app.add_typer(job_app, name="job")
app.add_typer(candidate_app, name="candidate")
app.add_typer(queue_app, name="queue")

STATE = {"json": False}
ICON = {"ok": "√", "warn": "!", "fail": "×"}


def _base_url() -> str:
    return os.environ.get("STUDIO_URL", DEFAULT_URL)


def _client() -> httpx.Client:
    return httpx.Client(base_url=_base_url(), timeout=120, trust_env=False)


def _call(method: str, path: str, **kw) -> Any:
    try:
        r = _client().request(method, path, **kw)
    except httpx.TransportError:
        typer.echo(f"连不上工作台服务（{_base_url()}），先运行：uv run studio-server", err=True)
        raise typer.Exit(2)
    if r.status_code >= 400:
        try:
            detail = r.json().get("detail")
        except ValueError:
            detail = r.text
        typer.echo(f"错误（HTTP {r.status_code}）：{detail}", err=True)
        raise typer.Exit(1)
    return r.json() if r.content else None


def _emit(data: Any, human: Callable[[Any], None]) -> None:
    if STATE["json"]:
        typer.echo(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    else:
        human(data)


@app.callback()
def main(as_json: bool = typer.Option(False, "--json", help="输出 JSON，便于脚本和 Claude 解析")):
    STATE["json"] = as_json


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 项目
def _print_projects(ps: list[dict]) -> None:
    if not ps:
        typer.echo("（还没有项目）")
    for p in ps:
        typer.echo(f"{p['slug']}\t{p['title']}\t{p['aspect']}\t{p['image_engine']} / {p['video_engine']}")


@project_app.command("list")
def project_list():
    _emit(_call("GET", "/api/projects"), _print_projects)


@project_app.command("create")
def project_create(title: str,
                   slug: Optional[str] = typer.Option(None, help="目录名，默认由标题生成"),
                   aspect: str = typer.Option("9:16", help="9:16 或 16:9"),
                   image_engine: str = typer.Option("zimage", "--image-engine"),
                   video_engine: str = typer.Option("h3_video", "--video-engine")):
    body = {"title": title, "aspect": aspect, "image_engine": image_engine,
            "video_engine": video_engine}
    if slug:
        body["slug"] = slug
    _emit(_call("POST", "/api/projects", json=body), lambda p: typer.echo(f"已创建项目 {p['slug']}"))


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 剧集
@episode_app.command("import")
def episode_import(project: str,
                   file: Path = typer.Option(..., "--file", "-f", exists=True, dir_okay=False,
                                             help="梗概或小说文本（UTF-8）"),
                   title: str = typer.Option("", help="本集标题，留空由剧本阶段生成"),
                   seconds: float = typer.Option(60.0, "--seconds", help="目标时长（秒）"),
                   number: Optional[int] = typer.Option(None, help="集数，默认接在最后")):
    text = file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    body: dict = {"title": title, "source_text": text, "target_seconds": seconds}
    if number:
        body["number"] = number
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/projects/{project}/episodes", json=body),
          lambda e: typer.echo(f"已导入第 {e['number']} 集（{len(text)} 字，目标 {seconds:.0f} 秒）"))


def _mark(shot: dict, kind: str) -> str:
    sl = next((x for x in shot.get("slots", []) if x["kind"] == kind), None)
    if not sl or not sl["selected_candidate_id"]:
        return "·"
    return "!" if sl["stale"] else "√"


def _print_episode(ep: dict) -> None:
    typer.echo(f"第 {ep['number']} 集 {ep['title']}  状态 {ep['status']}  "
               f"段落 {len(ep['scene_blocks'])}  镜头 {len(ep['shots'])}")
    typer.echo("编号   时长   首帧 视频  动作")
    for sh in ep["shots"]:
        typer.echo(f"{sh['code']:<6}{sh['duration']:>5.1f}s   {_mark(sh, 'first_frame')}    "
                   f"{_mark(sh, 'video')}    {sh['action'][:40]}")
    typer.echo("（√ 已选定  ! 已过期  · 还没有）")


@episode_app.command("show")
def episode_show(project: str, number: int):
    _emit(_call("GET", f"/api/projects/{project}/episodes/by-number/{number}"), _print_episode)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 运行
@app.command("run")
def run(stage: str = typer.Argument(..., help="script / storyboard / keyframes / videos / export / all"),
        project: str = typer.Argument(...),
        episode: int = typer.Option(1, "--episode", "-e", help="集数"),
        shots: Optional[str] = typer.Option(None, help="只跑这些镜头，逗号分隔，如 S01,S03"),
        candidates: int = typer.Option(1, help="每个镜头出几张首帧候选（1–4）"),
        force: bool = typer.Option(False, help="输入没变也重新生成")):
    ep = _call("GET", f"/api/projects/{project}/episodes/by-number/{episode}")
    body: dict = {"stage": stage, "candidates": candidates, "force": force}
    if shots:
        body["shots"] = [x.strip() for x in shots.split(",") if x.strip()]
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/episodes/{ep['id']}/run", json=body),
          lambda r: typer.echo(f"已提交 {len(r['job_ids'])} 个任务：{', '.join(map(str, r['job_ids']))}"))


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 任务
def _print_jobs(jobs: list[dict]) -> None:
    if not jobs:
        typer.echo("（没有任务）")
    for j in jobs:
        err = f"  {j['error'].splitlines()[0][:60]}" if j.get("error") else ""
        typer.echo(f"#{j['id']:<5} {j['status']:<14}{j['type']:<16}{j.get('model_family') or '':<8}{err}")


@app.command("jobs")
def jobs(status: Optional[str] = typer.Option(None, help="逗号分隔，如 queued,running"),
         project: Optional[str] = typer.Option(None, help="项目 slug"),
         limit: int = typer.Option(30),
         watch: bool = typer.Option(False, help="每 2 秒刷新，直到队列空了")):
    params: dict = {"limit": limit}
    if status:
        params["status"] = status
    if project:
        params["project"] = project
    while True:
        _emit(_call("GET", "/api/jobs", params=params), _print_jobs)
        if not watch:
            return
        counts = _call("GET", "/api/queue")["counts"]
        if counts["queued"] + counts["running"] == 0:
            typer.echo("队列已空")
            return
        time.sleep(2)
        typer.echo("")


def _print_job(j: dict) -> None:
    typer.echo(f"#{j['id']} {j['type']}  {j['status']}  族 {j.get('model_family') or '-'}")
    if j.get("error"):
        typer.echo(f"错误：{j['error']}")
    req = (j.get("result") or {}).get("input_request")
    if j["status"] == "waiting_input" and req:
        typer.echo(f"等待人工输入（key={req.get('key')}）。把下面的提示词交给任意模型，"
                   f"结果存成文件后运行：studio job input {j['id']} --file <文件>")
        typer.echo("---- system ----\n" + req.get("system", ""))
        typer.echo("---- user ----\n" + req.get("user", ""))
    tail = (j.get("log") or "").splitlines()[-20:]
    if tail:
        typer.echo("---- 日志（最后 20 行）----\n" + "\n".join(tail))


@job_app.command("show")
def job_show(job_id: int):
    _emit(_call("GET", f"/api/jobs/{job_id}"), _print_job)


@job_app.command("cancel")
def job_cancel(job_id: int):
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/cancel"),
          lambda r: typer.echo("已取消" if r["ok"] else "没能取消（任务可能已经结束）"))


@job_app.command("retry")
def job_retry(job_id: int):
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/retry", json={}),
          lambda r: typer.echo(f"已重新提交为任务 #{r['job_id']}"))


@job_app.command("input")
def job_input(job_id: int,
              file: Path = typer.Option(..., "--file", "-f", exists=True, dir_okay=False,
                                        help="模型输出（UTF-8 文本）")):
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/jobs/{job_id}/input", json={"text": file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")}),
          lambda j: typer.echo(f"已提交，任务 #{j['id']} 重新排队"))


# ------------------------------------------------------------ 候选、队列、体检
@candidate_app.command("select")
def candidate_select(candidate_id: int):
    _emit(_call("POST", f"/api/candidates/{candidate_id}/select"),
          lambda c: typer.echo(f"已选定候选 #{c['id']}：{c['file_path']}"))


def _print_queue(q: dict) -> None:
    c = q["counts"]
    typer.echo(f"{'已暂停' if q['paused'] else '运行中'}  排队 {c['queued']}  运行 {c['running']}  "
               f"等待输入 {c['waiting_input']}  当前模型族 {q.get('current_family') or '-'}")


@queue_app.command("status")
def queue_status():
    _emit(_call("GET", "/api/queue"), _print_queue)


@queue_app.command("pause")
def queue_pause():
    _emit(_call("POST", "/api/queue/pause"), _print_queue)


@queue_app.command("resume")
def queue_resume():
    _emit(_call("POST", "/api/queue/resume"), _print_queue)


def _print_doctor(rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    for r in rows:
        typer.echo(f"{ICON.get(r['status'], '?')} [{r['group']}] {r['name']}：{r['detail']}")


@app.command("doctor")
def doctor():
    rows = _call("GET", "/api/doctor")
    _emit(rows, _print_doctor)
    if any(r["status"] == "fail" for r in rows):
        raise typer.Exit(1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app()
```

- 可能修改：`backend/llm/providers.py`、`config.example.yaml`（取决于实测结果）

- [ ] **Step 1: 准备本地配置**

Run（PowerShell）: `Copy-Item config.example.yaml config.yaml; lms ls`
把 `lms ls` 里要用的模型 id（如 Gemma4-26B-A4B）填进 `config.yaml` 的 `llm.providers[lmstudio].model`。

Run: `claude --help | Select-String "system-prompt"`
Expected: 能看到 `--system-prompt-file`。如果只有 `--system-prompt`：把 `backend/llm/providers.py` 里 `_cli` 的 `"--system-prompt-file", str(sp)` 改成 `"--system-prompt", system`，`backend/tests/test_llm_gateway.py` 的 `FakeRun` 改为读 `cmd[cmd.index("--system-prompt") + 1]`，跑 `uv run pytest backend/tests/test_llm_gateway.py` 确认通过。

- [ ] **Step 2: 实测 Claude provider（设计文档风险项"Claude Agent SDK 在 Windows 上的调用方式"）**

Run: `uv run python -m backend.llm.selftest claude`
Run: `uv run python -m backend.llm.selftest claude --no-sdk`
Expected: 两条都打印 `通过：ok=True word='你好'`。
- 如果第一条的日志里出现 "Claude Agent SDK 调用失败，退回 claude -p"：说明 SDK 在本机不可用，把 `config.yaml` 和 `config.example.yaml` 里 claude provider 的 `use_sdk` 改成 `false`，记下错误信息。
- 两条都失败：记下错误，把 `llm.routes` 的 script / storyboard 改到 lmstudio，继续后面的步骤。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实测 LM Studio 与卸载（设计文档风险项"LM Studio 卸载接口的可用性"）**

Run: `lms server start; uv run python -m backend.llm.selftest lmstudio; lms ps`
Expected: selftest 打印"通过"，日志里有"已卸载 lmstudio 的模型（退出码 0）"；`lms ps` 显示没有已加载的模型。
如果 `lms unload --all` 退出码不为 0 或模型仍在：查 LM Studio 的 REST 卸载接口，能用的话把 `unload_cmd` 换成对应的 `curl` 命令；都不行就记下来，在 `config.yaml` 里把 script / storyboard 路由到 claude。

- [ ] **Step 4: 起服务并跑 doctor**

在另一个终端运行：`uv run studio-server`
Run: `uv run studio doctor`
Expected: ComfyUI √；zimage / h3_image / h3_video / wan22_i2v 的节点和模型都是 √；ffmpeg √。有 × 的项先按提示修好（缺节点就在 ComfyUI Manager 里装，缺模型就补模型或改 `config.yaml` 里引擎的模型参数），再继续。

- [ ] **Step 5: 冒烟：Z-Image + Wan2.2，两镜，10 秒**

```powershell
Set-Content -Encoding utf8 $env:TEMP\smoke.md "深夜的直播间，女主播林夏照着提词器念稿，突然发现屏幕上的词被人改成了她的秘密。她停顿了一秒，抬头看向导播间。"
uv run studio project create smoke --image-engine zimage --video-engine wan22_i2v
uv run studio episode import smoke --file $env:TEMP\smoke.md --seconds 10
uv run studio run all smoke -e 1
uv run studio jobs --watch
uv run studio episode show smoke 1
Get-ChildItem projects\smoke\exports
```

Expected: 剧本 → 分镜（2 镜）→ 首帧 → 视频 → 合成依次完成；`episode show` 里两镜的首帧、视频都是 √；`projects\smoke\exports\` 下有 `ep01_9x16_*.mp4`。用 `ffprobe -v error -show_entries stream=codec_type,width,height -of csv=p=0 <文件>` 确认是 1080×1920。

如果某个任务失败：`uv run studio job show <id>` 看错误和日志，按 superpowers:systematic-debugging 排查。修代码时先补一个能复现的失败测试，修完提交，再 `uv run studio job retry <id>`。

- [ ] **Step 6: 验收：H3 原生声音（M1 后端部分的验收）**

```powershell
uv run studio project create h3check --image-engine zimage --video-engine h3_video
uv run studio episode import h3check --file $env:TEMP\smoke.md --seconds 15
uv run studio run all h3check -e 1
uv run studio jobs --watch
```

Run: `ffprobe -v error -show_entries stream=codec_type -of csv=p=0 (Get-ChildItem projects\h3check\exports\*.mp4 | Select-Object -Last 1).FullName`
Expected: 输出里有 `video` 和 `audio` 两行。亲自播放一遍，确认能听到对白或环境声。

- [ ] **Step 7: 写实测记录和 README**

`docs/notes/m1-verification.md` 按下面的结构填实测结果（每项写结论 + 关键数字或报错原文）：

```markdown
- 各阶段耗时：剧本 …，分镜 …，首帧 …/张，视频 …/镜，合成 …
- 问题与修复：…

## 验收（zimage + h3_video，15 秒）

- 观感问题（留给 M2）：…
```

`README.md`：

```markdown
# AI Studio

本机 AI 漫剧工作台（私有）。设计文档：`docs/specs/2026-10-07-ai-studio-design.md`。

## 启动

    uv sync
    Copy-Item config.example.yaml config.yaml   # 按本机情况改
    uv run studio-server                        # http://127.0.0.1:8700

## 命令行

    uv run studio doctor
    uv run studio project create 提词员
    uv run studio episode import 提词员 --file 故事构思/提词员.md --seconds 90
    uv run studio run all 提词员 -e 1
    uv run studio jobs --watch
    uv run studio episode show 提词员 1

所有命令加 `--json` 输出 JSON。完整命令见 `uv run studio --help`。

## 测试

    uv run pytest
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 运行全部测试并提交**

Run: `uv run pytest -q`
Expected: 全部通过

```bash
git add README.md docs/notes config.example.yaml backend
git commit -m "docs: M1 后端实测记录与 README"
```

- 用 CLI 跑出了带 H3 原生声音的 1080×1920 成片。
